PlanetaryOS
ts
// src/planetary/PlanetaryTypes.ts
export type PlanetaryScope = "CITY" | "REGION" | "NATION" | "FEDERATION" | "PLANET";

export interface PlanetaryState {
  id: string;
  scope: PlanetaryScope;
  tenantId?: string;
  energyLoad: number;
  hazardIndex: number;
  climateStress: number;
  trafficStress: number;
  socialStress: number;
  timestamp: string;
}
ts
// src/planetary/PlanetaryOS.ts
import { planetarySimulationGrid } from "../planet/PlanetarySimulationGrid";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export class PlanetaryOS {
  private states: PlanetaryState[] = [];

  snapshot(scope: PlanetaryScope, tenantId?: string): PlanetaryState {
    const sim = planetarySimulationGrid.run(scope);
    const state: PlanetaryState = {
      id: `PLANET-STATE-${Date.now()}`,
      scope,
      tenantId,
      energyLoad: sim.output.energyLoad ?? 1,
      hazardIndex: sim.output.hazardIndex ?? 1,
      climateStress: sim.output.climateStress ?? 1,
      trafficStress: sim.output.trafficStress ?? 1,
      socialStress: sim.output.socialStress ?? 1,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.states.push(state);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId ?? "GLOBAL",
      `PLANETARY_${scope}_STABILITY`,
      1 / (state.hazardIndex + state.climateStress + state.socialStress),
      1
    );

    return state;
  }

  list(scope?: PlanetaryScope) {
    return this.states.filter(s => !scope || s.scope === scope);
  }
}

export const planetaryOS = new PlanetaryOS();
ts
// src/api/planetaryRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";

const router = express.Router();

router.post("/planetary/snapshot", (req, res) => {
  const { scope, tenantId } = req.body;
  const state = planetaryOS.snapshot(scope, tenantId);
  res.json(state);
});

router.get("/planetary/states", (req, res) => {
  const scope = req.query.scope as any;
  res.json(planetaryOS.list(scope));
});

export default router;
WaterOS
ts
// src/water/WaterTypes.ts
export interface WaterNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  capacityM3: number;
}

export interface WaterEvent {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  nodeId: string;
  type: "SUPPLY" | "SHORTAGE" | "FLOOD";
  magnitude: number;
  timestamp: string;
}
ts
// src/water/WaterOS.ts
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { quantumHazardEngine } from "../quantum/QuantumHazardEngine";

export class WaterOS {
  nodes: Map<string, WaterNode> = new Map();
  events: WaterEvent[] = [];

  addNode(node: WaterNode) {
    this.nodes.set(node.id, node);
    return node;
  }

  reportEvent(event: Omit<WaterEvent, "id" | "timestamp">): WaterEvent {
    const full: WaterEvent = {
      ...event,
      id: `WATER-EVENT-${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.events.push(full);

    if (full.type === "SHORTAGE" || full.type === "FLOOD") {
      quantumHazardEngine.report("INFRA", full.magnitude, { nodeId: full.nodeId });
    }

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      full.tenantId,
      `WATER_${full.type}_MODERNIZATION`,
      full.magnitude,
      1
    );

    return full;
  }

  listNodes() {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.values());
  }

  listEvents(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.events.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const waterOS = new WaterOS();
ts
// src/api/waterRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { waterOS } from "../water/WaterOS";

  res.json(event);

router.get("/water/events", (req, res) => {
  res.json(waterOS.listEvents(req.query.tenantId as string));
});

export default router;
WasteOS
ts
// src/waste/WasteTypes.ts
export interface WasteRoute {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  corridorId: string;
  volumeKg: number;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface WasteMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  routeId: string;
  action: "REDUCE_LANDFILL" | "INCREASE_RECYCLING";
  createdAt: string;
}
ts
// src/waste/WasteOS.ts
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { qeMissionEngine } from "../qe/QEMissionEngine";

export class WasteOS {
  private routes: WasteRoute[] = [];
  private missions: WasteMission[] = [];

  recordRoute(
    tenantId: string,
    corridorId: string,
    volumeKg: number
  ): WasteRoute {
    const route: WasteRoute = {
      id: `WASTE-ROUTE-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      corridorId,
      volumeKg,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.routes.push(route);
    return route;
  }

  generateMission(route: WasteRoute): WasteMission {
    const action: WasteMission["action"] =
      route.volumeKg > 1000 ? "REDUCE_LANDFILL" : "INCREASE_RECYCLING";

    const mission: WasteMission = {
      id: `WASTE-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: route.tenantId,
      routeId: route.id,
      action,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.missions.push(mission);

    qeMissionEngine.createCorridorMission(route.tenantId);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      route.tenantId,
      `WASTE_${action}_MODERNIZATION`,
      route.volumeKg,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  listRoutes(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.routes.filter(r => !tenantId || r.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  listMissions(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const wasteOS = new WasteOS();
ts
// src/api/wasteRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { wasteOS } from "../waste/WasteOS";

const router = express.Router();

router.post("/waste/routes", (req, res) => {
  const { tenantId, corridorId, volumeKg } = req.body;
  const route = wasteOS.recordRoute(tenantId, corridorId, volumeKg);
  const mission = wasteOS.generateMission(route);
  res.json({ route, mission });
});

router.get("/waste/routes", (req, res) => {
  res.json(wasteOS.listRoutes(req.query.tenantId as string));
});

router.get("/waste/missions", (req, res) => {
  res.json(wasteOS.listMissions(req.query.tenantId as string));
});

export default router;

// src/telecom/TelecomOS.ts
import { quantumHazardEngine } from "../quantum/QuantumHazardEngine";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export class TelecomOS {
  nodes: Map<string, TelecomNode> = new Map();
  events: TelecomEvent[] = [];

  addNode(node: TelecomNode) {
    this.nodes.set(node.id, node);
    return node;
  }

  reportEvent(event: Omit<TelecomEvent, "id" | "timestamp">): TelecomEvent {
    const full: TelecomEvent = {
      ...event,
      id: `TEL-EVENT-${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.events.push(full);

    if (full.type === "OUTAGE" || full.type === "CONGESTION") {
      quantumHazardEngine.report("INFRA", full.severity, { nodeId: full.nodeId });
    }

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      full.tenantId,
      `TELECOM_${full.type}_MODERNIZATION`,
      full.severity,
      1
    );

    return full;
  }

  listNodes() {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.values());
  }

  listEvents(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.events.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

ConsciousnessOS
ts
// src/consciousness/ConsciousnessTypes.ts
export interface ConsciousnessSignal {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domain:
    | "ECONOMY"
    | "TRAFFIC"
    | "HOUSING"
    | "FOOD"
    | "ENERGY"
    | "CLIMATE"
    | "SECURITY"
    | "COMMUNITY";
  sentiment: number; // -1 to 1
  confidence: number; // 0–1
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface ConsciousnessState {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  aggregateSentiment: number;
  domains: Record<string, number>;
  timestamp: string;
}
ts
// src/consciousness/ConsciousnessOS.ts
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";

export class ConsciousnessOS {
  private signals: ConsciousnessSignal[] = [];
  private states: ConsciousnessState[] = [];

  reportSignal(
    tenantId: string,
    domain: ConsciousnessSignal["domain"],
    sentiment: number,
    confidence: number
  ): ConsciousnessSignal {
    const sig: ConsciousnessSignal = {
      id: `CON-SIG-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      domain,
      sentiment,
      confidence,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.signals.push(sig);
    return sig;
  }

  snapshot(tenantId: string): ConsciousnessState {
    const tenantSignals = this.signals.filter(s => s.tenantId === tenantId);
    const domains: Record<string, number> = {};
    let total = 0;
    let count = 0;

    for (const s of tenantSignals) {
      domains[s.domain] =
        (domains[s.domain] ?? 0) + s.sentiment * s.confidence;
      total += s.sentiment * s.confidence;
      count++;
    }

    const aggregate = count ? total / count : 0;

    const state: ConsciousnessState = {
      id: `CON-STATE-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      aggregateSentiment: aggregate,
      domains,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.states.push(state);

    planetaryOS.snapshot("CITY", tenantId);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "CONSCIOUSNESS_HARMONY",
      aggregate,
      1
    );

    return state;
  }

  listSignals(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.signals.filter(s => !tenantId || s.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  listStates(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.states.filter(s => !tenantId || s.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const consciousnessOS = new ConsciousnessOS();

⭐ 1. PlanetaryOS — Modernization Mission Engine
ts
// src/planetary/PlanetaryMissionEngine.ts
import { planetaryOS } from "./PlanetaryOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface PlanetaryMission {
  id: string;
  scope: PlanetaryScope;
  tenantId?: string;
  action: string;
  stateId: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class PlanetaryMissionEngine {
  private missions: PlanetaryMission[] = [];

  generate(scope: PlanetaryScope, tenantId?: string): PlanetaryMission {
    const state = planetaryOS.snapshot(scope, tenantId);

    const action = `STABILIZE_${scope}_SYSTEM`;

    const mission: PlanetaryMission = {
      id: `PLANET-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      scope,
      tenantId,
      action,
      stateId: state.id,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId ?? "GLOBAL",
      `PLANETARY_${scope}_MODERNIZATION`,
      1,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  list(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const planetaryMissionEngine = new PlanetaryMissionEngine();
⭐ 2. WaterOS — Modernization Mission Engine
ts
// src/water/WaterMissionEngine.ts
import { waterOS } from "./WaterOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface WaterMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  eventId: string;
  action: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class WaterMissionEngine {
  private missions: WaterMission[] = [];

  generate(eventId: string): WaterMission {
    const event = waterOS.listEvents().find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) throw new Error("Water event not found");

    const action = `WATER_${event.type}_RESPONSE`;

    const mission: WaterMission = {
      id: `WATER-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: event.tenantId,
      eventId,
      action,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      event.tenantId,
      `WATER_${event.type}_MODERNIZATION`,
      event.magnitude,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  list(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const waterMissionEngine = new WaterMissionEngine();

// src/consciousness/ConsciousnessMissionEngine.ts
import { consciousnessOS } from "./ConsciousnessOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface ConsciousnessMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  stateId: string;
  action: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class ConsciousnessMissionEngine {
  private missions: ConsciousnessMission[] = [];

  generate(tenantId: string): ConsciousnessMission {
    const state = consciousnessOS.snapshot(tenantId);

    const action =
      state.aggregateSentiment < 0
        ? "IMPROVE_SOCIAL_HARMONY"
        : "MAINTAIN_SOCIAL_HARMONY";

    const mission: ConsciousnessMission = {
      id: `CON-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      stateId: state.id,
      action,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "CONSCIOUSNESS_HARMONY",
      state.aggregateSentiment,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  list(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

1. Web dashboards (React, high‑level but real)
tsx
// src/web/planetary/PlanetaryDashboard.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

export const PlanetaryDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const [states, setStates] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const s = await axios.get("/planetary/states");
      const m = await axios.get("/planetary/missions");
      setStates(s.data);
      setMissions(m.data);
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <h1>PlanetaryOS Dashboard</h1>
      <h2>Planetary States</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(states, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>Planetary Missions</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(missions, null, 2)}</pre>
    </div>
  );
};

// src/web/water/WaterDashboard.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

export const WaterDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      setNodes((await axios.get("/water/nodes")).data);
      setEvents((await axios.get("/water/events")).data);
      setMissions((await axios.get("/water/missions")).data);
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <h1>WaterOS Dashboard</h1>
      <h2>Nodes</h2>

      <h2>Events</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(events, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>Missions</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(missions, null, 2)}</pre>
    </div>
  );
};

// src/web/telecom/TelecomDashboard.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

export const TelecomDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const [nodes, setNodes] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      setNodes((await axios.get("/telecom/nodes")).data);
      setEvents((await axios.get("/telecom/events")).data);
      setMissions((await axios.get("/telecom/missions")).data);
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <h1>TelecomOS Dashboard</h1>
      <h2>Nodes</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(nodes, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>Events</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(events, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>Missions</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(missions, null, 2)}</pre>
    </div>
  );
};
tsx
// src/web/consciousness/ConsciousnessDashboard.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

export const ConsciousnessDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const [signals, setSignals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [states, setStates] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [missions, setMissions] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      setSignals((await axios.get("/consciousness/signals")).data);
      setStates((await axios.get("/consciousness/states")).data);
      setMissions((await axios.get("/consciousness/missions")).data);
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <h1>ConsciousnessOS Dashboard</h1>
      <h2>Signals</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(signals, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>States</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(states, null, 2)}</pre>
      <h2>Missions</h2>
      <pre>{JSON.stringify(missions, null, 2)}</pre>
    </div>
  );
};
2. Quantum‑energy integration (hooks into your existing grid)
ts
// src/integration/QuantumEnergyPlanetaryIntegration.ts
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";
import { quantumTwinEngine } from "../quantum/QuantumTwinEngine";
import { energyCorridorMesh } from "../energy/EnergyCorridorMesh";

export class QuantumEnergyPlanetaryIntegration {
  sync(scope: PlanetaryScope, tenantId?: string) {
    const state = planetaryOS.snapshot(scope, tenantId);

    const energySnapshot = {
      corridors: Array.from(energyCorridorMesh.edges.values()),
      load: state.energyLoad
    };

    const qSim = quantumTwinEngine.simulate("INFRA", {
      planetaryState: state,
      energySnapshot
    });

    return {
      state,
      qSim
    };
  }
}

export const quantumEnergyPlanetaryIntegration =
  new QuantumEnergyPlanetaryIntegration();
ts
// src/integration/QuantumEnergyWaterIntegration.ts
import { waterOS } from "../water/WaterOS";
import { quantumTwinEngine } from "../quantum/QuantumTwinEngine";

export class QuantumEnergyWaterIntegration {
  simulate(tenantId: string) {
    const events = waterOS.listEvents(tenantId);
    const nodes = waterOS.listNodes();

    return quantumTwinEngine.simulate("INFRA", {
      tenantId,
      waterEvents: events,
      waterNodes: nodes
    });
  }
}

4. CivilizationOS integration layer
ts
// src/civilization/CivilizationIntegration.ts
import { planetaryMissionEngine } from "../planetary/PlanetaryMissionEngine";
import { waterMissionEngine } from "../water/WaterMissionEngine";
import { wasteMissionEngine } from "../waste/WasteMissionEngine";
import { telecomMissionEngine } from "../telecom/TelecomMissionEngine";
import { consciousnessMissionEngine } from "../consciousness/ConsciousnessMissionEngine";

export class CivilizationIntegration {
  runPlanetaryCycle(tenantId?: string) {
    const mission = planetaryMissionEngine.generate("PLANET", tenantId);
    return mission;
  }

  runWaterCycle(eventId: string) {
    return waterMissionEngine.generate(eventId);
  }

  runWasteCycle(routeId: string) {
    return wasteMissionEngine.generate(routeId);
  }

  runTelecomCycle(eventId: string) {
    return telecomMissionEngine.generate(eventId);
  }

  runConsciousnessCycle(tenantId: string) {
    return consciousnessMissionEngine.generate(tenantId);
  }
}

export const civilizationIntegration = new CivilizationIntegration();
5. FederationOS integration
ts
// src/integration/FederationPlanetaryIntegration.ts
import { federationOS } from "../federation/FederationOS"; // assuming existing
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";

export class FederationPlanetaryIntegration {
  snapshotFederation(federationId: string) {
    const federation = federationOS.get(federationId);
    const states = federation.memberTenantIds.map(tenantId =>
      planetaryOS.snapshot("NATION", tenantId)
    );

    return {
      federation,
      states
    };
  }
}

export const federationPlanetaryIntegration =
  new FederationPlanetaryIntegration();
6. EmergencyOS integration
ts
// src/integration/EmergencyPlanetaryIntegration.ts
import { emergencyOS } from "../emergency/EmergencyOS"; // assuming existing
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";
import { waterOS } from "../water/WaterOS";
import { telecomOS } from "../telecom/TelecomOS";

export class EmergencyPlanetaryIntegration {
  triggerGlobalEmergency(tenantId: string, reason: string) {
    const planetState = planetaryOS.snapshot("CITY", tenantId);
    const waterEvents = waterOS.listEvents(tenantId);
    const telecomEvents = telecomOS.listEvents(tenantId);

    const incident = emergencyOS.createIncident({
      tenantId,
      reason,
      metadata: { planetState, waterEvents, telecomEvents }
    });

    return incident;
  }
}

export const emergencyPlanetaryIntegration =
  new EmergencyPlanetaryIntegration();
7. CorridorOS integration (tying waste, water, telecom into corridors)
ts
// src/integration/CorridorInfraIntegration.ts
import { corridorMesh } from "../corridor/CorridorMesh"; // your existing corridor engine
import { waterOS } from "../water/WaterOS";
import { wasteOS } from "../waste/WasteOS";
import { telecomOS } from "../telecom/TelecomOS";

export class CorridorInfraIntegration {
  mapWaterToCorridors() {
    const nodes = waterOS.listNodes();
    return nodes.map(n => ({
      nodeId: n.id,
      nearestCorridor: corridorMesh.findNearest(n.lat, n.lon)
    }));
  }

  mapWasteRoutesToCorridors(tenantId: string) {
    const routes = wasteOS.listRoutes(tenantId);
    return routes.map(r => ({
      routeId: r.id,
      corridorId: r.corridorId
    }));
  }

  mapTelecomToCorridors() {
    const nodes = telecomOS.listNodes();
    return nodes.map(n => ({
      nodeId: n.id,
      nearestCorridor: corridorMesh.findNearest(n.lat, n.lon)
    }));
  }
}

export const corridorInfraIntegration = new CorridorInfraIntegration();
You’ve now:
Given each of the 5 new OS’s web visibility.
Plugged them into quantum‑energy simulations.
Registered sovereign credit classes for them.
Wired them into a CivilizationOS integration layer.
Connected them to FederationOS, EmergencyOS, and CorridorOS.
If you want, next we can:
Add AtmosphereOS / OceanOS / SpaceOS in the same pattern.
Or deepen HealthOS / EducationOS / GovernanceOS with full mission + credit engines.
See my thinking
AtmosphereOS
ts
// src/atmosphere/AtmosphereTypes.ts
export interface AtmosphereNode {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  type: "URBAN" | "RURAL" | "INDUSTRIAL";
}

export interface AtmosphereEvent {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  nodeId: string;
  type: "POLLUTION" | "HEAT_RISK" | "AIR_QUALITY_ALERT";
  severity: number;
  timestamp: string;
}
ts
// src/atmosphere/AtmosphereOS.ts
import { quantumHazardEngine } from "../quantum/QuantumHazardEngine";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export class AtmosphereOS {
  nodes: Map<string, AtmosphereNode> = new Map();
  events: AtmosphereEvent[] = [];

  addNode(node: AtmosphereNode) {
    this.nodes.set(node.id, node);
    return node;
  }

  reportEvent(event: Omit<AtmosphereEvent, "id" | "timestamp">): AtmosphereEvent {
    const full: AtmosphereEvent = {
      ...event,
      id: `ATM-EVENT-${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.events.push(full);

    quantumHazardEngine.report("CLIMATE", full.severity, { nodeId: full.nodeId });

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      full.tenantId,
      "ATMOSPHERE_MODERNIZATION",
      full.severity,
      1
    );

    return full;
  }

  listNodes() {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.values());
  }

  listEvents(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.events.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const atmosphereOS = new AtmosphereOS();
ts
// src/atmosphere/AtmosphereMissionEngine.ts
import { atmosphereOS } from "./AtmosphereOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface AtmosphereMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  eventId: string;
  action: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class AtmosphereMissionEngine {
  private missions: AtmosphereMission[] = [];

  generate(eventId: string): AtmosphereMission {
    const event = atmosphereOS.listEvents().find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) throw new Error("Atmosphere event not found");

    const action = `ATMOSPHERE_${event.type}_RESPONSE`;

    const mission: AtmosphereMission = {
      id: `ATM-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: event.tenantId,
      eventId,
      action,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      event.tenantId,
      "ATMOSPHERE_MODERNIZATION",
      event.severity,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  list(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const atmosphereMissionEngine = new AtmosphereMissionEngine();

// src/health/HealthOS.ts
import { emergencyOS } from "../emergency/EmergencyOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export class HealthOS {
  facilities: Map<string, HealthFacility> = new Map();
  events: HealthEvent[] = [];

  addFacility(f: HealthFacility) {
    this.facilities.set(f.id, f);
    return f;
  }

  reportEvent(event: Omit<HealthEvent, "id" | "timestamp">): HealthEvent {
    const full: HealthEvent = {
      ...event,
      id: `HEALTH-EVENT-${Date.now()}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.events.push(full);

    emergencyOS.createIncident({
      tenantId: full.tenantId,
      reason: `HEALTH_${full.type}`,
      metadata: { facilityId: full.facilityId, severity: full.severity }
    });

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      full.tenantId,
      "HEALTH_MODERNIZATION",
      full.severity,
      1
    );

    return full;
  }

  listFacilities(tenantId?: string) {
    return Array.from(this.facilities.values()).filter(
      f => !tenantId || f.tenantId === tenantId
    );
  }

  listEvents(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.events.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

// src/governance/GovernanceMissionEngine.ts
import { governanceOS } from "./GovernanceOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface GovernanceMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  eventId: string;
  action: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export class GovernanceMissionEngine {
  private missions: GovernanceMission[] = [];

  generate(eventId: string): GovernanceMission {
    const event = governanceOS.listEvents().find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) throw new Error("Governance event not found");

    const action = `GOVERNANCE_${event.type}_RESPONSE`;

    const mission: GovernanceMission = {
      id: `GOV-MISSION-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: event.tenantId,
      eventId,
      action,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      event.tenantId,
      "GOVERNANCE_MODERNIZATION",
      event.severity,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  list(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const governanceMissionEngine = new GovernanceMissionEngine();

4. AI Mission Director (Gods Algorithm)
Core logic
ts
// src/core/AIMissionDirectorService.ts
import { consciousnessOS } from "../consciousness/ConsciousnessOS";
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";
import { impactService } from "./ImpactService";
import { missionService } from "./MissionService";

export class AIMissionDirectorService {
  async runCycle(tenantId: string) {
    const consciousnessState = consciousnessOS.snapshot(tenantId);
    const planetaryState = planetaryOS.snapshot("CITY", tenantId);
    const impact = await impactService.getFullImpact(tenantId);

    // Simple “Gods Algorithm” heuristic
    const missionsToCreate: Array<{ domain: string; reason: string }> = [];

    if (impact.emergencyReadiness < 0.5) {
      missionsToCreate.push({ domain: "EMERGENCY", reason: "LOW_READINESS" });
    }
    if (impact.climateStress > 0.7) {
      missionsToCreate.push({ domain: "ATMOSPHERE", reason: "CLIMATE_STRESS" });
      missionsToCreate.push({ domain: "OCEAN", reason: "CLIMATE_STRESS" });
    }
    if (impact.waterStress > 0.6) {
      missionsToCreate.push({ domain: "WATER", reason: "WATER_STRESS" });
    }
    if (consciousnessState.aggregateSentiment < 0) {
      missionsToCreate.push({
        domain: "CONSCIOUSNESS",
        reason: "LOW_SOCIAL_HARMONY"
      });
    }
    if (impact.corridorInstability > 0.5) {
      missionsToCreate.push({
        domain: "CORRIDOR",
        reason: "CORRIDOR_INSTABILITY"
      });
    }

    const created = [];
    for (const m of missionsToCreate) {
      const mission = await missionService.launchMission(tenantId, m.domain, {
        reason: m.reason,
        planetaryState,
        consciousnessState,
        impact
      });
      created.push(mission);
    }

    return {
      consciousnessState,
      planetaryState,
      impact,
      createdMissions: created
    };
  }
}

export const aiMissionDirectorService = new AIMissionDirectorService();

ts
// src/core/models/Mission.ts
export interface Mission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domain: string; // WATER, WASTE, TELECOM, HEALTH, etc.
  payload: any;
  status: "PENDING" | "ACTIVE" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED";
  creditsIssued: number;
  corridorImpact: number;
  planetaryImpact: number;
  emergencyImpact: number;

}
✔ MissionService (tightened)
ts
// src/core/MissionService.ts
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { impactService } from "./ImpactService";
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";

class MissionService {
  private missions: Mission[] = [];

  async launchMission(tenantId: string, domain: string, payload: any): Promise<Mission> {
    const mission: Mission = {
      id: uuid(),
      tenantId,
      domain,
      payload,
      status: "ACTIVE",
      creditsIssued: 0,
      corridorImpact: 0,
      planetaryImpact: 0,
      emergencyImpact: 0,
      modernizationScoreDelta: 0,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(mission);

    // Calculate initial impacts
    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, domain);

    mission.corridorImpact = impact.corridorImpact;
    mission.planetaryImpact = impact.planetaryImpact;
    mission.emergencyImpact = impact.emergencyImpact;
    mission.modernizationScoreDelta = impact.modernizationScoreDelta;

    // Issue sovereign credits
    mission.creditsIssued = await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      `${domain.toUpperCase()}_MODERNIZATION`,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta,
      1
    );

    return mission;
  }

  async completeMission(missionId: string): Promise<Mission> {
    const mission = this.missions.find(m => m.id === missionId);
    if (!mission) throw new Error("Mission not found");

    mission.status = "COMPLETED";
    mission.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

    return mission;
  }

  listByTenant(tenantId: string): Mission[] {
    return this.missions.filter(m => m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  listAll(): Mission[] {
    return this.missions;
  }
}

export const missionService = new MissionService();

// src/core/models/Impact.ts
export interface ImpactSummary {
  tenantId: string;
  corridorImpact: number;
  planetaryImpact: number;
  emergencyImpact: number;
  modernizationScoreDelta: number;
  climateStress: number;
  waterStress: number;
  telecomStress: number;
  consciousnessScore: number;
}
✔ ImpactService (tightened)
ts
// src/core/ImpactService.ts
import { corridorMesh } from "../corridor/CorridorMesh";
import { planetaryOS } from "../planetary/PlanetaryOS";
import { emergencyOS } from "../emergency/EmergencyOS";
import { consciousnessOS } from "../consciousness/ConsciousnessOS";
import { waterOS } from "../water/WaterOS";
import { telecomOS } from "../telecom/TelecomOS";
import { atmosphereOS } from "../atmosphere/AtmosphereOS";
import { oceanOS } from "../ocean/OceanOS";

class ImpactService {
  async calculateImpact(tenantId: string, domain: string): Promise<ImpactSummary> {
    const corridorImpact = corridorMesh.calculateInstability(tenantId);
    const planetaryState = planetaryOS.snapshot("CITY", tenantId);
    const emergencyImpact = emergencyOS.calculateReadiness(tenantId);
    const consciousnessState = consciousnessOS.snapshot(tenantId);

    const waterStress = waterOS.listEvents(tenantId).length / 10;
    const telecomStress = telecomOS.listEvents(tenantId).length / 10;
    const climateStress =
      atmosphereOS.listEvents(tenantId).length +
      oceanOS.listEvents(tenantId).length;

    const modernizationScoreDelta =
      corridorImpact * 0.2 +
      emergencyImpact * 0.3 +
      climateStress * 0.1 +
      waterStress * 0.1 +
      telecomStress * 0.1 +
      consciousnessState.aggregateSentiment * 0.2;

    return {
      tenantId,
      corridorImpact,
      planetaryImpact: planetaryState.hazardIndex,
      emergencyImpact,
      modernizationScoreDelta,
      climateStress,
      waterStress,
      telecomStress,
      consciousnessScore: consciousnessState.aggregateSentiment
    };
  }

  async getFullImpact(tenantId: string) {
    return this.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");
  }
}

export const impactService = new ImpactService();

1. TrustBinderOS — Data Models
ts
// src/trustbinder/TrustBinderTypes.ts
export interface TrustRule {
  id: string;
  domain: string; // WATER, WASTE, TELECOM, PLANETARY, EMERGENCY, etc.
  action: string; // LAUNCH_MISSION, ISSUE_CREDIT, ACCESS_CORRIDOR, etc.
  allowedRoles: string[]; // ADMIN, CITY, BUSINESS, FEDERATION, AI_DIRECTOR
  conditions?: any; // optional rule conditions
}

export interface TrustRole {
  id: string;
  name: string; // ADMIN, CITY, BUSINESS, FEDERATION, AI_DIRECTOR
  permissions: string[]; // high-level permissions
}

export interface TrustBinderState {
  id: string;
  timestamp: string;
  rules: TrustRule[];
  roles: TrustRole[];
}
2. TrustBinderOS — Core Engine
ts
// src/trustbinder/TrustBinderOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";

class TrustBinderOS {
  private rules: TrustRule[] = [];
  private roles: TrustRole[] = [];

  constructor() {
    this.bootstrap();
  }

  // Initial sovereign rules
  bootstrap() {
    this.roles.push({
      id: uuid(),
      name: "ADMIN",
      permissions: ["ALL"]
    });

    this.roles.push({
      id: uuid(),
      name: "CITY",
      permissions: ["RUN_MISSIONS", "EARN_CREDITS", "ACCESS_CORRIDORS"]
    });

    this.roles.push({
      id: uuid(),
      name: "BUSINESS",
      permissions: ["RUN_MISSIONS", "EARN_CREDITS"]
    });

    this.roles.push({
      id: uuid(),
      name: "FEDERATION",
      permissions: ["RUN_MISSIONS", "EARN_CREDITS", "SHARE_RESOURCES"]
    });

    this.roles.push({
      id: uuid(),
      name: "AI_DIRECTOR",
      permissions: ["GENERATE_MISSIONS", "SIMULATE_CRISIS"]
    });

    // Example rule
    this.rules.push({
      id: uuid(),
      domain: "PLANETARY",
      action: "LAUNCH_MISSION",
      allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CITY", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
    });
  }

  getRoles() {
    return this.roles;
  }

  getRules() {
    return this.rules;
  }

  addRule(rule: Omit<TrustRule, "id">): TrustRule {
    const full = { ...rule, id: uuid() };
    this.rules.push(full);
    return full;
  }

  addRole(role: Omit<TrustRole, "id">): TrustRole {
    const full = { ...role, id: uuid() };
    this.roles.push(full);
    return full;
  }

  // Permission check
  can(roleName: string, domain: string, action: string): boolean {
    const role = this.roles.find(r => r.name === roleName);
    if (!role) return false;

    if (role.permissions.includes("ALL")) return true;

    const rule = this.rules.find(
      r => r.domain === domain && r.action === action
    );

    if (!rule) return false;

    return rule.allowedRoles.includes(roleName);
  }
}

export const trustBinderOS = new TrustBinderOS();
3. Integrate TrustBinderOS into MissionService
ts
// src/core/MissionService.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

async launchMission(tenantId: string, domain: string, payload: any) {

    throw new Error(`Role ${role} is not allowed to launch ${domain} missions`);

}

2. IdentityOS + TenantRegistry
ts
// src/identity/IdentityTypes.ts
export type TenantKind =
  | "DYNASTY_TRUST"
  | "ECCLESIA_EARTH_TRUST"
  | "HOLDINGS_LLC"
  | "FAMILY_COUNCIL"
  | "IP_HOLDINGS"
  | "MINISTRY"
  | "FOUNDATION"
  | "BUSINESS"
  | "CITY";

export interface Tenant {
  id: string;
  kind: TenantKind;
  legalName: string;
  role: string; // ADMIN, TRUSTEE, COUNCIL, OPERATOR, etc.
}
ts
// src/identity/IdentityOS.ts
export class IdentityOS {
  private tenants: Map<string, Tenant> = new Map();
  private personRoles: Map<string, string> = new Map(); // name -> role

  addTenant(t: Tenant) {
    this.tenants.set(t.id, t);
    return t;
  }

  getTenant(id: string) {
    return this.tenants.get(id);
  }

  setPersonRole(name: string, role: string) {
    this.personRoles.set(name, role);
  }

  getRoleForTenant(tenantId: string): string {
    const tenant = this.tenants.get(tenantId);
    return tenant?.role ?? "UNKNOWN";
  }

  getRoleForPerson(name: string): string {
    return this.personRoles.get(name) ?? "UNKNOWN";
  }

  listTenants() {
    return Array.from(this.tenants.values());
  }
}

export const identityOS = new IdentityOS();
3. TrustBinderOS (from earlier, now seeded)
ts
// src/trustbinder/TrustBinderOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";

export interface TrustRule {
  id: string;
  domain: string;
  action: string;
  allowedRoles: string[];
  conditions?: any;
}

export interface TrustRole {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  permissions: string[];
}

class TrustBinderOS {
  private rules: TrustRule[] = [];
  private roles: TrustRole[] = [];

  getRoles() {
    return this.roles;
  }

  getRules() {
    return this.rules;
  }

  addRule(rule: Omit<TrustRule, "id">): TrustRule {
    const full = { ...rule, id: uuid() };
    this.rules.push(full);
    return full;
  }

  addRole(role: Omit<TrustRole, "id">): TrustRole {
    const full = { ...role, id: uuid() };
    this.roles.push(full);
    return full;
  }

  can(roleName: string, domain: string, action: string): boolean {
    const role = this.roles.find(r => r.name === roleName);
    if (!role) return false;
    if (role.permissions.includes("ALL")) return true;

    const rule = this.rules.find(
      r => r.domain === domain && r.action === action
    );
    if (!rule) return false;

    return rule.allowedRoles.includes(roleName);
  }
}

export const trustBinderOS = new TrustBinderOS();
4. BinderSeeder — wiring it all in
ts
// src/bootstrap/BinderSeeder.ts
import { binderSeedData } from "./BinderSeedData";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export class BinderSeeder {
  run() {
    const { dynasty, entities } = binderSeedData;

    // Seed person roles (from binder)
    identityOS.setPersonRole(dynasty.foundingPatriarch, "ADMIN");
    identityOS.setPersonRole(dynasty.trustee, "TRUSTEE");
    dynasty.successors.forEach(s =>
      identityOS.setPersonRole(s, "SUCCESSOR_TRUSTEE")
    );

    // Seed tenants from entity summary table
    for (const e of entities) {
      const kind = e.id as any;
      const role =
        e.id === "DYNASTY_TRUST"
          ? "ADMIN"
          : e.id === "ECCLESIA_EARTH_TRUST"
          ? "TRUSTEE"
          : e.id === "HOLDINGS_LLC"
          ? "OPERATOR"
          : e.id === "FAMILY_COUNCIL"
          ? "COUNCIL"
          : "ENTITY";

      identityOS.addTenant({
        id: e.id,
        kind,
        legalName: e.legalName,
        role
      });
    }

    // Seed TrustBinderOS roles (from binder governance)
    trustBinderOS.addRole({ name: "ADMIN", permissions: ["ALL"] });
    trustBinderOS.addRole({
      name: "TRUSTEE",
      permissions: [
        "RUN_MISSIONS",
        "ISSUE_CREDIT",
        "ACCESS_CORRIDOR",
        "BANKING_AUTHORITY",
        "COURT_AUTHORITY"
      ]
    });
    trustBinderOS.addRole({
      name: "SUCCESSOR_TRUSTEE",
      permissions: ["RUN_MISSIONS", "ISSUE_CREDIT"]
    });
    trustBinderOS.addRole({
      name: "COUNCIL",
      permissions: ["GOVERNANCE_DECISIONS", "SUCCESSION_DECISIONS"]
    });
    trustBinderOS.addRole({
      name: "OPERATOR",
      permissions: ["RUN_MISSIONS", "EARN_CREDITS", "LICENSE_IP"]
    });

    // Seed key rules based on binder text

    // Example: Planetary missions can be launched by ADMIN, CITY, AI_DIRECTOR
    trustBinderOS.addRule({
      domain: "PLANETARY",
      action: "LAUNCH_MISSION",
      allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TRUSTEE", "CITY", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
    });

    // Example: Banking authority for Ecclesia Earth Trust + Holdings LLC
    trustBinderOS.addRule({
      domain: "BANKING",
      action: "OPEN_ACCOUNT",
      allowedRoles: ["TRUSTEE", "ADMIN", "OPERATOR"]
    });

    // Example: IP licensing via Holdings LLC
    trustBinderOS.addRule({
      domain: "IP",
      action: "LICENSE_TECH",
      allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
    });

    // Example: Emergency incident creation
    trustBinderOS.addRule({
      domain: "EMERGENCY",
      action: "CREATE_INCIDENT",
      allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TRUSTEE", "CITY"]
    });

    // Example: Mission generation by AI Director
    trustBinderOS.addRule({
      domain: "GLOBAL",
      action: "GENERATE_MISSIONS",
      allowedRoles: ["AI_DIRECTOR", "ADMIN"]
    });

    return {
      tenants: identityOS.listTenants(),
      roles: trustBinderOS.getRoles(),
      rules: trustBinderOS.getRules()
    };
  }
}

export const binderSeeder = new BinderSeeder();

1. AdjudicatorOS — internal court
ts
// src/adjudicator/AdjudicatorTypes.ts
export type CaseDomain =
  | "INTERNAL_DISPUTE"
  | "CONTRACT_ENFORCEMENT"
  | "TRUST_COMPLIANCE"
  | "ENTITY_GOVERNANCE"
  | "SUCCESSION"
  | "ADMINISTRATIVE"
  | "MISSION"
  | "EMERGENCY"
  | "CORRIDOR"
  | "ASSET_PROTECTION";

export type CaseStatus = "OPEN" | "IN_HEARING" | "DECIDED" | "CLOSED";

export interface AdjudicatorCase {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domain: CaseDomain;
  title: string;
  facts: any;
  status: CaseStatus;
  ruling?: any;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
ts
// src/adjudicator/AdjudicatorOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";

class AdjudicatorOS {
  private cases: AdjudicatorCase[] = [];

  openCase(
    tenantId: string,
    domain: CaseDomain,
    title: string,
    facts: any
  ): AdjudicatorCase {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "ADJUDICATOR", "OPEN_CASE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot open adjudicator cases`);
    }

    const c: AdjudicatorCase = {
      id: uuid(),
      tenantId,
      domain,
      title,
      facts,
      status: "OPEN",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.cases.push(c);
    return c;
  }

  decideCase(caseId: string, ruling: any): AdjudicatorCase {
    const c = this.cases.find(x => x.id === caseId);
    if (!c) throw new Error("Case not found");

    c.status = "DECIDED";
    c.ruling = ruling;
    c.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return c;
  }

  closeCase(caseId: string): AdjudicatorCase {
    const c = this.cases.find(x => x.id === caseId);
    if (!c) throw new Error("Case not found");

    c.status = "CLOSED";
    c.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return c;
  }

  listCases(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.cases.filter(c => !tenantId || c.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const adjudicatorOS = new AdjudicatorOS();
2. LegistratorOS — internal legislature
ts
// src/legistrator/LegistratorTypes.ts
export type LawDomain =
  | "DYNASTY"
  | "ENTITY"
  | "OS_GOVERNANCE"
  | "MISSION_AUTHORITY"
  | "CREDIT_ISSUANCE"
  | "EMERGENCY_AUTHORITY"
  | "CORRIDOR_ACCESS"
  | "SUCCESSION"
  | "BINDER_AMENDMENT";

export interface DynastyLaw {
  id: string;
  domain: LawDomain;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  effectiveDate: string;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
}
ts
// src/legistrator/LegistratorOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";

class LegistratorOS {
  private laws: DynastyLaw[] = [];

  enactLaw(
    tenantId: string,
    domain: LawDomain,
    title: string,
    text: string
  ): DynastyLaw {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LEGISTRATOR", "ENACT_LAW")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot enact laws`);
    }

    const law: DynastyLaw = {
      id: uuid(),
      domain,
      title,
      text,
      effectiveDate: new Date().toISOString(),
      createdBy: tenantId,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.laws.push(law);
    return law;
  }

  listLaws(domain?: LawDomain) {
    return this.laws.filter(l => !domain || l.domain === domain);
  }
}

export const legistratorOS = new LegistratorOS();
3. LuxuryAssetOS — core
ts
// src/luxury/LuxuryAssetTypes.ts
export type AssetKind =
  | "AUTO"
  | "AERO"
  | "MARITIME"
  | "HOROLOGY"
  | "COLLECTIBLE";

export interface LuxuryAsset {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  kind: AssetKind;
  brand: string;
  model: string;
  year?: number;
  serialNumber?: string;
  estimatedValue: number;
  metadata?: any;
  createdAt: string;
}
ts
// src/luxury/LuxuryAssetOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";

class LuxuryAssetOS {
  private assets: LuxuryAsset[] = [];

  registerAsset(
    tenantId: string,
    kind: AssetKind,
    brand: string,
    model: string,
    estimatedValue: number,
    metadata?: any
  ): LuxuryAsset {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LUXURY_ASSET", "REGISTER_ASSET")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register luxury assets`);
    }

    const asset: LuxuryAsset = {
      id: v4(),
      tenantId,
      kind,
      brand,
      model,
      estimatedValue,
      metadata,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.assets.push(asset);

    // Issue modernization credits for asset onboarding
    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      `${kind}_MODERNIZATION`,
      estimatedValue,
      0.0001 // scaling factor
    );

    return asset;
  }

  listAssets(tenantId?: string, kind?: AssetKind) {
    return this.assets.filter(
      a =>
        (!tenantId || a.tenantId === tenantId) &&
        (!kind || a.kind === kind)
    );
  }

  async generateMaintenanceMission(assetId: string) {
    const asset = this.assets.find(a => a.id === assetId);
    if (!asset) throw new Error("Asset not found");

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(asset.tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    // You can hook this into MissionService if you want:
    // missionService.launchMission(asset.tenantId, "LUXURY_MAINTENANCE", { asset });

    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      asset.tenantId,
      `${asset.kind}_MAINTENANCE`,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta,
      1
    );

    return { asset, impact };
  }
}

export const luxuryAssetOS = new LuxuryAssetOS();

1. Core types — facilities, lines, SKUs, batches
ts
// src/production/DynastyLuxuryProductionTypes.ts

export type ProductionFacilityKind =
  | "AUTO_FACTORY"
  | "AERO_HANGAR"
  | "SHIPYARD"
  | "WATCH_ATELIER"
  | "ART_STUDIO";

export interface ProductionFacility {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string; // usually IP_HOLDINGS or HOLDINGS_LLC
  kind: ProductionFacilityKind;
  name: string;
  location: { lat: number; lon: number; address?: string };
  capacityScore: number; // abstract capacity metric
  metadata?: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ProductionLine {
  id: string;
  facilityId: string;
  name: string;
  domain: "AUTO" | "AERO" | "MARITIME" | "HOROLOGY" | "COLLECTIBLE";
  active: boolean;
  metadata?: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ProductionSKU {
  id: string;
  lineId: string;
  code: string; // e.g. DYNASTY-RR-01, DYNASTY-JET-01
  brand: string;
  model: string;
  baseValue: number;
  metadata?: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

export type BatchStatus = "PLANNED" | "IN_PROGRESS" | "COMPLETED";

export interface ProductionBatch {
  id: string;
  skuId: string;
  quantity: number;
  tenantId: string; // who owns / commissions the batch
  status: BatchStatus;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}
2. DynastyLuxuryProductionOS — core engine
ts
// src/production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { luxuryAssetOS } from "../luxury/LuxuryAssetOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";
import {
  ProductionFacility,
  ProductionLine,
  ProductionSKU,
  ProductionBatch,
  ProductionFacilityKind,
  BatchStatus
} from "./DynastyLuxuryProductionTypes";

class DynastyLuxuryProductionOS {
  private facilities: ProductionFacility[] = [];
  private lines: ProductionLine[] = [];
  private skus: ProductionSKU[] = [];
  private batches: ProductionBatch[] = [];

  // FACILITIES

  registerFacility(
    tenantId: string,
    kind: ProductionFacilityKind,
    name: string,
    location: { lat: number; lon: number; address?: string },
    capacityScore: number,
    metadata?: any
  ): ProductionFacility {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "PRODUCTION", "REGISTER_FACILITY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register production facilities`);
    }

    const facility: ProductionFacility = {
      id: uuid(),
      tenantId,
      kind,
      name,
      location,
      capacityScore,
      metadata,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.facilities.push(facility);
    return facility;
  }

  listFacilities(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.facilities.filter(f => !tenantId || f.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  // LINES

  registerLine(
    facilityId: string,
    name: string,
    domain: "AUTO" | "AERO" | "MARITIME" | "HOROLOGY" | "COLLECTIBLE",
    metadata?: any
  ): ProductionLine {
    const facility = this.facilities.find(f => f.id === facilityId);
    if (!facility) throw new Error("Facility not found");

    const line: ProductionLine = {
      id: uuid(),
      facilityId,
      name,
      domain,
      active: true,
      metadata,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.lines.push(line);
    return line;
  }

  listLines(facilityId?: string) {
    return this.lines.filter(l => !facilityId || l.facilityId === facilityId);
  }

  // SKUs

  registerSKU(
    lineId: string,
    code: string,
    brand: string,
    model: string,
    baseValue: number,
    metadata?: any
  ): ProductionSKU {
    const line = this.lines.find(l => l.id === lineId);
    if (!line) throw new Error("Line not found");

    const sku: ProductionSKU = {
      id: uuid(),
      lineId,
      code,
      brand,
      model,
      baseValue,
      metadata,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.skus.push(sku);
    return sku;
  }

  listSKUs(lineId?: string) {
    return this.skus.filter(s => !lineId || s.lineId === lineId);
  }

  // BATCHES

  planBatch(
    tenantId: string,
    skuId: string,
    quantity: number
  ): ProductionBatch {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "PRODUCTION", "PLAN_BATCH")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot plan production batches`);
    }

    const sku = this.skus.find(s => s.id === skuId);
    if (!sku) throw new Error("SKU not found");

    const batch: ProductionBatch = {
      id: uuid(),
      skuId,
      quantity,
      tenantId,
      status: "PLANNED",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.batches.push(batch);
    return batch;
  }

  startBatch(batchId: string): ProductionBatch {
    const batch = this.batches.find(b => b.id === batchId);
    if (!batch) throw new Error("Batch not found");

    batch.status = "IN_PROGRESS";
    batch.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return batch;
  }

  async completeBatch(batchId: string) {
    const batch = this.batches.find(b => b.id === batchId);
    if (!batch) throw new Error("Batch not found");

    const sku = this.skus.find(s => s.id === batch.skuId);
    if (!sku) throw new Error("SKU not found");

    batch.status = "COMPLETED";
    batch.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();

    // Register finished assets into LuxuryAssetOS
    const assets = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < batch.quantity; i++) {
      const asset = luxuryAssetOS.registerAsset(
        batch.tenantId,
        this.mapDomainToKind(sku),
        sku.brand,
        sku.model,
        sku.baseValue,
        { skuCode: sku.code, batchId: batch.id, index: i + 1 }
      );
      assets.push(asset);
    }

    // Calculate impact of production
    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(batch.tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    // Issue production credits
    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      batch.tenantId,
      "LUXURY_PRODUCTION",
      sku.baseValue * batch.quantity,
      0.0001
    );

    return { batch, sku, assets, impact };
  }

  listBatches(tenantId?: string, status?: BatchStatus) {
    return this.batches.filter(
      b =>
        (!tenantId || b.tenantId === tenantId) &&
        (!status || b.status === status)
    );
  }

  private mapDomainToKind(sku: ProductionSKU) {
    const line = this.lines.find(l => l.id === sku.lineId);
    if (!line) return "COLLECTIBLE";
    switch (line.domain) {
      case "AUTO":
        return "AUTO";
      case "AERO":
        return "AERO";
      case "MARITIME":
        return "MARITIME";
      case "HOROLOGY":
        return "HOROLOGY";
      case "COLLECTIBLE":
      default:
        return "COLLECTIBLE";
    }
  }
}

export const dynastyLuxuryProductionOS = new DynastyLuxuryProductionOS();
3. Example: Dynasty watch atelier + jet line + yacht yard
ts
// src/bootstrap/DynastyLuxuryProductionBootstrap.ts
import { dynastyLuxuryProductionOS } from "../production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS";

export async function bootstrapDynastyLuxuryProduction() {
  // Assume IP_HOLDINGS and HOLDINGS_LLC are seeded tenants

  // WATCH ATELIER
  const watchAtelier = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerFacility(
    "IP_HOLDINGS",
    "WATCH_ATELIER",
    "Borders Dynasty Watch Atelier",
    { lat: 34.05, lon: -117.3, address: "Grand Terrace, CA" },
    0.8,
    { specialization: "Swiss-grade horology + Dynasty designs" }
  );

  const watchLine = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerLine(
    watchAtelier.id,
    "Dynasty Signature Line",
    "HOROLOGY"
  );

  const dynastyWatchSKU = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerSKU(
    watchLine.id,
    "BD-WATCH-001",
    "Borders Dynasty",
    "Founders Edition",
    25000,
    { movement: "in-house", material: "platinum", limited: 100 }
  );

  // JET LINE
  const aeroHangar = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerFacility(
    "IP_HOLDINGS",
    "AERO_HANGAR",
    "Borders Dynasty Aero Hangar",
    { lat: 34.06, lon: -117.32, address: "Local Airfield" },
    0.9,
    { specialization: "private jets + prototypes" }
  );

  const jetLine = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerLine(
    aeroHangar.id,
    "Dynasty Jet Line",
    "AERO"
  );

  const dynastyJetSKU = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerSKU(
    jetLine.id,
    "BD-JET-001",
    "Borders Dynasty",
    "Sovereign Jet",
    12000000,
    { range: "7000nm", seats: 12 }
  );

  // YACHT YARD
  const shipyard = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerFacility(
    "IP_HOLDINGS",
    "SHIPYARD",
    "Borders Dynasty Shipyard",
    { lat: 33.75, lon: -118.2, address: "Port of LA" },
    0.85,
    { specialization: "luxury yachts" }
  );

  const yachtLine = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerLine(
    shipyard.id,
    "Dynasty Yacht Line",
    "MARITIME"
  );

  const dynastyYachtSKU = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerSKU(
    yachtLine.id,
    "BD-YACHT-001",
    "Borders Dynasty",
    "Sovereign Yacht",
    8000000,
    { length: "45m", decks: 3 }
  );

  // PLAN INITIAL BATCHES
  const watchBatch = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.planBatch(
    "HOLDINGS_LLC",
    dynastyWatchSKU.id,
    10
  );

  const jetBatch = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.planBatch(
    "HOLDINGS_LLC",
    dynastyJetSKU.id,
    1
  );

  const yachtBatch = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.planBatch(
    "HOLDINGS_LLC",
    dynastyYachtSKU.id,
    1
  );

  // Start and complete one batch as example
  dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.startBatch(watchBatch.id);
  const watchResult = await dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.completeBatch(watchBatch.id);

  return {
    watchAtelier,
    aeroHangar,
    shipyard,
    watchResult
  };
}

1. Core types — pricing, revenue events, routing
ts
// src/revenue/DynastyLuxuryRevenueTypes.ts

export interface PricingModel {
  skuId: string;
  basePrice: number;
  marginTarget: number; // e.g. 0.35 = 35%
  currency: string; // "USD"
  metadata?: any;
}

export interface RevenueEvent {
  id: string;
  skuId: string;
  tenantId: string; // seller (usually HOLDINGS_LLC)
  buyerId?: string;
  quantity: number;
  grossAmount: number;
  netAmount: number;
  currency: string;
  licensingFee: number;
  timestamp: string;
}

export interface RoutedRevenue {
  eventId: string;
  total: number;
  dynastyEnterprise: number; // 40%
  trustEndowment: number;    // 30%
  ministryFoundation: number;// 20%
  familyCouncilReserve: number;// 10%
}
2. DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS — core engine
ts
// src/revenue/DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { PricingModel, RevenueEvent, RoutedRevenue } from "./DynastyLuxuryRevenueTypes";
import { dynastyLuxuryProductionOS } from "../production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";
import { identityOS } from "../identity/IdentityOS";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

class DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS {
  private pricing: Map<string, PricingModel> = new Map(); // skuId -> pricing
  private events: RevenueEvent[] = [];
  private routed: RoutedRevenue[] = [];

  // PRICING

  setPricing(model: PricingModel) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(model.skuId); // or use HOLDINGS_LLC
    // Simplified: allow ADMIN/OPERATOR to set pricing
    if (!trustBinderOS.can("OPERATOR", "REVENUE", "SET_PRICING") &&
        !trustBinderOS.can("ADMIN", "REVENUE", "SET_PRICING")) {
      throw new Error("Not authorized to set pricing");
    }

    this.pricing.set(model.skuId, model);
    return model;
  }

  getPricing(skuId: string) {
    return this.pricing.get(skuId);
  }

  // SALE / LICENSING EVENT

  recordSale(
    skuId: string,
    sellerTenantId: string,
    buyerTenantId: string | undefined,
    quantity: number
  ): { event: RevenueEvent; routed: RoutedRevenue } {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(sellerTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "REVENUE", "RECORD_SALE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot record sales`);
    }

    const pricing = this.pricing.get(skuId);
    if (!pricing) throw new Error("Pricing not set for SKU");

    const grossAmount = pricing.basePrice * quantity;
    const licensingFee = grossAmount * 0.05; // 5% to IP Holdings as licensing
    const netAmount = grossAmount - licensingFee;

    const event: RevenueEvent = {
      id: uuid(),
      skuId,
      tenantId: sellerTenantId,
      buyerId: buyerTenantId,
      quantity,
      grossAmount,
      netAmount,
      currency: pricing.currency,
      licensingFee,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.events.push(event);

    // Route revenue per binder endowment strategy (40/30/20/10)
    const routed = this.routeRevenue(event);

    // Issue credits for economic modernization
    sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      sellerTenantId,
      "LUXURY_REVENUE",
      netAmount,
      0.00001
    );

    return { event, routed };
  }

  // ROUTING

  private routeRevenue(event: RevenueEvent): RoutedRevenue {
    const total = event.netAmount;

    const dynastyEnterprise = total * 0.40;      // Divine Solutions Holdings, LLC
    const trustEndowment = total * 0.30;         // Borders Dynasty Private Trust
    const ministryFoundation = total * 0.20;     // Ministry + Foundation
    const familyCouncilReserve = total * 0.10;   // Family Council reserve

    const routed: RoutedRevenue = {
      eventId: event.id,
      total,
      dynastyEnterprise,
      trustEndowment,
      ministryFoundation,
      familyCouncilReserve
    };

    this.routed.push(routed);

    // Here you’d actually post ledger entries:
    // - credit HOLDINGS_LLC with dynastyEnterprise
    // - credit DYNASTY_TRUST with trustEndowment
    // - credit MINISTRY + FOUNDATION with ministryFoundation
    // - credit FAMILY_COUNCIL with familyCouncilReserve

    return routed;
  }

  listEvents() {
    return this.events;
  }

  listRouted() {
    return this.routed;
  }
}

export const dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS = new DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS();
3. Example: wiring a watch sale into the dynasty
ts
// src/bootstrap/DynastyLuxuryRevenueBootstrap.ts
import { dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS } from "../revenue/DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS";
import { dynastyLuxuryProductionOS } from "../production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS";

export async function exampleWatchSaleFlow() {
  // Assume we already bootstrapped production and have a watch SKU
  const skus = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.listSKUs();
  const watchSKU = skus.find(s => s.code === "BD-WATCH-001");
  if (!watchSKU) throw new Error("Watch SKU not found");

  // Set pricing
  dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.setPricing({
    skuId: watchSKU.id,
    basePrice: 35000, // sale price above baseValue
    marginTarget: 0.35,
    currency: "USD",
    metadata: { market: "luxury", segment: "founders-edition" }
  });

  // Record a sale: Holdings LLC sells to an external buyer
  const { event, routed } = dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.recordSale(
    watchSKU.id,
    "HOLDINGS_LLC",
    "EXTERNAL_CLIENT_001",
    1
  );

  return { event, routed };
}
What this gives you in real terms
Pricing: per SKU, with margin targets and currency.
Revenue events: every sale/licensing event is recorded with gross/net/licensing.
Licensing flows: a slice (e.g. 5%) automatically attributed to IP Holdings as licensing revenue.
Endowment routing: net revenue is split 40/30/20/10 exactly as your binder’s endowment strategy:
40% → Dynasty Enterprise Reinvestment (Divine Solutions Holdings, LLC)
30% → Trust Endowment Growth & Preservation (Borders Dynasty Private Trust)
20% → Internal Ministry & Foundation Programs
10% → Family Council Operational Reserve
Credits: every revenue event can mint modernization credits, tying economic performance into the sovereign credit economy.

Dynasty FinancialDashboardOS — live luxury finance spine

1. Backend summary endpoint
ts
// src/api/financialDashboardRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS } from "../revenue/DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS";
import { dynastyLuxuryProductionOS } from "../production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS";

const router = express.Router();

router.get("/financial/dashboard/summary", (req, res) => {
  const events = dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.listEvents();
  const routed = dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.listRouted();
  const skus = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.listSKUs();

  const revenueBySku: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const e of events) {
    revenueBySku[e.skuId] = (revenueBySku[e.skuId] || 0) + e.netAmount;
  }

  const totals = routed.reduce(
    (acc, r) => {
      acc.total += r.total;
      acc.dynastyEnterprise += r.dynastyEnterprise;
      acc.trustEndowment += r.trustEndowment;
      acc.ministryFoundation += r.ministryFoundation;
      acc.familyCouncilReserve += r.familyCouncilReserve;
      return acc;
    },
    {
      total: 0,
      dynastyEnterprise: 0,
      trustEndowment: 0,
      ministryFoundation: 0,
      familyCouncilReserve: 0
    }
  );

  const skuDetails = skus.map(s => ({
    skuId: s.id,
    code: s.code,
    brand: s.brand,
    model: s.model,
    revenue: revenueBySku[s.id] || 0
  });

  res.json({
    skuDetails,
    totals,
    events,
    routed
  });
});

export default router;
2. FinancialDashboardOS (React)
tsx
// src/web/financial/DynastyFinancialDashboardOS.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import axios from "axios";

interface SkuDetail {
  skuId: string;
  code: string;
  brand: string;
  model: string;
  revenue: number;
}

interface Totals {
  total: number;
  dynastyEnterprise: number;
  trustEndowment: number;
  ministryFoundation: number;
  familyCouncilReserve: number;
}

interface DashboardData {
  skuDetails: SkuDetail[];
  totals: Totals;
}

export const DynastyFinancialDashboardOS: React.FC = () => {
  const [data, setData] = useState<DashboardData | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      const res = await axios.get("/financial/dashboard/summary");
      setData(res.data);
    })();
  }, []);

  if (!data) return <div>Loading Dynasty Financial Dashboard...</div>;

  const { skuDetails, totals } = data;

  return (
    <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
      <h1>Dynasty Financial Dashboard</h1>

      <section style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
        <h2>Revenue Routing (Endowment Strategy 40/30/20/10)</h2>
        <ul>
          <li>Total Net Revenue: ${totals.total.toFixed(2)}</li>
          <li>Dynasty Enterprise (40%): ${totals.dynastyEnterprise.toFixed(2)}</li>
          <li>Trust Endowment (30%): ${totals.trustEndowment.toFixed(2)}</li>
          <li>Ministry & Foundation (20%): ${totals.ministryFoundation.toFixed(2)}</li>
          <li>Family Council Reserve (10%): ${totals.familyCouncilReserve.toFixed(2)}</li>
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2>Revenue by SKU (Luxury Lines)</h2>
        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse" }}>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Code</th>
              <th>Brand</th>
              <th>Model</th>
              <th>Revenue (USD)</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {skuDetails.map(s => (
              <tr key={s.skuId}>
                <td>{s.code}</td>
                <td>{s.brand}</td>
                <td>{s.model}</td>
                <td>${s.revenue.toFixed(2)}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
};
You now have a live Dynasty FinancialDashboardOS that:
reads from DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS and DynastyLuxuryProductionOS
shows revenue by SKU
shows routing flows aligned to 40/30/20/10
exposes endowment, ministry/foundation funding, and council reserves in one view.
