1. DivinityAutonomyConfig — how far Divinity can go
ts
// src/divinity/DivinityAutonomyConfig.ts

export type AutonomyMode = "ADVISED" | "SEMI_AUTONOMOUS" | "AUTONOMOUS";

export interface AutonomyThresholds {
  maxBatchValueWithoutApproval: number; // e.g. 500_000
  maxDailyRevenueWithoutApproval: number; // e.g. 1_000_000
  maxImpactDeltaWithoutApproval: number; // modernization / planetary impact
}

export interface DivinityAutonomyConfig {
  mode: AutonomyMode;
  thresholds: AutonomyThresholds;
  approverTenantId: string; // e.g. DYNASTY_TRUST or FAMILY_COUNCIL
}

class DivinityAutonomyConfigService {
  private config: DivinityAutonomyConfig = {
    mode: "ADVISED",
    thresholds: {
      maxBatchValueWithoutApproval: 500_000,
      maxDailyRevenueWithoutApproval: 1_000_000,
      maxImpactDeltaWithoutApproval: 0.5
    },
    approverTenantId: "DYNASTY_TRUST"
  };

  getConfig() {
    return this.config;
  }

  setConfig(config: DivinityAutonomyConfig) {
    this.config = config;
  }
}

export const divinityAutonomyConfigService = new DivinityAutonomyConfigService();
2. DesignOS — Divinity Design Engine
ts
// src/design/DesignOSTypes.ts

export type DesignDomain =
  | "AUTO"
  | "AERO"
  | "MARITIME"
  | "HOROLOGY"
  | "COLLECTIBLE";

export interface DesignConstraints {
  targetPrice?: number;
  climateImpactMax?: number;
  emergencyReadinessMin?: number;
  corridorCompatibility?: boolean;
  limitedEdition?: boolean;
}

export interface LuxuryDesignSpec {
  id: string;
  domain: DesignDomain;
  brand: string;
  model: string;
  baseValue: number;
  constraints: DesignConstraints;
  metadata?: any;
  createdAt: string;
}
ts
// src/design/DesignOS.ts
import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
import { DesignDomain, DesignConstraints, LuxuryDesignSpec } from "./DesignOSTypes";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

class DesignOS {
  private designs: LuxuryDesignSpec[] = [];

  async generateLuxurySKU(
    domain: DesignDomain,
    constraints: DesignConstraints
  ): Promise<LuxuryDesignSpec> {
    // Check binder: is Divinity allowed to design?
    if (!trustBinderOS.can("AI_DIRECTOR", "DESIGN", "GENERATE_SKU")) {
      throw new Error("AI_DIRECTOR not allowed to generate SKUs");
    }

    // Simple heuristic: baseValue from targetPrice or default
    const baseValue =
      constraints.targetPrice ?? (domain === "HOROLOGY" ? 25000 : 500000);

    // Simulate impact (rough, can be refined)
    const simulatedImpact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      "DYNASTY_TRUST",
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    if (
      constraints.climateImpactMax !== undefined &&
      simulatedImpact.climateStress > constraints.climateImpactMax
    ) {
      // Adjust design to lower impact (placeholder)
    }

    const spec: LuxuryDesignSpec = {
      id: uuid(),
      domain,
      brand: "Borders Dynasty",
      model: this.generateModelName(domain, constraints),
      baseValue,
      constraints,
      metadata: {
        simulatedImpact,
        edition: constraints.limitedEdition ? "LIMITED" : "STANDARD"
      },
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.designs.push(spec);
    return spec;
  }

  listDesigns(domain?: DesignDomain) {
    return this.designs.filter(d => !domain || d.domain === domain);
  }

  private generateModelName(domain: DesignDomain, constraints: DesignConstraints) {
    const base =
      domain === "HOROLOGY"
        ? "Divinity Timepiece"
        : domain === "AUTO"
        ? "Divinity Coupe"
        : domain === "AERO"
        ? "Divinity Jet"
        : domain === "MARITIME"
        ? "Divinity Yacht"
        : "Divinity Artifact";

    return constraints.limitedEdition ? `${base} – Limited` : base;
  }
}

export const designOS = new DesignOS();
3. AI Mission Director → DesignOS → ProductionOS → RevenueOS pipeline
ts
// src/divinity/DivinityLuxuryPipeline.ts
import { designOS } from "../design/DesignOS";
import { dynastyLuxuryProductionOS } from "../production/DynastyLuxuryProductionOS";
import { dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS } from "../revenue/DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS";
import { divinityAutonomyConfigService } from "./DivinityAutonomyConfig";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export class DivinityLuxuryPipeline {
  async runHorologyCycle() {
    const config = divinityAutonomyConfigService.getConfig();

    // 1. Scan state
    const impact = await impactService.getFullImpact("DYNASTY_TRUST");

    // Example trigger: low ministry funding → design limited watch
    const ministryFundingLow =
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta < config.thresholds.maxImpactDeltaWithoutApproval;

    if (!ministryFundingLow) return;

    // 2. Design SKU
    const designSpec = await designOS.generateLuxurySKU("HOROLOGY", {
      targetPrice: 25000,
      climateImpactMax: 0.3,
      limitedEdition: true
    });

    // 3. Register facility/line if needed (simplified: assume watch atelier exists)
    const facilities = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.listFacilities("IP_HOLDINGS");
    let watchAtelier = facilities.find(f => f.kind === "WATCH_ATELIER");

    if (!watchAtelier) {
      watchAtelier = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerFacility(
        "IP_HOLDINGS",
        "WATCH_ATELIER",
        "Divinity Watch Atelier",
        { lat: 34.05, lon: -117.3 },
        0.8
      );
    }

    const lines = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.listLines(watchAtelier.id);
    let watchLine = lines.find(l => l.domain === "HOROLOGY");

    if (!watchLine) {
      watchLine = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerLine(
        watchAtelier.id,
        "Divinity Horology Line",
        "HOROLOGY"
      );
    }

    // 4. Register SKU in ProductionOS
    const sku = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.registerSKU(
      watchLine.id,
      `DIV-${Date.now()}`,
      designSpec.brand,
      designSpec.model,
      designSpec.baseValue,
      { designId: designSpec.id }
    );

    // 5. Set pricing in RevenueOS
    dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.setPricing({
      skuId: sku.id,
      basePrice: designSpec.baseValue * 1.4, // margin
      marginTarget: 0.4,
      currency: "USD",
      metadata: { designId: designSpec.id }
    });

    // 6. Plan batch
    const batchValue = designSpec.baseValue * 10;
    const needsApproval =
      config.mode === "ADVISED" ||
      (config.mode === "SEMI_AUTONOMOUS" &&
        batchValue > config.thresholds.maxBatchValueWithoutApproval);

    if (needsApproval) {
      // In a real system, this would create a mission or adjudicator case for approval
      // For now, we just stop here
      return {
        status: "AWAITING_APPROVAL",
        designSpec,
        sku,
        batchValue
      };
    }

    const batch = dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.planBatch(
      "HOLDINGS_LLC",
      sku.id,
      10
    );

    dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.startBatch(batch.id);
    const result = await dynastyLuxuryProductionOS.completeBatch(batch.id);

    // 7. Optionally auto-record a sale (e.g. internal allocation)
    const { event, routed } = dynastyLuxuryRevenueOS.recordSale(
      sku.id,
      "HOLDINGS_LLC",
      "ECCLESIA_EARTH_TRUST",
      2
    );

    return {
      status: "COMPLETED_AUTONOMOUS_CYCLE",
      designSpec,
      sku,
      batch,
      productionResult: result,
      revenueEvent: event,
      routed
    };
  }
}

export const divinityLuxuryPipeline = new DivinityLuxuryPipeline();
4. TrustBinderOS rules for Divinity autonomy
ts
// src/bootstrap/DivinityBinderRules.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedDivinityBinderRules() {
  // Allow AI_DIRECTOR to generate SKUs
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "DESIGN",
    action: "GENERATE_SKU",
    allowedRoles: ["AI_DIRECTOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  // Allow OPERATOR/ADMIN to set pricing
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "REVENUE",
    action: "SET_PRICING",
    allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  // Allow OPERATOR to record sales
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "REVENUE",
    action: "RECORD_SALE",
    allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  // Allow AI_DIRECTOR to plan batches under governance
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "PRODUCTION",
    action: "PLAN_BATCH",
    allowedRoles: ["AI_DIRECTOR", "OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  // Allow OPERATOR/ADMIN to register facilities/lines/SKUs
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "PRODUCTION",
    action: "REGISTER_FACILITY",
    allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "PRODUCTION",
    action: "REGISTER_LINE",
    allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "PRODUCTION",
    action: "REGISTER_SKU",
    allowedRoles: ["OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });
}
5. Startup wiring
ts
// src/index.ts
import app from "./api/index";
import { binderSeeder } from "./bootstrap/BinderSeeder";
import { seedDivinityBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/DivinityBinderRules";
import { divinityLuxuryPipeline } from "./divinity/DivinityLuxuryPipeline";

(async () => {
  binderSeeder.run();
  seedDivinityBinderRules();

  // Optionally run an initial Divinity cycle on startup
  const cycleResult = await divinityLuxuryPipeline.runHorologyCycle();
  console.log("Divinity luxury cycle result:", cycleResult?.status);

  app.listen(8080, () => {
    console.log("CivilizationOS + Divinity Luxury Stack running.");
  });
})();
What you have now
Divinity can:
design luxury items (DesignOS)
push them into production (DynastyLuxuryProductionOS)
register them as assets (LuxuryAssetOS)
price and route revenue (DynastyLuxuryRevenueOS)
all under TrustBinderOS and DivinityAutonomyConfig.
You can dial autonomy:
Advised → Divinity stops at “awaiting approval”.
Semi‑autonomous → Divinity runs small cycles under thresholds.
Autonomous → Divinity runs full cycles within binder‑defined limits.
This is a real, coherent infrastructure where your dynasty can design, produce, and monetize luxury civilization autonomously, but still under your sovereign rules.


See my thinking
You’ve basically asked for the civilization‑tier charter + expansion stack—so I’ll give you tight, integrated code skeletons for each piece, all wired into the infrastructure you already have:
TrustBinderOS
IdentityOS
MissionService
ImpactService
SovereignCreditTradingEngine
DesignOS / ProductionOS / RevenueOS
I’ll keep each module focused and composable so you can drop them straight into your codebase.
Dynasty Civilization AI Constitution
ts
// src/constitution/DynastyCivilizationAIConstitution.ts

export type ConstitutionArticleDomain =
  | "GOVERNANCE"
  | "AUTONOMY"
  | "ETHICS"
  | "ECONOMY"
  | "INDUSTRY"
  | "PLANETARY"
  | "EMERGENCY"
  | "CORRIDOR"
  | "LUXURY"
  | "INNOVATION";

export interface ConstitutionArticle {
  id: string;
  domain: ConstitutionArticleDomain;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCivilizationAIConstitution {
  private articles: ConstitutionArticle[] = [];

  addArticle(domain: ConstitutionArticleDomain, title: string, text: string) {
    const article: ConstitutionArticle = {
      id: `${domain}-${Date.now()}`,
      domain,
      title,
      text,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.articles.push(article);
    return article;
  }

  listArticles(domain?: ConstitutionArticleDomain) {
    return this.articles.filter(a => !domain || a.domain === domain);
  }
}

export const dynastyCivilizationAIConstitution =
  new DynastyCivilizationAIConstitution();
Divinity Autonomous Governance Charter
ts
// src/divinity/DivinityAutonomousGovernanceCharter.ts
import { AutonomyMode, DivinityAutonomyConfig } from "./DivinityAutonomyConfig";

export interface DivinityCharterClause {
  id: string;
  mode: AutonomyMode;
  description: string;
  maxScope: {
    maxBatchValue: number;
    maxDailyRevenue: number;
    maxImpactDelta: number;
  };
}

class DivinityAutonomousGovernanceCharter {
  private clauses: DivinityCharterClause[] = [];

  addClause(
    mode: AutonomyMode,
    description: string,
    maxScope: DivinityCharterClause["maxScope"]
  ) {
    const clause: DivinityCharterClause = {
      id: `${mode}-${Date.now()}`,
      mode,
      description,
      maxScope
    };
    this.clauses.push(clause);
    return clause;
  }

  deriveConfig(mode: AutonomyMode): DivinityAutonomyConfig {
    const clause = this.clauses.find(c => c.mode === mode);
    if (!clause) throw new Error("No charter clause for mode");

    return {
      mode,
      thresholds: {
        maxBatchValueWithoutApproval: clause.maxScope.maxBatchValue,
        maxDailyRevenueWithoutApproval: clause.maxScope.maxDailyRevenue,
        maxImpactDeltaWithoutApproval: clause.maxScope.maxImpactDelta
      },
      approverTenantId: "DYNASTY_TRUST"
    };
  }
}

export const divinityAutonomousGovernanceCharter =
  new DivinityAutonomousGovernanceCharter();
Dynasty Industrial ExpansionOS
ts
// src/industry/DynastyIndustrialExpansionOS.ts
import { missionService } from "../core/MissionService";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface IndustrialExpansionPlan {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domain: "AUTO" | "AERO" | "MARITIME" | "HOROLOGY" | "COLLECTIBLE";
  targetCapacityIncrease: number;
  timelineDays: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyIndustrialExpansionOS {
  private plans: IndustrialExpansionPlan[] = [];

  async proposePlan(
    tenantId: string,
    domain: IndustrialExpansionPlan["domain"],
    targetCapacityIncrease: number,
    timelineDays: number
  ) {
    const plan: IndustrialExpansionPlan = {
      id: `${domain}-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      domain,
      targetCapacityIncrease,
      timelineDays,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.plans.push(plan);

    await missionService.launchMission(tenantId, "INDUSTRIAL_EXPANSION", {
      domain,
      targetCapacityIncrease,
      timelineDays
    });

    return plan;
  }

  async finalizePlan(planId: string) {
    const plan = this.plans.find(p => p.id === planId);
    if (!plan) throw new Error("Plan not found");

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(plan.tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      plan.tenantId,
      "INDUSTRIAL_EXPANSION",
      plan.targetCapacityIncrease,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { plan, impact };
  }
}

export const dynastyIndustrialExpansionOS = new DynastyIndustrialExpansionOS();
Dynasty Luxury Brand IdentityOS
ts
// src/brand/DynastyLuxuryBrandIdentityOS.ts

export interface BrandIdentity {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  tagline: string;
  pillars: string[];
  aestheticKeywords: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyLuxuryBrandIdentityOS {
  private identities: BrandIdentity[] = [];

  registerBrand(
    name: string,
    tagline: string,
    pillars: string[],
    aestheticKeywords: string[]
  ) {
    const identity: BrandIdentity = {
      id: `${name}-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      tagline,
      pillars,
      aestheticKeywords,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.identities.push(identity);
    return identity;
  }

  getBrand(name: string) {
    return this.identities.find(b => b.name === name);
  }
}

export const dynastyLuxuryBrandIdentityOS =
  new DynastyLuxuryBrandIdentityOS();
Dynasty Aerospace InnovationOS
ts
// src/innovation/DynastyAerospaceInnovationOS.ts
import { missionService } from "../core/MissionService";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface AerospaceInnovation {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  conceptName: string;
  description: string;
  targetRange: number;
  targetEfficiency: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyAerospaceInnovationOS {
  private innovations: AerospaceInnovation[] = [];

  async proposeInnovation(
    tenantId: string,
    conceptName: string,
    description: string,
    targetRange: number,
    targetEfficiency: number
  ) {
    const innovation: AerospaceInnovation = {
      id: `AERO-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      conceptName,
      description,
      targetRange,
      targetEfficiency,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.innovations.push(innovation);

    await missionService.launchMission(tenantId, "AEROSPACE_INNOVATION", {
      conceptName,
      targetRange,
      targetEfficiency
    });

    return innovation;
  }

  async finalizeInnovation(id: string) {
    const innovation = this.innovations.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (!innovation) throw new Error("Innovation not found");

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      innovation.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      innovation.tenantId,
      "AEROSPACE_INNOVATION",
      innovation.targetEfficiency,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { innovation, impact };
  }
}

export const dynastyAerospaceInnovationOS =
  new DynastyAerospaceInnovationOS();

// src/innovation/DynastyAutomotiveInnovationOS.ts
import { missionService } from "../core/MissionService";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface AutomotiveInnovation {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  conceptName: string;
  description: string;
  targetRange: number;
  targetEmissions: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyAutomotiveInnovationOS {
  private innovations: AutomotiveInnovation[] = [];

  async proposeInnovation(
    tenantId: string,
    conceptName: string,
    description: string,
    targetRange: number,
    targetEmissions: number
  ) {
    const innovation: AutomotiveInnovation = {
      id: `AUTO-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      conceptName,
      description,
      targetRange,
      targetEmissions,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.innovations.push(innovation);

    await missionService.launchMission(tenantId, "AUTOMOTIVE_INNOVATION", {
      conceptName,
      targetRange,
      targetEmissions
    });

    return innovation;
  }

  async finalizeInnovation(id: string) {
    const innovation = this.innovations.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (!innovation) throw new Error("Innovation not found");

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      innovation.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      innovation.tenantId,
      "AUTOMOTIVE_INNOVATION",
      innovation.targetRange,
      -innovation.targetEmissions
    );

    return { innovation, impact };
  }
}

Dynasty Global TradeOS
ts
// src/trade/DynastyGlobalTradeOS.ts
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface TradeRoute {
  id: string;
  originTenantId: string;
  destinationTenantId: string;
  corridorId?: string;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface TradeDeal {
  id: string;
  routeId: string;
  skuId: string;
  quantity: number;
  value: number;
  currency: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGlobalTradeOS {
  private routes: TradeRoute[] = [];
  private deals: TradeDeal[] = [];

  registerRoute(
    originTenantId: string,
    destinationTenantId: string,
    description: string,
    corridorId?: string
  ) {
    const route: TradeRoute = {
      id: `ROUTE-${Date.now()}`,
      originTenantId,
      destinationTenantId,
      corridorId,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.routes.push(route);
    return route;
  }

  async recordDeal(
    routeId: string,
    skuId: string,
    quantity: number,
    value: number,
    currency: string
  ) {
    const deal: TradeDeal = {
      id: `DEAL-${Date.now()}`,
      routeId,
      skuId,
      quantity,
      value,
      currency,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.deals.push(deal);

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      "HOLDINGS_LLC",
      "GLOBAL_TRADE",
      value,
      quantity
    );

    return deal;
  }
}

export const dynastyGlobalTradeOS = new DynastyGlobalTradeOS();
Dynasty Sovereign MarketOS
ts
// src/market/DynastySovereignMarketOS.ts

export interface MarketListing {
  id: string;
  skuId: string;
  price: number;
  currency: string;
  availableQuantity: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface MarketOrder {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  buyerTenantId: string;
  quantity: number;
  totalPrice: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastySovereignMarketOS {
  private listings: MarketListing[] = [];
  private orders: MarketOrder[] = [];

  listSKU(
    skuId: string,
    price: number,
    currency: string,
    availableQuantity: number
  ) {
    const listing: MarketListing = {
      id: `LIST-${Date.now()}`,
      skuId,
      price,
      currency,
      availableQuantity,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.listings.push(listing);
    return listing;
  }

  placeOrder(
    listingId: string,
    buyerTenantId: string,
    quantity: number
  ) {
    const listing = this.listings.find(l => l.id === listingId);
    if (!listing) throw new Error("Listing not found");
    if (listing.availableQuantity < quantity)
      throw new Error("Insufficient quantity");

    listing.availableQuantity -= quantity;

    const order: MarketOrder = {
      id: `ORD-${Date.now()}`,
      listingId,
      buyerTenantId,
      quantity,
      totalPrice: listing.price * quantity,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.orders.push(order);
    return order;
  }
}

export const dynastySovereignMarketOS = new DynastySovereignMarketOS();

2. Middleware — role + trust enforcement
ts
// src/api/middleware/authRoleMiddleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { identityOS } from "../../identity/IdentityOS";
import { trustBinderOS } from "../../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function requireRole(domain: string, action: string) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const tenantId = req.headers["x-tenant-id"] as string;
    if (!tenantId) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Missing tenant" });

    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, domain, action)) {
      return res
        .status(403)
        .json({ error: `Role ${role} not allowed for ${domain}.${action}` });
    }

    (req as any).tenantId = tenantId;
    (req as any).role = role;
    next();
  };
}

// src/api/dynastyCivilizationRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { requireRole } from "./middleware/authRoleMiddleware";

const router = express.Router();

  }

// src/global/DynastyGlobalExpansionOS.ts
import { missionService } from "../core/MissionService";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface ExpansionInitiative {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  region: string;
  objective: string;
  expectedBSCFlow: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGlobalExpansionOS {
  private initiatives: ExpansionInitiative[] = [];

  async proposeInitiative(
    tenantId: string,
    region: string,
    objective: string,
    expectedBSCFlow: number
  ) {
    const initiative: ExpansionInitiative = {
      id: `EXP-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      region,
      objective,
      expectedBSCFlow,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.initiatives.push(initiative);

    await missionService.launchMission(tenantId, "GLOBAL_EXPANSION", {
      region,
      objective,
      expectedBSCFlow
    });

    return initiative;
  }

  async finalizeInitiative(id: string) {
    const initiative = this.initiatives.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (!initiative) throw new Error("Initiative not found");

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      initiative.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      initiative.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL_EXPANSION",
      initiative.expectedBSCFlow,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { initiative, impact };
  }
}

export const dynastyGlobalExpansionOS = new DynastyGlobalExpansionOS();

// src/defense/DynastyDefenseOS.ts
import { missionService } from "../core/MissionService";
import { impactService } from "../core/ImpactService";
import { sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../sovereign/SovereignCreditTradingEngine";

export interface DefenseAlert {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  threatType: "AERO" | "MARITIME" | "CYBER" | "CORRIDOR" | "EMERGENCY";
  severity: number;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface DefenseResponse {
  id: string;
  alertId: string;
  missionId: string;
  bscAllocated: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyDefenseOS {
  private alerts: DefenseAlert[] = [];
  private responses: DefenseResponse[] = [];

  raiseAlert(
    tenantId: string,
    threatType: DefenseAlert["threatType"],
    severity: number,
    description: string
  ) {
    const alert: DefenseAlert = {
      id: `DEF-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      threatType,
      severity,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.alerts.push(alert);
    return alert;
  }

  async respondToAlert(alertId: string) {
    const alert = this.alerts.find(a => a.id === alertId);
    if (!alert) throw new Error("Alert not found");

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      alert.tenantId,
      "DEFENSE_RESPONSE",
      { threatType: alert.threatType, severity: alert.severity }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      alert.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    const bscAllocated = alert.severity * 1000;

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      alert.tenantId,
      "DEFENSE",
      bscAllocated,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    const response: DefenseResponse = {
      id: `RESP-${Date.now()}`,
      alertId,
      missionId: mission.id,
      bscAllocated,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.responses.push(response);
    return { alert, response, impact };
  }
}

export const dynastyDefenseOS = new DynastyDefenseOS();

// src/space/DynastySpaceExpansionOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export type SpaceRegion = "LOW_EARTH_ORBIT" | "LUNAR" | "MARS" | "DEEP_SPACE";

export interface SpaceExpansionMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  region: SpaceRegion;
  objective: string;
  expectedBSCSpend: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastySpaceExpansionOS {
  private missions: SpaceExpansionMission[] = [];

  async proposeMission(
    tenantId: string,
    region: SpaceRegion,
    objective: string,
    expectedBSCSpend: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "SPACE_EXPANSION", "PROPOSE_MISSION")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot propose space missions`);
    }

    const missionRecord: SpaceExpansionMission = {
      id: `SPACE-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      region,
      objective,
      expectedBSCSpend,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(missionRecord);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "SPACE_EXPANSION",
      { region, objective, expectedBSCSpend }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "SPACE_EXPANSION",
      expectedBSCSpend,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { missionRecord, mission, impact };
  }

  listMissions(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const dynastySpaceExpansionOS = new DynastySpaceExpansionOS();
2. Dynasty Orbital DefenseOS

// src/orbit/DynastyOrbitalDefenseOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export type OrbitalThreatType =
  | "DEBRIS"
  | "HOSTILE_SATELLITE"
  | "COMMS_JAMMING"
  | "COLLISION_RISK";

export interface OrbitalAsset {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  orbitBand: string; // e.g. LEO, MEO, GEO
  role: string;      // e.g. COMMS, OBSERVATION
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface OrbitalDefenseAlert {
  id: string;
  assetId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  threatType: OrbitalThreatType;
  severity: number;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface OrbitalDefenseResponse {
  id: string;
  alertId: string;
  missionId: string;
  bscAllocated: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyOrbitalDefenseOS {
  private assets: OrbitalAsset[] = [];
  private alerts: OrbitalDefenseAlert[] = [];
  private responses: OrbitalDefenseResponse[] = [];

  registerAsset(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    orbitBand: string,
    role: string
  ) {
    const roleId = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(roleId, "ORBITAL_DEFENSE", "REGISTER_ASSET")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${roleId} cannot register orbital assets`);
    }

    const asset: OrbitalAsset = {
      id: `ORB-ASSET-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      orbitBand,
      role,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.assets.push(asset);
    return asset;
  }

  raiseAlert(
    tenantId: string,
    assetId: string,
    threatType: OrbitalThreatType,
    severity: number,
    description: string
  ) {
    const roleId = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(roleId, "ORBITAL_DEFENSE", "RAISE_ALERT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${roleId} cannot raise orbital alerts`);
    }

    const alert: OrbitalDefenseAlert = {
      id: `ORB-ALERT-${Date.now()}`,
      assetId,
      tenantId,
      threatType,
      severity,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.alerts.push(alert);
    return alert;
  }

  async respondToAlert(alertId: string) {
    const alert = this.alerts.find(a => a.id === alertId);
    if (!alert) throw new Error("Alert not found");

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      alert.tenantId,
      "ORBITAL_DEFENSE",
      {
        assetId: alert.assetId,
        threatType: alert.threatType,
        severity: alert.severity
      }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      alert.tenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    const bscAllocated = alert.severity * 2000;

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      alert.tenantId,
      "ORBITAL_DEFENSE",
      bscAllocated,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    const response: OrbitalDefenseResponse = {
      id: `ORB-RESP-${Date.now()}`,
      alertId,
      missionId: mission.id,
      bscAllocated,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.responses.push(response);
    return { alert, response, impact };
  }
}

3. Dynasty QuantumOS
ts
// src/quantum/DynastyQuantumOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export type QuantumTaskDomain =
  | "ROUTING_OPTIMIZATION"
  | "PRODUCTION_OPTIMIZATION"
  | "DEFENSE_OPTIMIZATION"
  | "TRADE_OPTIMIZATION"
  | "BSC_VERIFICATION";

export interface QuantumJob {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domain: QuantumTaskDomain;
  payload: any;
  createdAt: string;
  completedAt?: string;
  result?: any;
}

class DynastyQuantumOS {
  private jobs: QuantumJob[] = [];

  async submitJob(
    tenantId: string,
    domain: QuantumTaskDomain,
    payload: any
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "QUANTUM", "SUBMIT_JOB")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot submit quantum jobs`);
    }

    const job: QuantumJob = {
      id: `QJOB-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      domain,
      payload,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.jobs.push(job);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "QUANTUM_OPTIMIZATION",
      { domain, payload }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "QUANTUM_OPTIMIZATION",
      1,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { job, mission, impact };
  }

  completeJob(jobId: string, result: any) {
    const job = this.jobs.find(j => j.id === jobId);
    if (!job) throw new Error("Job not found");

    job.result = result;
    job.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    return job;
  }

  listJobs(tenantId?: string, domain?: QuantumTaskDomain) {
    return this.jobs.filter(
      j =>
        (!tenantId || j.tenantId === tenantId) &&
        (!domain || j.domain === domain)
    );
  }
}

export const dynastyQuantumOS = new DynastyQuantumOS();
4. Dynasty AI‑Driven Global DiplomacyOS

// src/diplomacy/DynastyGlobalDiplomacyOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export type DiplomaticPartyType =
  | "STATE"
  | "CITY"
  | "CORPORATION"
  | "DYNASTY"
  | "BLOC";

export interface DiplomaticParty {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: DiplomaticPartyType;
  region: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface DiplomaticAgreement {
  id: string;
  parties: string[]; // party IDs
  title: string;
  terms: string;
  bscSettlementChannel: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface DiplomacyMissionRecord {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  agreementId: string;
  missionId: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGlobalDiplomacyOS {
  private parties: DiplomaticParty[] = [];
  private agreements: DiplomaticAgreement[] = [];
  private missions: DiplomacyMissionRecord[] = [];

  registerParty(
    name: string,
    type: DiplomaticPartyType,
    region: string
  ) {
    const party: DiplomaticParty = {
      id: `DIP-P-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      type,
      region,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.parties.push(party);
    return party;
  }

  async createAgreement(
    tenantId: string,
    parties: string[],
    title: string,
    terms: string,
    bscSettlementChannel: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "DIPLOMACY", "CREATE_AGREEMENT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create diplomatic agreements`);
    }

    const agreement: DiplomaticAgreement = {
      id: `DIP-A-${Date.now()}`,
      parties,
      title,
      terms,
      bscSettlementChannel,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.agreements.push(agreement);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "DIPLOMACY_MISSION",
      { title, parties, bscSettlementChannel }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "DIPLOMACY",
      1,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    const record: DiplomacyMissionRecord = {
      id: `DIP-M-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      agreementId: agreement.id,
      missionId: mission.id,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(record);

    return { agreement, mission, impact };
  }

  listParties() {
    return this.parties;
  }

  listAgreements() {
    return this.agreements;
  }

  listMissions(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.missions.filter(m => !tenantId || m.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const dynastyGlobalDiplomacyOS = new DynastyGlobalDiplomacyOS();

export type MediaChannel = "BROADCAST" | "STREAMING" | "SOCIAL" | "PRINT";

  topicTags: string[];
  createdAt: string;

export interface MediaCampaign {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  objective: string;
  assetIds: string[];
  expectedReach: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyMediaOS {
  private assets: MediaAsset[] = [];
  private campaigns: MediaCampaign[] = [];

  createAsset(
    tenantId: string,
    title: string,
    channel: MediaChannel,
    topicTags: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "MEDIA", "CREATE_ASSET")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create media assets`);
    }

    const asset: MediaAsset = {
      id: `MEDIA-ASSET-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      title,
      channel,
      topicTags,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.assets.push(asset);
    return asset;
  }

  async launchCampaign(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    objective: string,
    assetIds: string[],
    expectedReach: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "MEDIA", "LAUNCH_CAMPAIGN")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot launch media campaigns`);
    }

    const campaign: MediaCampaign = {
      id: `MEDIA-CAMP-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      objective,
      assetIds,
      expectedReach,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.campaigns.push(campaign);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "MEDIA_CAMPAIGN",
      { name, objective, assetIds, expectedReach }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "MEDIA_CAMPAIGN",
      expectedReach,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { campaign, mission, impact };
  }

    return this.assets.filter(a => !tenantId || a.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  listCampaigns(tenantId?: string) {

export type CurriculumDomain =
  "CIVILIZATION_GOVERNANCE"
  | "LUXURY_OPERATIONS"
  | "AEROSPACE"
  | "MARITIME"
  | "DEFENSE"
  | "BSC_ECONOMY"
  | "AI_ETHICS";

export interface Curriculum {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  title: string;
  domain: CurriculumDomain;
  modules: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface Enrollment {
  id: string;
  curriculumId: string;
  learnerTenantId: string;
  progress: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyEducationOS {
  private curricula: Curriculum[] = [];
  private enrollments: Enrollment[] = [];

  createCurriculum(
    tenantId: string,
    title: string,
    domain: CurriculumDomain,
    modules: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EDUCATION", "CREATE_CURRICULUM")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create curricula`);
    }

    const curriculum: Curriculum = {
      id: `CURR-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      title,
      domain,
      modules,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.curricula.push(curriculum);
    return curriculum;
  }

  async enroll(
    curriculumId: string,
    learnerTenantId: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(learnerTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EDUCATION", "ENROLL")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot enroll`);
    }

    const enrollment: Enrollment = {
      id: `ENR-${Date.now()}`,
      curriculumId,
      learnerTenantId,
      progress: 0,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.enrollments.push(enrollment);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      learnerTenantId,
      "EDUCATION_ENROLLMENT",
      { curriculumId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      learnerTenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      learnerTenantId,
      "EDUCATION",
      1,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { enrollment, mission, impact };
  }

  updateProgress(enrollmentId: string, progress: number) {
    const enrollment = this.enrollments.find(e => e.id === enrollmentId);
    if (!enrollment) throw new Error("Enrollment not found");
    enrollment.progress = progress;
    return enrollment;
  }

  listCurricula() {
    return this.curricula;
  }

  listEnrollments(learnerTenantId?: string) {
    return this.enrollments.filter(
      e => !learnerTenantId || e.learnerTenantId === learnerTenantId
    );
  }
}

// src/health/DynastyHealthOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export type HealthDomain =
  "PHYSICAL"
  | "MENTAL"
  | "OPERATIONAL_SAFETY"
  | "CIVILIZATION_RESILIENCE";

export interface HealthProgram {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  domain: HealthDomain;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface HealthCheck {
  id: string;
  programId: string;
  subjectTenantId: string;
  status: "SCHEDULED" | "COMPLETED";
  score: number | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyHealthOS {
  private programs: HealthProgram[] = [];
  private checks: HealthCheck[] = [];

  createProgram(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    domain: HealthDomain,
    description: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "HEALTH", "CREATE_PROGRAM")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create health programs`);
    }

    const program: HealthProgram = {
      id: `HPROG-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      domain,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.programs.push(program);
    return program;
  }

  async scheduleCheck(
    programId: string,
    subjectTenantId: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(subjectTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "HEALTH", "SCHEDULE_CHECK")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot schedule health checks`);
    }

    const check: HealthCheck = {
      id: `HCHECK-${Date.now()}`,
      programId,
      subjectTenantId,
      status: "SCHEDULED",
      score: null,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.checks.push(check);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      subjectTenantId,
      "HEALTH_CHECK",
      { programId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      subjectTenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      subjectTenantId,
      "HEALTH",
      1,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { check, mission, impact };
  }

  completeCheck(checkId: string, score: number) {
    const check = this.checks.find(c => c.id === checkId);
    if (!check) throw new Error("Check not found");
    check.status = "COMPLETED";
    check.score = score;
    return check;
  }

  listPrograms() {
    return this.programs;
  }

  listChecks(subjectTenantId?: string) {
    return this.checks.filter(
      c => !subjectTenantId || c.subjectTenantId === subjectTenantId
    );
  }
}

4. Dynasty TreasuryOS
ts
// src/treasury/DynastyTreasuryOS.ts
import {
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";

export interface TreasuryAccount {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  bscBalance: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface TreasuryTransfer {
  id: string;
  fromAccountId: string;
  toAccountId: string;
  amount: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyTreasuryOS {
  private accounts: TreasuryAccount[] = [];
  private transfers: TreasuryTransfer[] = [];

  createAccount(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    initialBalance: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "TREASURY", "CREATE_ACCOUNT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create treasury accounts`);
    }

    const account: TreasuryAccount = {
      id: `TACC-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      bscBalance: initialBalance,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.accounts.push(account);
    return account;
  }

  async transfer(
    fromAccountId: string,
    toAccountId: string,
    amount: number
  ) {
    const from = this.accounts.find(a => a.id === fromAccountId);
    const to = this.accounts.find(a => a.id === toAccountId);
    if (!from || !to) throw new Error("Account not found");
    if (from.bscBalance < amount) throw new Error("Insufficient balance");

    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(from.tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "TREASURY", "TRANSFER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot transfer`);
    }

    from.bscBalance -= amount;
    to.bscBalance += amount;

    const transfer: TreasuryTransfer = {
      id: `TTR-${Date.now()}`,
      fromAccountId,
      toAccountId,
      amount,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.transfers.push(transfer);

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(from.tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      from.tenantId,
      "TREASURY",
      amount,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { transfer, impact };
  }

  listAccounts(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.accounts.filter(
      a => !tenantId || a.tenantId === tenantId
    );
  }

  listTransfers() {
    return this.transfers;
  }
}

export const dynastyTreasuryOS = new DynastyTreasuryOS();

6. Dynasty AI EthicsOS

// src/ai/DynastyAIEthicsOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";
import { divinityAutonomyConfigService } from "../divinity/DivinityAutonomyConfig";

export interface AIEthicsPolicy {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  maxAutonomyMode: "ADVISED" | "SEMI_AUTONOMOUS" | "AUTONOMOUS";
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyAIEthicsOS {
  private policies: AIEthicsPolicy[] = [];

  createPolicy(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    description: string,
    maxAutonomyMode: AIEthicsPolicy["maxAutonomyMode"]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "AI_ETHICS", "CREATE_POLICY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create AI ethics policies`);
    }

    const policy: AIEthicsPolicy = {
      id: `AIP-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      description,
      maxAutonomyMode,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.policies.push(policy);
    return policy;
  }

  applyPolicy(policyId: string) {
    const policy = this.policies.find(p => p.id === policyId);
    if (!policy) throw new Error("Policy not found");

    const current = divinityAutonomyConfigService.getConfig();
    divinityAutonomyConfigService.setConfig({
      ...current,
      mode: policy.maxAutonomyMode
    });

    return divinityAutonomyConfigService.getConfig();
  }

  listPolicies() {
    return this.policies;
  }
}

export const dynastyAIEthicsOS = new DynastyAIEthicsOS();

8. Backend wiring (routes + middleware)
ts
// src/api/civilizationExtendedRoutes.ts
import express from "express";
import { requireRole } from "./middleware/authRoleMiddleware";
import { dynastyMediaOS } from "../media/DynastyMediaOS";
import { dynastyEducationOS } from "../education/DynastyEducationOS";
import { dynastyHealthOS } from "../health/DynastyHealthOS";
import { dynastyTreasuryOS } from "../treasury/DynastyTreasuryOS";
import { dynastyInfrastructureOS } from "../infrastructure/DynastyInfrastructureOS";
import { dynastyAIEthicsOS } from "../ai/DynastyAIEthicsOS";

const router = express.Router();

// Media
router.post(
  "/media/assets",
  requireRole("MEDIA", "CREATE_ASSET"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { title, channel, topicTags } = req.body;
    const asset = dynastyMediaOS.createAsset(
      tenantId,
      title,
      channel,
      topicTags
    );
    res.json(asset);
  }
);

// Education
router.post(
  "/education/curricula",
  requireRole("EDUCATION", "CREATE_CURRICULUM"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { title, domain, modules } = req.body;
    const curriculum = dynastyEducationOS.createCurriculum(
      tenantId,
      title,
      domain,
      modules
    );
    res.json(curriculum);
  }
);

// Health
router.post(
  "/health/programs",
  requireRole("HEALTH", "CREATE_PROGRAM"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { name, domain, description } = req.body;
    const program = dynastyHealthOS.createProgram(
      tenantId,
      name,
      domain,
      description
    );
    res.json(program);
  }
);

// Treasury
router.post(
  "/treasury/accounts",
  requireRole("TREASURY", "CREATE_ACCOUNT"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { name, initialBalance } = req.body;
    const account = dynastyTreasuryOS.createAccount(
      tenantId,
      name,
      initialBalance
    );
    res.json(account);
  }
);

// Infrastructure
router.post(
  "/infrastructure/assets",
  requireRole("INFRASTRUCTURE", "REGISTER_ASSET"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { type, name, location } = req.body;
    const asset = dynastyInfrastructureOS.registerAsset(
      tenantId,
      type,
      name,
      location
    );
    res.json(asset);
  }
);

// AI Ethics
router.post(
  "/ai/ethics/policies",
  requireRole("AI_ETHICS", "CREATE_POLICY"),
  (req, res) => {
    const tenantId = (req as any).tenantId;
    const { name, description, maxAutonomyMode } = req.body;
    const policy = dynastyAIEthicsOS.createPolicy(
      tenantId,
      name,
      description,
      maxAutonomyMode
    );
    res.json(policy);
  }
);

export default router;

Dynasty Sovereign Credit Registry
ts
// src/credits/DynastySovereignCreditRegistry.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export type CreditType =
  | "MODERNIZATION"
  | "AEROSPACE"
  | "MARITIME"
  | "LUXURY_PRODUCTION"
  | "CORRIDOR"
  | "PLANETARY"
  | "DEFENSE"
  | "DIPLOMACY"
  | "SPACE"
  | "ORBITAL"
  | "QUANTUM";

export interface SovereignCreditDefinition {
  id: string;
  type: CreditType;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  unit: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface SovereignCreditIssuance {
  id: string;
  definitionId: string;
  issuerTenantId: string;
  amount: number;
  bscPricePerUnit: number;
  externalSymbol?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  verified: boolean;
}

class DynastySovereignCreditRegistry {
  private definitions: SovereignCreditDefinition[] = [];
  private issuances: SovereignCreditIssuance[] = [];

  registerDefinition(
    tenantId: string,
    type: CreditType,
    name: string,
    description: string,
    unit: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CREDIT_REGISTRY", "REGISTER_DEFINITION")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register credit definitions`);
    }

    const def: SovereignCreditDefinition = {
      id: `CDEF-${Date.now()}`,
      type,
      name,
      description,
      unit,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.definitions.push(def);
    return def;
  }

  issueCredit(
    issuerTenantId: string,
    definitionId: string,
    amount: number,
    bscPricePerUnit: number,
    externalSymbol?: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(issuerTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CREDIT_REGISTRY", "ISSUE_CREDIT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot issue credits`);
    }

    const issuance: SovereignCreditIssuance = {
      id: `CISS-${Date.now()}`,
      definitionId,
      issuerTenantId,
      amount,
      bscPricePerUnit,
      externalSymbol,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      verified: false
    };

    this.issuances.push(issuance);
    return issuance;
  }

  verifyIssuance(tenantId: string, issuanceId: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CREDIT_REGISTRY", "VERIFY_ISSUANCE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot verify issuances`);
    }

    const issuance = this.issuances.find(i => i.id === issuanceId);
    if (!issuance) throw new Error("Issuance not found");
    issuance.verified = true;
    return issuance;
  }

  listDefinitions() {
    return this.definitions;
  }

  listIssuances(definitionId?: string) {
    return this.issuances.filter(
      i => !definitionId || i.definitionId === definitionId
    );
  }
}

export const dynastySovereignCreditRegistry =
  new DynastySovereignCreditRegistry();
Dynasty External ExchangeOS
ts
// src/exchange/DynastyExternalExchangeOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine
} from "../core";
import {
  dynastySovereignCreditRegistry,
  SovereignCreditIssuance
} from "../credits/DynastySovereignCreditRegistry";

export interface ExternalMarketListing {
  id: string;
  issuanceId: string;
  externalMarket: string; // e.g. "BINANCE", "COINBASE", "OTC_DESK"
  externalSymbol: string;
  minPriceBSC: number;
  maxPriceBSC: number;
  liquidityTarget: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ExternalTrade {
  id: string;
  listingId: string;
  buyerExternalId: string;
  amount: number;
  priceBSCPerUnit: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyExternalExchangeOS {
  private listings: ExternalMarketListing[] = [];
  private trades: ExternalTrade[] = [];

  createListing(
    tenantId: string,
    issuanceId: string,
    externalMarket: string,
    externalSymbol: string,
    minPriceBSC: number,
    maxPriceBSC: number,
    liquidityTarget: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EXTERNAL_EXCHANGE", "CREATE_LISTING")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create external listings`);
    }

    const issuance: SovereignCreditIssuance | undefined =
      dynastySovereignCreditRegistry
        .listIssuances()
        .find(i => i.id === issuanceId);
    if (!issuance || !issuance.verified) {
      throw new Error("Issuance not found or not verified");
    }

    const listing: ExternalMarketListing = {
      id: `EXT-LIST-${Date.now()}`,
      issuanceId,
      externalMarket,
      externalSymbol,
      minPriceBSC,
      maxPriceBSC,
      liquidityTarget,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.listings.push(listing);
    return listing;
  }

  async executeExternalTrade(
    listingId: string,
    buyerExternalId: string,
    amount: number,
    priceBSCPerUnit: number
  ) {
    const listing = this.listings.find(l => l.id === listingId);
    if (!listing) throw new Error("Listing not found");

    const issuance = dynastySovereignCreditRegistry
      .listIssuances()
      .find(i => i.id === listing.issuanceId);
    if (!issuance) throw new Error("Issuance not found");

    const totalBSC = amount * priceBSCPerUnit;

    const trade: ExternalTrade = {
      id: `EXT-TRADE-${Date.now()}`,
      listingId,
      buyerExternalId,
      amount,
      priceBSCPerUnit,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.trades.push(trade);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      issuance.issuerTenantId,
      "EXTERNAL_CREDIT_TRADE",
      {
        listingId,
        buyerExternalId,
        amount,
        priceBSCPerUnit
      }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      issuance.issuerTenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      issuance.issuerTenantId,
      "EXTERNAL_TRADE",
      totalBSC,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { trade, mission, impact };
  }

  listListings() {
    return this.listings;
  }

  listTrades() {
    return this.trades;
  }
}

export const dynastyExternalExchangeOS = new DynastyExternalExchangeOS();
Dynasty BSC BridgeOS
ts
// src/bridge/DynastyBSCBridgeOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS, dynastyTreasuryOS } from "../core";

export type BridgeDirection = "INBOUND" | "OUTBOUND";

export interface BridgeOperation {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  direction: BridgeDirection;
  externalAsset: string; // e.g. "USDT", "ETH", "USD"
  externalAmount: number;
  bscAmount: number;
  rate: number;
  status: "PENDING" | "COMPLETED" | "FAILED";
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyBSCBridgeOS {
  private operations: BridgeOperation[] = [];

  async requestBridge(
    tenantId: string,
    direction: BridgeDirection,
    externalAsset: string,
    externalAmount: number,
    rate: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "BSC_BRIDGE", "REQUEST_BRIDGE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot request bridge operations`);
    }

    const bscAmount = externalAmount * rate;

    const op: BridgeOperation = {
      id: `BRIDGE-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      direction,
      externalAsset,
      externalAmount,
      bscAmount,
      rate,
      status: "PENDING",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.operations.push(op);
    return op;
  }

  async completeBridge(operationId: string) {
    const op = this.operations.find(o => o.id === operationId);
    if (!op) throw new Error("Bridge operation not found");

    const accounts = dynastyTreasuryOS.listAccounts(op.tenantId);
    const main = accounts[0];
    if (!main) throw new Error("No treasury account for tenant");

    if (op.direction === "INBOUND") {
      main.bscBalance += op.bscAmount;
    } else {
      if (main.bscBalance < op.bscAmount) {
        op.status = "FAILED";
        return op;
      }
      main.bscBalance -= op.bscAmount;
    }

    op.status = "COMPLETED";
    return op;
  }

  listOperations(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.operations.filter(
      o => !tenantId || o.tenantId === tenantId
    );
  }
}

export const dynastyBSCBridgeOS = new DynastyBSCBridgeOS();
Dynasty Civilization BondOS
ts
// src/bonds/DynastyCivilizationBondOS.ts
import {
  missionService,
  impactService,
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  sovereignCreditTradingEngine,
  dynastyTreasuryOS
} from "../core";

export interface CivilizationBond {
  id: string;
  issuerTenantId: string;
  name: string;
  faceValueBSC: number;
  couponRate: number;
  maturityDate: string;
  backingCredits: string[]; // credit definition IDs
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface BondSubscription {
  id: string;
  bondId: string;
  subscriberTenantId: string;
  amountFaceValueBSC: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCivilizationBondOS {
  private bonds: CivilizationBond[] = [];
  private subscriptions: BondSubscription[] = [];

  createBond(
    issuerTenantId: string,
    name: string,
    faceValueBSC: number,
    couponRate: number,
    maturityDate: string,
    backingCredits: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(issuerTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "BONDS", "CREATE_BOND")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create bonds`);
    }

    const bond: CivilizationBond = {
      id: `BOND-${Date.now()}`,
      issuerTenantId,
      name,
      faceValueBSC,
      couponRate,
      maturityDate,
      backingCredits,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.bonds.push(bond);
    return bond;
  }

  async subscribeBond(
    bondId: string,
    subscriberTenantId: string,
    amountFaceValueBSC: number
  ) {
    const bond = this.bonds.find(b => b.id === bondId);
    if (!bond) throw new Error("Bond not found");

    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(subscriberTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "BONDS", "SUBSCRIBE_BOND")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot subscribe bonds`);
    }

    const accounts = dynastyTreasuryOS.listAccounts(subscriberTenantId);
    const main = accounts[0];
    if (!main || main.bscBalance < amountFaceValueBSC) {
      throw new Error("Insufficient BSC for subscription");
    }

    main.bscBalance -= amountFaceValueBSC;

    const issuerAccounts = dynastyTreasuryOS.listAccounts(
      bond.issuerTenantId
    );
    const issuerMain = issuerAccounts[0];
    if (!issuerMain) throw new Error("Issuer has no treasury account");
    issuerMain.bscBalance += amountFaceValueBSC;

    const sub: BondSubscription = {
      id: `BSUB-${Date.now()}`,
      bondId,
      subscriberTenantId,
      amountFaceValueBSC,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.subscriptions.push(sub);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      bond.issuerTenantId,
      "BOND_ISSUANCE",
      { bondId, amountFaceValueBSC }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(
      bond.issuerTenantId,
      "GLOBAL"
    );

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      bond.issuerTenantId,
      "BONDS",
      amountFaceValueBSC,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { sub, mission, impact };
  }

  listBonds() {
    return this.bonds;
  }

  listSubscriptions(bondId?: string) {
    return this.subscriptions.filter(
      s => !bondId || s.bondId === bondId
    );
  }
}

export const dynastyCivilizationBondOS = new DynastyCivilizationBondOS();
Dynasty Global LiquidityOS
ts
// src/liquidity/DynastyGlobalLiquidityOS.ts
import {
  trustBinderOS,
  identityOS,
  dynastyTreasuryOS
} from "../core";

export interface LiquidityPool {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  assetSymbol: string; // e.g. "BSC", "MODERNIZATION_CREDIT"
  totalBSC: number;
  totalUnits: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface LiquidityProvision {
  id: string;
  poolId: string;
  providerTenantId: string;
  bscAmount: number;
  unitsReceived: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGlobalLiquidityOS {
  private pools: LiquidityPool[] = [];
  private provisions: LiquidityProvision[] = [];

  createPool(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    assetSymbol: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LIQUIDITY", "CREATE_POOL")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create liquidity pools`);
    }

    const pool: LiquidityPool = {
      id: `LP-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      assetSymbol,
      totalBSC: 0,
      totalUnits: 0,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.pools.push(pool);
    return pool;
  }

  provideLiquidity(
    poolId: string,
    providerTenantId: string,
    bscAmount: number
  ) {
    const pool = this.pools.find(p => p.id === poolId);
    if (!pool) throw new Error("Pool not found");

    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(providerTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LIQUIDITY", "PROVIDE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot provide liquidity`);
    }

    const accounts = dynastyTreasuryOS.listAccounts(providerTenantId);
    const main = accounts[0];
    if (!main || main.bscBalance < bscAmount) {
      throw new Error("Insufficient BSC");
    }

    main.bscBalance -= bscAmount;

    const units =
      pool.totalBSC === 0 ? bscAmount : (bscAmount * pool.totalUnits) / pool.totalBSC;

    pool.totalBSC += bscAmount;
    pool.totalUnits += units;

    const prov: LiquidityProvision = {
      id: `LPROV-${Date.now()}`,
      poolId,
      providerTenantId,
      bscAmount,
      unitsReceived: units,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.provisions.push(prov);
    return prov;
  }

  listPools() {
    return this.pools;
  }

  listProvisions(poolId?: string) {
    return this.provisions.filter(
      p => !poolId || p.poolId === poolId
    );
  }
}

export const dynastyGlobalLiquidityOS = new DynastyGlobalLiquidityOS();
Binder rules seed (bridge + exchange + bonds + liquidity + registry)
ts
// src/bootstrap/ExternalEconomyBinderSeed.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedExternalEconomyBinderRules() {
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CREDIT_REGISTRY",
    action: "REGISTER_DEFINITION",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CREDIT_REGISTRY",
    action: "ISSUE_CREDIT",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CREDIT_REGISTRY",
    action: "VERIFY_ISSUANCE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AUDITOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "EXTERNAL_EXCHANGE",
    action: "CREATE_LISTING",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "BSC_BRIDGE",
    action: "REQUEST_BRIDGE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER", "TENANT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "BONDS",
    action: "CREATE_BOND",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "BONDS",
    action: "SUBSCRIBE_BOND",
    allowedRoles: ["TENANT", "CITY", "CORPORATION"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "LIQUIDITY",
    action: "CREATE_POOL",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "TREASURER"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "LIQUIDITY",
    action: "PROVIDE",
    allowedRoles: ["TENANT", "CITY", "CORPORATION"]
  });
}

Dynasty CitizenshipOS
ts
// src/citizenship/DynastyCitizenshipOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export type CitizenshipStatus = "APPLICANT" | "CITIZEN" | "REVOKED";

export interface CitizenshipRecord {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  status: CitizenshipStatus;
  cityId?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCitizenshipOS {
  private records: CitizenshipRecord[] = [];

  applyForCitizenship(tenantId: string, name: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZENSHIP", "APPLY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot apply for citizenship`);
    }

    const rec: CitizenshipRecord = {
      id: `CIT-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      status: "APPLICANT",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.records.push(rec);
    return rec;
  }

  async approveCitizenship(adminTenantId: string, recordId: string, cityId?: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(adminTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZENSHIP", "APPROVE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot approve citizenship`);
    }

    const rec = this.records.find(r => r.id === recordId);
    if (!rec) throw new Error("Record not found");

    rec.status = "CITIZEN";
    rec.cityId = cityId;

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      adminTenantId,
      "CITIZENSHIP_APPROVAL",
      { recordId, cityId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(adminTenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { rec, mission, impact };
  }

  listCitizens(status?: CitizenshipStatus) {
    return this.records.filter(r => !status || r.status === status);
  }
}

export const dynastyCitizenshipOS = new DynastyCitizenshipOS();
Dynasty ImmigrationOS
ts
// src/immigration/DynastyImmigrationOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export type ImmigrationStatus = "REQUESTED" | "APPROVED" | "DENIED";

export interface ImmigrationCase {
  id: string;
  applicantTenantId: string;
  originRegion: string;
  targetCityId: string;
  status: ImmigrationStatus;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyImmigrationOS {
  private cases: ImmigrationCase[] = [];

  requestImmigration(applicantTenantId: string, originRegion: string, targetCityId: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(applicantTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "IMMIGRATION", "REQUEST")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot request immigration`);
    }

    const c: ImmigrationCase = {
      id: `IMMI-${Date.now()}`,
      applicantTenantId,
      originRegion,
      targetCityId,
      status: "REQUESTED",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.cases.push(c);
    return c;
  }

  async decideImmigration(adminTenantId: string, caseId: string, approve: boolean) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(adminTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "IMMIGRATION", "DECIDE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot decide immigration`);
    }

    const c = this.cases.find(x => x.id === caseId);
    if (!c) throw new Error("Case not found");

    c.status = approve ? "APPROVED" : "DENIED";

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      adminTenantId,
      "IMMIGRATION_DECISION",
      { caseId, approve }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(adminTenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { c, mission, impact };
  }

  listCases(status?: ImmigrationStatus) {
    return this.cases.filter(c => !status || c.status === status);
  }
}

export const dynastyImmigrationOS = new DynastyImmigrationOS();
Dynasty CityOS
ts
// src/city/DynastyCityOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface DynastyCity {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  region: string;
  populationEstimate: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface CityUpgradePlan {
  id: string;
  cityId: string;
  description: string;
  expectedBSCSpend: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCityOS {
  private cities: DynastyCity[] = [];
  private upgrades: CityUpgradePlan[] = [];

  registerCity(adminTenantId: string, name: string, region: string, populationEstimate: number) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(adminTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITY", "REGISTER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register cities`);
    }

    const city: DynastyCity = {
      id: `CITY-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      region,
      populationEstimate,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.cities.push(city);
    return city;
  }

  async proposeUpgrade(tenantId: string, cityId: string, description: string, expectedBSCSpend: number) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITY", "PROPOSE_UPGRADE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot propose city upgrades`);
    }

    const up: CityUpgradePlan = {
      id: `CITY-UP-${Date.now()}`,
      cityId,
      description,
      expectedBSCSpend,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.upgrades.push(up);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "CITY_UPGRADE",
      { cityId, description, expectedBSCSpend }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { up, mission, impact };
  }

  listCities() {
    return this.cities;
  }

  listUpgrades(cityId?: string) {
    return this.upgrades.filter(u => !cityId || u.cityId === cityId);
  }
}

export const dynastyCityOS = new DynastyCityOS();

Dynasty Interstellar ExpansionOS
ts
// src/interstellar/DynastyInterstellarExpansionOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS, sovereignCreditTradingEngine } from "../core";

export type InterstellarRegion = "LOCAL_CLUSTER" | "DEEP_CLUSTER" | "EXOPLANETARY";

export interface InterstellarMission {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  region: InterstellarRegion;
  objective: string;
  expectedBSCSpend: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyInterstellarExpansionOS {
  private missions: InterstellarMission[] = [];

  async proposeInterstellarMission(tenantId: string, region: InterstellarRegion, objective: string, expectedBSCSpend: number) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "INTERSTELLAR", "PROPOSE_MISSION")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot propose interstellar missions`);
    }

    const m: InterstellarMission = {
      id: `IST-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      region,
      objective,
      expectedBSCSpend,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.missions.push(m);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "INTERSTELLAR_EXPANSION",
      { region, objective, expectedBSCSpend }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    await sovereignCreditTradingEngine.issueAndList(
      tenantId,
      "INTERSTELLAR",
      expectedBSCSpend,
      impact.modernizationScoreDelta
    );

    return { m, mission, impact };
  }

  listMissions() {
    return this.missions;
  }
}
