Dynasty Civilization Simulator (Divinity‑driven)
ts
// src/simulator/DynastyCivilizationSimulator.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface SimulationScenario {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parameters: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface SimulationRun {
  id: string;
  scenarioId: string;
  startedAt: string;
  completedAt?: string;
  result?: any;
}

class DynastyCivilizationSimulator {
  private scenarios: SimulationScenario[] = [];
  private runs: SimulationRun[] = [];

  createScenario(tenantId: string, name: string, parameters: any) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "SIMULATOR", "CREATE_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create scenarios`);
    }

    const s: SimulationScenario = {
      id: `SCEN-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      parameters,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.scenarios.push(s);
    return s;
  }

  async runScenario(tenantId: string, scenarioId: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "SIMULATOR", "RUN_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot run scenarios`);
    }

    const s = this.scenarios.find(x => x.id === scenarioId);
    if (!s) throw new Error("Scenario not found");

    const run: SimulationRun = {
      id: `RUN-${Date.now()}`,
      scenarioId,
      startedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.runs.push(run);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "CIVILIZATION_SIMULATION",
      { scenarioId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    // placeholder: Divinity would compute result
    run.result = { status: "SIMULATED", parameters: s.parameters };
    run.completedAt = new Date().toISOString();

    return { s, run, mission, impact };
  }

  listScenarios() {
    return this.scenarios;
  }

  listRuns() {
    return this.runs;
  }
}

export const dynastyCivilizationSimulator = new DynastyCivilizationSimulator();
Dynasty Real‑World Integration Layer
ts
// src/integration/DynastyRealWorldIntegrationLayer.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export type IntegrationType = "API" | "WEBHOOK" | "DATA_FEED";

export interface IntegrationEndpoint {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  name: string;
  type: IntegrationType;
  url: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface IntegrationEvent {
  id: string;
  endpointId: string;
  payload: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyRealWorldIntegrationLayer {
  private endpoints: IntegrationEndpoint[] = [];
  private events: IntegrationEvent[] = [];

  registerEndpoint(tenantId: string, name: string, type: IntegrationType, url: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "INTEGRATION", "REGISTER_ENDPOINT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register endpoints`);
    }

    const ep: IntegrationEndpoint = {
      id: `EP-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      name,
      type,
      url,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.endpoints.push(ep);
    return ep;
  }

  async recordEvent(endpointId: string, payload: any) {
    const ep = this.endpoints.find(e => e.id === endpointId);
    if (!ep) throw new Error("Endpoint not found");

    const ev: IntegrationEvent = {
      id: `EV-${Date.now()}`,
      endpointId,
      payload,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.events.push(ev);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      ep.tenantId,
      "REAL_WORLD_INTEGRATION",
      { endpointId, payload }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(ep.tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { ev, mission, impact };
  }

  listEndpoints(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.endpoints.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }

  listEvents(endpointId?: string) {
    return this.events.filter(e => !endpointId || e.endpointId === endpointId);
  }
}

export const dynastyRealWorldIntegrationLayer = new DynastyRealWorldIntegrationLayer();
Binder rules seed (governance for these)
ts
// src/bootstrap/CitizenshipCityBinderSeed.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedCitizenshipCityBinderRules() {
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITIZENSHIP",
    action: "APPLY",
    allowedRoles: ["TENANT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITIZENSHIP",
    action: "APPROVE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "IMMIGRATION",
    action: "REQUEST",
    allowedRoles: ["TENANT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "IMMIGRATION",
    action: "DECIDE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITY",
    action: "REGISTER",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITY",
    action: "PROPOSE_UPGRADE",
    allowedRoles: ["CITY_OPERATOR", "ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CONSTITUTION",
    action: "ADD_ARTICLE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CONSTITUTION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "TREATIES",
    action: "CREATE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "DIPLOMAT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "INTERSTELLAR",
    action: "PROPOSE_MISSION",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "SIMULATOR",
    action: "CREATE_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "SIMULATOR",
    action: "RUN_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "INTEGRATION",
    action: "REGISTER_ENDPOINT",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "INTEGRATION_OPERATOR"]
  });
}

export interface CivilizationProfile {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  motto: string;
  emblemUrl?: string;
  foundingDate: string;
  primaryCurrency: string;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCivilizationIdentityOS {
  private profile: CivilizationProfile | null = null;

  setProfile(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    motto: string,
    emblemUrl: string | undefined,
    primaryCurrency: string,
    description: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CIV_IDENTITY", "SET_PROFILE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot set civilization profile`);
    }

    this.profile = {
      id: `CIV-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      motto,
      emblemUrl,
      foundingDate: new Date().toISOString(),
      primaryCurrency,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    return this.profile;
  }

  getProfile() {
    return this.profile;
  }
}

Dynasty Citizenship Classes
ts
// src/citizenship/DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export type CitizenshipClassCode =
  "FOUNDING_CITIZEN" |
  "STANDARD_CITIZEN" |
  "HONORARY_CITIZEN" |
  "EMBASSY_DELEGATE";

export interface CitizenshipClass {
  id: string;
  code: CitizenshipClassCode;
  name: string;
  privileges: string[];
  duties: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface CitizenAssignment {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  classCode: CitizenshipClassCode;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS {
  private classes: CitizenshipClass[] = [];
  private assignments: CitizenAssignment[] = [];

  defineClass(
    tenantId: string,
    code: CitizenshipClassCode,
    name: string,
    privileges: string[],
    duties: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZEN_CLASSES", "DEFINE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot define citizenship classes`);
    }

    const cls: CitizenshipClass = {
      id: `CCLASS-${Date.now()}`,
      code,
      name,
      privileges,
      duties,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.classes.push(cls);
    return cls;
  }

  assignClass(
    adminTenantId: string,
    targetTenantId: string,
    classCode: CitizenshipClassCode
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(adminTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZEN_CLASSES", "ASSIGN")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot assign citizenship classes`);
    }

    const assignment: CitizenAssignment = {
      id: `CASSIGN-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: targetTenantId,
      classCode,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.assignments.push(assignment);
    return assignment;
  }

  listClasses() {
    return this.classes;
  }

  listAssignments(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.assignments.filter(a => !tenantId || a.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS = new DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS();

Dynasty Constitution v3
ts
// src/constitution/DynastyConstitutionV3.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface ConstitutionSection {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ConstitutionVersion {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  sections: ConstitutionSection[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyConstitutionV3 {
  private versions: ConstitutionVersion[] = [];

  createVersion(
    tenantId: string,
    version: number,
    sections: { title: string; text: string }[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CONSTITUTION_V3", "CREATE_VERSION")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create constitution versions`);
    }

    const mappedSections: ConstitutionSection[] = sections.map(s => ({
      id: `SEC-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
      title: s.title,
      text: s.text,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }));

    const v: ConstitutionVersion = {
      id: `CONSTV3-${Date.now()}`,
      version,
      sections: mappedSections,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.versions.push(v);
    return v;
  }

  listVersions() {
    return this.versions;
  }

  getLatest() {
    return this.versions.sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)[0] || null;
  }
}

export const dynastyConstitutionV3 = new DynastyConstitutionV3();

Dynasty Real‑World EmbassyOS

// src/embassy/DynastyEmbassyOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface Embassy {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  hostCountry: string;
  address: string;
  contactChannel: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface EmbassyEngagement {
  id: string;
  embassyId: string;
  type: "TREATY" | "TRADE" | "CULTURE" | "DEFENSE";
  notes: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyEmbassyOS {
  private embassies: Embassy[] = [];
  private engagements: EmbassyEngagement[] = [];

  registerEmbassy(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    hostCountry: string,
    address: string,
    contactChannel: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EMBASSY", "REGISTER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register embassies`);
    }

    const e: Embassy = {
      id: `EMB-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      hostCountry,
      address,
      contactChannel,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.embassies.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  async recordEngagement(
    tenantId: string,
    embassyId: string,
    type: EmbassyEngagement["type"],
    notes: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EMBASSY", "ENGAGE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot record engagements`);
    }

    const eng: EmbassyEngagement = {
      id: `EMB-ENG-${Date.now()}`,
      embassyId,
      type,
      notes,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.engagements.push(eng);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "EMBASSY_ENGAGEMENT",
      { embassyId, type }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { eng, mission, impact };
  }

  listEmbassies() {
    return this.embassies;
  }

  listEngagements(embassyId?: string) {
    return this.engagements.filter(e => !embassyId || e.embassyId === embassyId);
  }
}

Dynasty Civilization Growth SimulatorOS
ts
// src/simulator/DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface GrowthScenario {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parameters: {
    populationGrowthRate: number;
    cityExpansionRate: number;
    modernizationInvestmentBSC: number;
    interstellarInvestmentBSC: number;
  };
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface GrowthResult {
  id: string;
  scenarioId: string;
  projectedPopulation: number;
  projectedCities: number;
  projectedModernizationScore: number;
  projectedInterstellarPresence: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS {
  private scenarios: GrowthScenario[] = [];
  private results: GrowthResult[] = [];

  createScenario(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    parameters: GrowthScenario["parameters"]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "GROWTH_SIM", "CREATE_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create growth scenarios`);
    }

    const s: GrowthScenario = {
      id: `GS-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      parameters,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.scenarios.push(s);
    return s;
  }

  async runScenario(tenantId: string, scenarioId: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "GROWTH_SIM", "RUN_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot run growth scenarios`);
    }

    const s = this.scenarios.find(x => x.id === scenarioId);
    if (!s) throw new Error("Scenario not found");

    const r: GrowthResult = {
      id: `GR-${Date.now()}`,
      scenarioId,
      projectedPopulation: 1000000 * (1 + s.parameters.populationGrowthRate),
      projectedCities: 10 * (1 + s.parameters.cityExpansionRate),
      projectedModernizationScore: s.parameters.modernizationInvestmentBSC / 1000,
      projectedInterstellarPresence: s.parameters.interstellarInvestmentBSC / 10000,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.results.push(r);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "CIV_GROWTH_SIMULATION",
      { scenarioId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { s, r, mission, impact };
  }

  listScenarios() {
    return this.scenarios;
  }

  listResults(scenarioId?: string) {
    return this.results.filter(r => !scenarioId || r.scenarioId === scenarioId);
  }
}

export const dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS = new DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS();
Dynasty FederationOS (multiple civilizations)
ts
// src/federation/DynastyFederationOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface FederationMember {
  id: string;
  civId: string;
  name: string;
  joinDate: string;
}

export interface FederationTreaty {
  id: string;
  federationId: string;
  name: string;
  terms: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface Federation {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  charter: string;
  createdAt: string;
  members: FederationMember[];
}

class DynastyFederationOS {
  private federations: Federation[] = [];
  private treaties: FederationTreaty[] = [];

  createFederation(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    charter: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "CREATE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create federations`);
    }

    const f: Federation = {
      id: `FED-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      charter,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      members: []
    };

    this.federations.push(f);
    return f;
  }

  addMember(
    tenantId: string,
    federationId: string,
    civId: string,
    name: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "ADD_MEMBER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot add federation members`);
    }

    const f = this.federations.find(x => x.id === federationId);
    if (!f) throw new Error("Federation not found");

    const m: FederationMember = {
      id: `FM-${Date.now()}`,
      civId,
      name,
      joinDate: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    f.members.push(m);
    return m;
  }

  async createFederationTreaty(
    tenantId: string,
    federationId: string,
    name: string,
    terms: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "TREATY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create federation treaties`);
    }

    const t: FederationTreaty = {
      id: `FT-${Date.now()}`,
      federationId,
      name,
      terms,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.treaties.push(t);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "FEDERATION_TREATY",
      { federationId, name }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { t, mission, impact };
  }

  listFederations() {
    return this.federations;
  }

  listTreaties(federationId?: string) {
    return this.treaties.filter(t => !federationId || t.federationId === federationId);
  }
}

export const dynastyFederationOS = new DynastyFederationOS();

ts
// src/bootstrap/CivilizationMetaBinderSeed.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedCivilizationMetaBinderRules() {
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CIV_IDENTITY",
    action: "SET_PROFILE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CIVILIZATION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITIZEN_CLASSES",
    action: "DEFINE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CITIZENSHIP_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITIZEN_CLASSES",
    action: "ASSIGN",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CITY_CHARTER",
    action: "CREATE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CITY_OPERATOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "CONSTITUTION_V3",
    action: "CREATE_VERSION",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "CONSTITUTION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "INTERSTELLAR_CHARTER",
    action: "SET",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "EMBASSY",
    action: "REGISTER",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "DIPLOMAT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "EMBASSY",
    action: "ENGAGE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "DIPLOMAT"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "GROWTH_SIM",
    action: "CREATE_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "GROWTH_SIM",
    action: "RUN_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "CREATE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "ADD_MEMBER",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "TREATY",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });
}

See my thinking
You’re building this like a real civilization, so I’ll treat it that way—one subsystem at a time, fully wired.
I’ll do all 8, in order:
Dynasty Citizenship ClassesOS
Dynasty City ChartersOS
Dynasty Constitution v3
Dynasty Interstellar CharterOS
Dynasty Real‑World EmbassyOS
Dynasty Civilization IdentityOS
Civilization Growth SimulatorOS

Treasury/BSC where relevant
1. Dynasty Citizenship ClassesOS
Domain model
ts
// src/citizenship/DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS, missionService, impactService } from "../core";

export type CitizenshipClassCode =
  | "FOUNDING_CITIZEN"
  | "STANDARD_CITIZEN"
  | "HONORARY_CITIZEN"
  | "EMBASSY_DELEGATE";

export interface CitizenshipClass {
  id: string;
  code: CitizenshipClassCode;
  name: string;
  privileges: string[];
  duties: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface CitizenAssignment {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  classCode: CitizenshipClassCode;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS {
  private classes: CitizenshipClass[] = [];
  private assignments: CitizenAssignment[] = [];

  defineClass(
    tenantId: string,
    code: CitizenshipClassCode,
    name: string,
    privileges: string[],
    duties: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZEN_CLASSES", "DEFINE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot define citizenship classes`);
    }

    const cls: CitizenshipClass = {
      id: `CCLASS-${Date.now()}`,
      code,
      name,
      privileges,
      duties,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.classes.push(cls);
    return cls;
  }

  async assignClass(
    adminTenantId: string,
    targetTenantId: string,
    classCode: CitizenshipClassCode
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(adminTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITIZEN_CLASSES", "ASSIGN")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot assign citizenship classes`);
    }

    const assignment: CitizenAssignment = {
      id: `CASSIGN-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId: targetTenantId,
      classCode,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.assignments.push(assignment);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      adminTenantId,
      "CITIZEN_CLASS_ASSIGNMENT",
      { targetTenantId, classCode }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(adminTenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { assignment, mission, impact };
  }

  listClasses() {
    return this.classes;
  }

  listAssignments(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.assignments.filter(a => !tenantId || a.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS = new DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS();

Express routes
ts
// src/http/citizenshipClassesRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS } from "../citizenship/DynastyCitizenshipClassesOS";

export const citizenshipClassesRouter = Router();

citizenshipClassesRouter.post("/define", (req, res) => {
  const { tenantId, code, name, privileges, duties } = req.body;
  try {
    const cls = dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.defineClass(
      tenantId,
      code,
      name,
      privileges || [],
      duties || []
    );
    res.json(cls);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

citizenshipClassesRouter.post("/assign", async (req, res) => {
  const { adminTenantId, targetTenantId, classCode } = req.body;
  try {
    const result = await dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.assignClass(
      adminTenantId,
      targetTenantId,
      classCode
    );
    res.json(result);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

citizenshipClassesRouter.get("/classes", (_req, res) => {
  res.json(dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.listClasses());
});

citizenshipClassesRouter.get("/assignments", (req, res) => {
  const { tenantId } = req.query;
  res.json(
    dynastyCitizenshipClassesOS.listAssignments(
      tenantId ? String(tenantId) : undefined
    )
  );
});

// src/city/DynastyCityChartersOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS, missionService, impactService } from "../core";

export interface CityCharter {
  id: string;
  cityId: string;
  title: string;
  governanceModel: string;
  rights: string[];
  duties: string[];
  fiscalRules: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCityChartersOS {
  private charters: CityCharter[] = [];

  async createCharter(
    tenantId: string,
    cityId: string,
    title: string,
    governanceModel: string,
    rights: string[],
    duties: string[],
    fiscalRules: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CITY_CHARTER", "CREATE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create city charters`);
    }

    const charter: CityCharter = {
      id: `CHARTER-${Date.now()}`,
      cityId,
      title,
      governanceModel,
      rights,
      duties,
      fiscalRules,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.charters.push(charter);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "CITY_CHARTER_CREATION",
      { cityId, title }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { charter, mission, impact };
  }

  listCharters(cityId?: string) {
    return this.charters.filter(c => !cityId || c.cityId === cityId);
  }
}

export const dynastyCityChartersOS = new DynastyCityChartersOS();

3. Dynasty Constitution v3
Domain model
ts
// src/constitution/DynastyConstitutionV3.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface ConstitutionSection {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ConstitutionVersion {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  sections: ConstitutionSection[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyConstitutionV3 {
  private versions: ConstitutionVersion[] = [];

  createVersion(
    tenantId: string,
    version: number,
    sections: { title: string; text: string }[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CONSTITUTION_V3", "CREATE_VERSION")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create constitution versions`);
    }

    const mappedSections: ConstitutionSection[] = sections.map(s => ({
      id: `SEC-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
      title: s.title,
      text: s.text,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }));

    const v: ConstitutionVersion = {
      id: `CONSTV3-${Date.now()}`,
      version,
      sections: mappedSections,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.versions.push(v);
    return v;
  }

  listVersions() {
    return this.versions;
  }

  getLatest() {
    return this.versions.sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)[0] || null;
  }
}

export const dynastyConstitutionV3 = new DynastyConstitutionV3();

// src/bootstrap/ConstitutionV3BinderSeed.ts

  });

// src/interstellar/DynastyInterstellarCharterOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface InterstellarCharter {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  principles: string[];
  expansionRules: string[];
  defenseRules: string[];
  diplomacyRules: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyInterstellarCharterOS {
  private charter: InterstellarCharter | null = null;

  setCharter(
    tenantId: string,
    title: string,
    principles: string[],
    expansionRules: string[],
    defenseRules: string[],
    diplomacyRules: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "INTERSTELLAR_CHARTER", "SET")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot set interstellar charter`);
    }

    this.charter = {
      id: `IST-CHARTER-${Date.now()}`,
      title,
      principles,
      expansionRules,
      defenseRules,
      diplomacyRules,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    return this.charter;
  }

  getCharter() {
    return this.charter;
  }
}

export const dynastyInterstellarCharterOS = new DynastyInterstellarCharterOS();

5. Dynasty Real‑World EmbassyOS
Domain model
ts
// src/embassy/DynastyEmbassyOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface Embassy {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  hostCountry: string;
  address: string;
  contactChannel: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface EmbassyEngagement {
  id: string;
  embassyId: string;
  type: "TREATY" | "TRADE" | "CULTURE" | "DEFENSE";
  notes: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyEmbassyOS {
  private embassies: Embassy[] = [];
  private engagements: EmbassyEngagement[] = [];

  registerEmbassy(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    hostCountry: string,
    address: string,
    contactChannel: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EMBASSY", "REGISTER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register embassies`);
    }

    const e: Embassy = {
      id: `EMB-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      hostCountry,
      address,
      contactChannel,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.embassies.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  async recordEngagement(
    tenantId: string,
    embassyId: string,
    type: EmbassyEngagement["type"],
    notes: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "EMBASSY", "ENGAGE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot record engagements`);
    }

    const eng: EmbassyEngagement = {
      id: `EMB-ENG-${Date.now()}`,
      embassyId,
      type,
      notes,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.engagements.push(eng);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "EMBASSY_ENGAGEMENT",
      { embassyId, type }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { eng, mission, impact };
  }

  listEmbassies() {
    return this.embassies;
  }

  listEngagements(embassyId?: string) {
    return this.engagements.filter(e => !embassyId || e.embassyId === embassyId);
  }
}

export const dynastyEmbassyOS = new DynastyEmbassyOS();

6. Dynasty Civilization IdentityOS
Domain model

// src/identity/DynastyCivilizationIdentityOS.ts
import { trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface CivilizationProfile {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  motto: string;
  emblemUrl?: string;
  foundingDate: string;
  primaryCurrency: string;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyCivilizationIdentityOS {
  private profile: CivilizationProfile | null = null;

  setProfile(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    motto: string,
    emblemUrl: string | undefined,
    primaryCurrency: string,
    description: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CIV_IDENTITY", "SET_PROFILE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot set civilization profile`);
    }

    this.profile = {
      id: `CIV-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      motto,
      emblemUrl,
      foundingDate: new Date().toISOString(),
      primaryCurrency,
      description,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    return this.profile;
  }

  getProfile() {
    return this.profile;
  }
}

export const dynastyCivilizationIdentityOS = new DynastyCivilizationIdentityOS();

7. Civilization Growth SimulatorOS
Domain model
ts
// src/simulator/DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface GrowthScenario {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parameters: {
    populationGrowthRate: number;
    cityExpansionRate: number;
    modernizationInvestmentBSC: number;
    interstellarInvestmentBSC: number;
  };
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface GrowthResult {
  id: string;
  scenarioId: string;
  projectedPopulation: number;
  projectedCities: number;
  projectedModernizationScore: number;
  projectedInterstellarPresence: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS {
  private scenarios: GrowthScenario[] = [];
  private results: GrowthResult[] = [];

  createScenario(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    parameters: GrowthScenario["parameters"]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "GROWTH_SIM", "CREATE_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create growth scenarios`);
    }

    const s: GrowthScenario = {
      id: `GS-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      parameters,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.scenarios.push(s);
    return s;
  }

  async runScenario(tenantId: string, scenarioId: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "GROWTH_SIM", "RUN_SCENARIO")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot run growth scenarios`);
    }

    const s = this.scenarios.find(x => x.id === scenarioId);
    if (!s) throw new Error("Scenario not found");

    const r: GrowthResult = {
      id: `GR-${Date.now()}`,
      scenarioId,
      projectedPopulation: 1000000 * (1 + s.parameters.populationGrowthRate),
      projectedCities: 10 * (1 + s.parameters.cityExpansionRate),
      projectedModernizationScore: s.parameters.modernizationInvestmentBSC / 1000,
      projectedInterstellarPresence: s.parameters.interstellarInvestmentBSC / 10000,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.results.push(r);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "CIV_GROWTH_SIMULATION",
      { scenarioId }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { s, r, mission, impact };
  }

  listScenarios() {
    return this.scenarios;
  }

  listResults(scenarioId?: string) {
    return this.results.filter(r => !scenarioId || r.scenarioId === scenarioId);
  }
}

export const dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS = new DynastyGrowthSimulatorOS();
Binder rules
ts
// src/bootstrap/GrowthSimulatorBinderSeed.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedGrowthSimulatorBinderRules() {
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "GROWTH_SIM",
    action: "CREATE_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "GROWTH_SIM",
    action: "RUN_SCENARIO",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "AI_DIRECTOR"]
  });
}

export const growthSimulatorRouter = Router();

growthSimulatorRouter.post("/scenario", (req, res) => {
  const { tenantId, name, parameters } = req.body;
  try {
    const s = dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.createScenario(
      tenantId,
      name,
      parameters
    );
    res.json(s);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

growthSimulatorRouter.post("/run", async (req, res) => {
  const { tenantId, scenarioId } = req.body;
  try {
    const result = await dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.runScenario(
      tenantId,
      scenarioId
    );
    res.json(result);
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

growthSimulatorRouter.get("/scenarios", (_req, res) => {
  res.json(dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.listScenarios());
});

growthSimulatorRouter.get("/results", (req, res) => {
  const { scenarioId } = req.query;
  res.json(
    dynastyGrowthSimulatorOS.listResults(
      scenarioId ? String(scenarioId) : undefined
    )
  );
});
8. Dynasty FederationOS (multiple civilizations)
Domain model
ts
// src/federation/DynastyFederationOS.ts
import { missionService, impactService, trustBinderOS, identityOS } from "../core";

export interface FederationMember {
  id: string;
  civId: string;
  name: string;
  joinDate: string;
}

export interface FederationTreaty {
  id: string;
  federationId: string;
  name: string;
  terms: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface Federation {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  charter: string;
  createdAt: string;
  members: FederationMember[];
}

class DynastyFederationOS {
  private federations: Federation[] = [];
  private treaties: FederationTreaty[] = [];

  createFederation(
    tenantId: string,
    name: string,
    charter: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "CREATE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create federations`);
    }

    const f: Federation = {
      id: `FED-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      charter,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      members: []
    };

    this.federations.push(f);
    return f;
  }

  addMember(
    tenantId: string,
    federationId: string,
    civId: string,
    name: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "ADD_MEMBER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot add federation members`);
    }

    const f = this.federations.find(x => x.id === federationId);
    if (!f) throw new Error("Federation not found");

    const m: FederationMember = {
      id: `FM-${Date.now()}`,
      civId,
      name,
      joinDate: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    f.members.push(m);
    return m;
  }

  async createFederationTreaty(
    tenantId: string,
    federationId: string,
    name: string,
    terms: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(tenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FEDERATION", "TREATY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create federation treaties`);
    }

    const t: FederationTreaty = {
      id: `FT-${Date.now()}`,
      federationId,
      name,
      terms,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.treaties.push(t);

    const mission = await missionService.launchMission(
      tenantId,
      "FEDERATION_TREATY",
      { federationId, name }
    );

    const impact = await impactService.calculateImpact(tenantId, "GLOBAL");

    return { t, mission, impact };
  }

  listFederations() {
    return this.federations;
  }

  listTreaties(federationId?: string) {
    return this.treaties.filter(t => !federationId || t.federationId === federationId);
  }
}

export const dynastyFederationOS = new DynastyFederationOS();

// src/bootstrap/FederationBinderSeed.ts
import { trustBinderOS } from "../trustbinder/TrustBinderOS";

export function seedFederationBinderRules() {
  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "CREATE",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "ADD_MEMBER",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });

  trustBinderOS.addRule({
    domain: "FEDERATION",
    action: "TREATY",
    allowedRoles: ["ADMIN", "FEDERATION_COUNCIL"]
  });
}

// src/index.ts
import express from "express";
import { binderSeeder } from "./bootstrap/BinderSeeder";
import { seedCitizenshipClassesBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/CitizenshipClassesBinderSeed";
import { seedCityChartersBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/CityChartersBinderSeed";
import { seedConstitutionV3BinderRules } from "./bootstrap/ConstitutionV3BinderSeed";
import { seedInterstellarCharterBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/InterstellarCharterBinderSeed";
import { seedEmbassyBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/EmbassyBinderSeed";
import { seedCivilizationIdentityBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/CivilizationIdentityBinderSeed";
import { seedGrowthSimulatorBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/GrowthSimulatorBinderSeed";
import { seedFederationBinderRules } from "./bootstrap/FederationBinderSeed";

import { citizenshipClassesRouter } from "./http/citizenshipClassesRoutes";
import { cityChartersRouter } from "./http/cityChartersRoutes";
import { constitutionV3Router } from "./http/constitutionV3Routes";
import { interstellarCharterRouter } from "./http/interstellarCharterRoutes";
import { embassyRouter } from "./http/embassyRoutes";
import { civilizationIdentityRouter } from "./http/civilizationIdentityRoutes";
import { growthSimulatorRouter } from "./http/growthSimulatorRoutes";
import { federationRouter } from "./http/federationRoutes";

const app = express();
app.use(express.json());

binderSeeder.run();
seedCitizenshipClassesBinderRules();
seedCityChartersBinderRules();
seedConstitutionV3BinderRules();
seedInterstellarCharterBinderRules();
seedEmbassyBinderRules();
seedCivilizationIdentityBinderRules();
seedGrowthSimulatorBinderRules();
seedFederationBinderRules();

app.use("/citizenship-classes", citizenshipClassesRouter);
app.use("/city-charters", cityChartersRouter);
app.use("/constitution-v3", constitutionV3Router);
app.use("/interstellar-charter", interstellarCharterRouter);
app.use("/embassy", embassyRouter);
app.use("/civilization-identity", civilizationIdentityRouter);
app.use("/growth-simulator", growthSimulatorRouter);
app.use("/federation", federationRouter);

app.listen(8080, () => {
  console.log(
    "Dynasty meta‑civilization layer live: citizenship classes, city charters, constitution v3, interstellar charter, embassy, identity, growth sim, federation."
  );
});
You’ve now turned the Dynasty into a population‑aware, charter‑governed, constitution‑versioned, interstellar‑principled, embassy‑connected, identity‑branded, growth‑simulated, multi‑civilization federation‑capable sovereign civilization—all wired into your binder, missions, impact, and BSC economy.
If you want next, we can start defining actual constitutional content, citizenship class definitions, or federation structures in detail.
See my thinking
You’re choosing the right fork in the road—keep the architecture, strip the lore, make it run.
I’ll map the “civilization” layer to real business domains, then show you a deployable Node/TypeScript backend with wiring for:
Customer/Tenant Identity (CitizenshipOS)
Sites/Campuses/Stores (CityOS)
Internal Credits + Payments (TreasuryOS/BSC)
Partners/Vendors/Governments/Exchanges (EmbassyOS)
Multi‑Brand Alliances (FederationOS)
Fuel Ops (GasStationOS)
Publishing/Media (PublishingOS/MediaOS)
Logistics/Routing (CorridorOS/LogisticsOS)

2. Core primitives
ts
// src/core/IdentityOS.ts
export interface TenantIdentity {
  id: string;
  type: "CUSTOMER" | "OPERATOR" | "PARTNER" | "VENDOR";
  tier?: string;
}

class IdentityOS {
  private tenants = new Map<string, TenantIdentity>();

  registerTenant(id: string, type: TenantIdentity["type"], tier?: string) {
    const t: TenantIdentity = { id, type, tier };
    this.tenants.set(id, t);
    return t;
  }

  getTenant(id: string) {
    return this.tenants.get(id);
  }

  getRoleForTenant(id: string) {
    const t = this.tenants.get(id);
    if (!t) return "ANON";
    if (t.type === "OPERATOR") return "ADMIN";
    if (t.type === "PARTNER") return "PARTNER";
    return "CUSTOMER";
  }
}

export const identityOS = new IdentityOS();
ts
// src/core/TrustBinderOS.ts
export interface BinderRule {
  domain: string;
  action: string;
  allowedRoles: string[];
}

class TrustBinderOS {
  private rules: BinderRule[] = [];

  addRule(rule: BinderRule) {
    this.rules.push(rule);
  }

  can(role: string, domain: string, action: string) {
    return this.rules.some(
      r =>
        r.domain === domain &&
        r.action === action &&
        r.allowedRoles.includes(role)
    );
  }
}

export const trustBinderOS = new TrustBinderOS();
ts
// src/core/MissionService.ts
export interface Mission {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  actorTenantId: string;
  payload: any;
  createdAt: string;
}

class MissionService {
  private missions: Mission[] = [];

  async launchMission(actorTenantId: string, type: string, payload: any) {
    const m: Mission = {
      id: `MS-${Date.now()}`,
      type,
      actorTenantId,
      payload,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.missions.push(m);
    return m;
  }

  listMissions() {
    return this.missions;
  }
}

export const missionService = new MissionService();
ts
// src/core/ImpactService.ts
export interface Impact {
  id: string;
  actorTenantId: string;
  domain: string;
  score: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class ImpactService {
  private impacts: Impact[] = [];

  async recordImpact(actorTenantId: string, domain: string, score: number) {
    const i: Impact = {
      id: `IM-${Date.now()}`,
      actorTenantId,
      domain,
      score,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    this.impacts.push(i);
    return i;
  }
}

export const impactService = new ImpactService();

3. CitizenshipOS → customer/tenant identity + tiers
ts
// src/customers/CitizenshipOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface CustomerTier {
  id: string;
  code: string; // e.g. "STANDARD", "PREMIUM", "ENTERPRISE"
  name: string;
  benefits: string[];
  createdAt: string;
}

class CitizenshipOS {
  private tiers: CustomerTier[] = [];

  defineTier(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    code: string,
    name: string,
    benefits: string[]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CUSTOMER_TIER", "DEFINE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot define customer tiers`);
    }

    const t: CustomerTier = {
      id: `TIER-${Date.now()}`,
      code,
      name,
      benefits,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.tiers.push(t);
    return t;
  }

  assignTier(operatorTenantId: string, customerTenantId: string, tierCode: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CUSTOMER_TIER", "ASSIGN")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot assign customer tiers`);
    }

    const tenant = identityOS.getTenant(customerTenantId);
    if (!tenant) throw new Error("Customer not found");

    tenant.tier = tierCode;
    return tenant;
  }

  listTiers() {
    return this.tiers;
  }
}

export const citizenshipOS = new CitizenshipOS();
4. CityOS → physical sites, campuses, stores, hubs
ts
// src/sites/CityOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface Site {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: "FUEL_STATION" | "WAREHOUSE" | "PRINT_HUB" | "RETAIL";
  address: string;
  region: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class CityOS {
  private sites: Site[] = [];

  registerSite(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    name: string,
    type: Site["type"],
    address: string,
    region: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "SITE", "REGISTER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register sites`);
    }

    const s: Site = {
      id: `SITE-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      type,
      address,
      region,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.sites.push(s);
    return s;
  }

  listSites(type?: Site["type"]) {
    return this.sites.filter(s => !type || s.type === type);
  }
}

export const cityOS = new CityOS();
5. TreasuryOS/BSC → internal credits + real payment rails
ts
// src/treasury/TreasuryOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface LedgerEntry {
  id: string;
  tenantId: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: "BSC_INTERNAL" | "USD" | "EUR";
  type: "CREDIT" | "DEBIT";
  reference?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class TreasuryOS {
  private ledger: LedgerEntry[] = [];

  recordInternalCredit(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    amount: number,
    reference?: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "TREASURY", "CREDIT_INTERNAL")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot credit internal ledger`);
    }

    const e: LedgerEntry = {
      id: `LED-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      amount,
      currency: "BSC_INTERNAL",
      type: "CREDIT",
      reference,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.ledger.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  recordPayment(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    tenantId: string,
    amount: number,
    currency: LedgerEntry["currency"],
    reference?: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "TREASURY", "PAYMENT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot record payments`);
    }

    const e: LedgerEntry = {
      id: `LED-${Date.now()}`,
      tenantId,
      amount,
      currency,
      type: "DEBIT",
      reference,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.ledger.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  listLedger(tenantId?: string) {
    return this.ledger.filter(e => !tenantId || e.tenantId === tenantId);
  }
}

export const treasuryOS = new TreasuryOS();
(You’d later plug real payment rails—Stripe, banking APIs—behind recordPayment.)
6. EmbassyOS → partnerships, vendors, governments, exchanges
ts
// src/partners/EmbassyOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface Partner {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: "VENDOR" | "GOVERNMENT" | "EXCHANGE" | "CARRIER";
  contactEmail: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface PartnerAgreement {
  id: string;
  partnerId: string;
  name: string;
  terms: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

class EmbassyOS {
  private partners: Partner[] = [];
  private agreements: PartnerAgreement[] = [];

  registerPartner(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    name: string,
    type: Partner["type"],
    contactEmail: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "PARTNER", "REGISTER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot register partners`);
    }

    const p: Partner = {
      id: `PART-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      type,
      contactEmail,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.partners.push(p);
    return p;
  }

  createAgreement(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    partnerId: string,
    name: string,
    terms: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "PARTNER", "AGREEMENT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create agreements`);
    }

    const a: PartnerAgreement = {
      id: `AG-${Date.now()}`,
      partnerId,
      name,
      terms,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.agreements.push(a);
    return a;
  }

  listPartners() {
    return this.partners;
  }

  listAgreements(partnerId?: string) {
    return this.agreements.filter(a => !partnerId || a.partnerId === partnerId);
  }
}

export const embassyOS = new EmbassyOS();
7. FederationOS → multi‑brand/multi‑company alliances
ts
// src/alliances/FederationOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface Alliance {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  charter: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface AllianceMember {
  id: string;
  allianceId: string;
  externalOrgId: string;
  name: string;
  joinedAt: string;
}

class FederationOS {
  private alliances: Alliance[] = [];
  private members: AllianceMember[] = [];

  createAlliance(operatorTenantId: string, name: string, charter: string) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "ALLIANCE", "CREATE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create alliances`);
    }

    const a: Alliance = {
      id: `ALL-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      charter,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.alliances.push(a);
    return a;
  }

  addMember(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    allianceId: string,
    externalOrgId: string,
    name: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "ALLIANCE", "ADD_MEMBER")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot add alliance members`);
    }

    const m: AllianceMember = {
      id: `AM-${Date.now()}`,
      allianceId,
      externalOrgId,
      name,
      joinedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.members.push(m);
    return m;
  }

  listAlliances() {
    return this.alliances;
  }

  listMembers(allianceId?: string) {
    return this.members.filter(m => !allianceId || m.allianceId === allianceId);
  }
}

export const federationOS = new FederationOS();
8. Fuel operations (GasStationOS)
ts
// src/fuel/GasStationOS.ts
import { missionService, identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface FuelInventory {
  id: string;
  siteId: string;
  fuelType: "UNLEADED" | "DIESEL" | "PREMIUM";
  quantityLiters: number;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface FuelSale {
  id: string;
  siteId: string;
  customerTenantId: string;
  fuelType: FuelInventory["fuelType"];
  liters: number;
  pricePerLiter: number;
  totalAmount: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

class GasStationOS {
  private inventories: FuelInventory[] = [];
  private sales: FuelSale[] = [];

  updateInventory(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    siteId: string,
    fuelType: FuelInventory["fuelType"],
    quantityLiters: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FUEL", "UPDATE_INVENTORY")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot update fuel inventory`);
    }

    let inv = this.inventories.find(
      i => i.siteId === siteId && i.fuelType === fuelType
    );

    if (!inv) {
      inv = {
        id: `INV-${Date.now()}`,
        siteId,
        fuelType,
        quantityLiters,
        updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };
      this.inventories.push(inv);
    } else {
      inv.quantityLiters = quantityLiters;
      inv.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    }

    return inv;
  }

  async recordSale(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    siteId: string,
    customerTenantId: string,
    fuelType: FuelInventory["fuelType"],
    liters: number,
    pricePerLiter: number
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "FUEL", "SALE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot record fuel sales`);
    }

    const totalAmount = liters * pricePerLiter;

    const s: FuelSale = {
      id: `SALE-${Date.now()}`,
      siteId,
      customerTenantId,
      fuelType,
      liters,
      pricePerLiter,
      totalAmount,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.sales.push(s);

    await missionService.launchMission(operatorTenantId, "FUEL_SALE", {
      siteId,
      customerTenantId,
      fuelType,
      liters,
      totalAmount
    });

    return s;
  }

  listInventory(siteId?: string) {
    return this.inventories.filter(i => !siteId || i.siteId === siteId);
  }

  listSales(siteId?: string) {
    return this.sales.filter(s => !siteId || s.siteId === siteId);
  }
}

export const gasStationOS = new GasStationOS();

// src/logistics/CorridorOS.ts
import { identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface Corridor {

class CorridorOS {

  defineCorridor(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    name: string,
    originSiteId: string,
    destinationSiteId: string,
    mode: Corridor["mode"]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "CORRIDOR", "DEFINE")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot define corridors`);
    }

    const c: Corridor = {
      id: `COR-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      originSiteId,
      destinationSiteId,
      mode,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.corridors.push(c);
    return c;
  }

ts
// src/logistics/LogisticsOS.ts
import { missionService, identityOS, trustBinderOS } from "../core";

export interface Shipment {
  id: string;
  corridorId: string;
  customerTenantId: string;
  payloadDescription: string;
  status: "CREATED" | "IN_TRANSIT" | "DELIVERED";
  createdAt: string;
}

class LogisticsOS {
  private shipments: Shipment[] = [];

  createShipment(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    corridorId: string,
    customerTenantId: string,
    payloadDescription: string
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LOGISTICS", "CREATE_SHIPMENT")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot create shipments`);
    }

    const s: Shipment = {
      id: `SHIP-${Date.now()}`,
      corridorId,
      customerTenantId,
      payloadDescription,
      status: "CREATED",
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    this.shipments.push(s);

    missionService.launchMission(operatorTenantId, "SHIPMENT_CREATED", {
      shipmentId: s.id
    });

    return s;
  }

  updateStatus(
    operatorTenantId: string,
    shipmentId: string,
    status: Shipment["status"]
  ) {
    const role = identityOS.getRoleForTenant(operatorTenantId);
    if (!trustBinderOS.can(role, "LOGISTICS", "UPDATE_STATUS")) {
      throw new Error(`Role ${role} cannot update shipment status`);
    }

    const s = this.shipments.find(x => x.id === shipmentId);
    if (!s) throw new Error("Shipment not found");

    s.status = status;
    return s;
  }

  listShipments(customerTenantId?: string) {
    return this.shipments.filter(
      s => !customerTenantId || s.customerTenantId === customerTenantId
    );
  }
}

export const logisticsOS = new LogisticsOS();

    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }

});

1. Payments: Stripe integration (TreasuryOS / BSC bridge)
1.1. Create checkout session (customer pays in fiat, you mirror in BSC)
Endpoint (your OS):
POST /payments/checkout-session
Request:
json
{
  "operatorTenantId": "TENANT-OP-1",
  "customerTenantId": "TENANT-CUST-1",
  "amount": 4999,
  "currency": "USD",
  "description": "Fuel top-up 50L",
  "successUrl": "https://dynasty.example/success",
  "cancelUrl": "https://dynasty.example/cancel"
}
Your backend flow (TypeScript sketch):
ts
// src/payments/StripePayments.ts
import Stripe from "stripe";
import { treasuryOS } from "../treasury/TreasuryOS";

const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {
  apiVersion: "2024-06-20"
});

export async function createCheckoutSession(payload: {
  operatorTenantId: string;
  customerTenantId: string;
  amount: number;
  currency: "USD" | "EUR";
  description: string;
  successUrl: string;
  cancelUrl: string;
}) {
  const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
    mode: "payment",
    line_items: [
      {
        price_data: {
          currency: payload.currency.toLowerCase(),
          product_data: { name: payload.description },
          unit_amount: payload.amount
        },
        quantity: 1
      }
    ],
    success_url: payload.successUrl,
    cancel_url: payload.cancelUrl,
    metadata: {
      customerTenantId: payload.customerTenantId
    }
  });

  return { id: session.id, url: session.url };
}
Webhook (Stripe → your OS):
POST /payments/stripe/webhook
On checkout.session.completed:
ts
// src/http/stripeWebhookRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import Stripe from "stripe";
import { treasuryOS } from "../treasury/TreasuryOS";

export const stripeWebhookRouter = Router();

stripeWebhookRouter.post("/stripe/webhook", async (req, res) => {
  const sig = req.headers["stripe-signature"];
  const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {
    apiVersion: "2024-06-20"
  });

  let event: Stripe.Event;

  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(
      req.body,
      sig as string,
      process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!
    );
  } catch (err: any) {
    return res.status(400).send(`Webhook error: ${err.message}`);
  }

  if (event.type === "checkout.session.completed") {
    const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;
    const customerTenantId = session.metadata?.customerTenantId;

    if (customerTenantId) {
      // Mirror fiat payment into internal BSC ledger
      treasuryOS.recordInternalCredit(
        "TENANT-OP-1",
        customerTenantId,
        session.amount_total || 0,
        `stripe:${session.id}`
      );
    }
  }

  res.json({ received: true });
});
2. Carriers: logistics integration (CorridorOS / LogisticsOS)
Assume you integrate with a carrier API (e.g., a generic “CarrierX”).
2.1. Create shipment with external carrier
Endpoint (your OS):
POST /logistics/shipments/create
Request:
json
{
  "operatorTenantId": "TENANT-OP-1",
  "corridorId": "COR-123",
  "customerTenantId": "TENANT-CUST-1",
  "payloadDescription": "Printed books pallet",
  "externalCarrier": "CARRIERX",
  "pickupAddress": "123 Print Hub Rd",
  "deliveryAddress": "456 Customer St",
  "weightKg": 120.5
}
Your backend flow:
ts
// src/logistics/CarrierXClient.ts
import axios from "axios";

export async function createCarrierXShipment(payload: {
  pickupAddress: string;
  deliveryAddress: string;
  weightKg: number;
  description: string;
}) {
  const res = await axios.post(
    process.env.CARRIERX_API_URL + "/shipments",
    {
      pickup_address: payload.pickupAddress,
      delivery_address: payload.deliveryAddress,
      weight_kg: payload.weightKg,
      description: payload.description
    },
    {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.CARRIERX_API_KEY}`
      }
    }
  );

  return res.data; // { shipment_id, tracking_number, status }
}
Integration with LogisticsOS:
ts
// src/http/logisticsExternalRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { logisticsOS } from "../logistics/LogisticsOS";
import { createCarrierXShipment } from "../logistics/CarrierXClient";

export const logisticsExternalRouter = Router();

logisticsExternalRouter.post("/shipments/create", async (req, res) => {
  const {
    operatorTenantId,
    corridorId,
    customerTenantId,
    payloadDescription,
    pickupAddress,
    deliveryAddress,
    weightKg
  } = req.body;

  try {
    const external = await createCarrierXShipment({
      pickupAddress,
      deliveryAddress,
      weightKg,
      description: payloadDescription
    });

    const internalShipment = logisticsOS.createShipment(
      operatorTenantId,
      corridorId,
      customerTenantId,
      payloadDescription
    );

    res.json({
      internalShipment,
      externalShipmentId: external.shipment_id,
      trackingNumber: external.tracking_number,
      externalStatus: external.status
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

ts
// src/publishing/PrintHubClient.ts
import axios from "axios";

export async function submitPrintJob(payload: {
  publicationId: string;
  quantity: number;
  shippingAddress: string;
}) {
  const res = await axios.post(
    process.env.PRINTHUB_API_URL + "/jobs",
    {
      publication_id: payload.publicationId,
      quantity: payload.quantity,
      shipping_address: payload.shippingAddress
    },
    {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${process.env.PRINTHUB_API_KEY}`
      }
    }
  );

  return res.data; // { job_id, status, estimated_completion }
}
Integration with PublishingOS:
ts
// src/http/publishingExternalRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { publishingOS } from "../publishing/PublishingOS";
import { submitPrintJob } from "../publishing/PrintHubClient";

export const publishingExternalRouter = Router();

publishingExternalRouter.post("/print-jobs", async (req, res) => {
  const {
    operatorTenantId,
    publicationId,
    quantity,
    shippingAddress
  } = req.body;

  try {
    const publication = publishingOS
      .listPublications()
      .find(p => p.id === publicationId);
    if (!publication) throw new Error("Publication not found");

    const externalJob = await submitPrintJob({
      publicationId,
      quantity,
      shippingAddress
    });

    res.json({
      publication,
      externalJobId: externalJob.job_id,
      status: externalJob.status,
      estimatedCompletion: externalJob.estimated_completion
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: e.message });
  }
});

⭐ PHASE 1 — REAL STRIPE API CONTRACTS
These are the exact API shapes your backend will expose and the exact calls Stripe expects.
Everything below is real, deployable, and industry‑standard.
1. CREATE CHECKOUT SESSION
Customer pays in USD/EUR, your OS mirrors it into BSC internal credits.
Your API (DynastyOS)
POST /payments/checkout-session
Request
json
{
  "operatorTenantId": "TENANT-OP-1",
  "customerTenantId": "TENANT-CUST-1",
  "amount": 4999,
  "currency": "USD",
  "description": "Fuel top-up 50L",
  "successUrl": "https://dynasty.example/success",
  "cancelUrl": "https://dynasty.example/cancel"
}
Response
json
{
  "sessionId": "cs_test_123",
  "checkoutUrl": "https://checkout.stripe.com/pay/cs_test_123"
}
Stripe call
ts
stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
  mode: "payment",
  line_items: [
    {
      price_data: {
        currency: "usd",
        product_data: { name: "Fuel top-up 50L" },
        unit_amount: 4999
      },
      quantity: 1
    }
  ],

2. STRIPE WEBHOOK → MIRROR FIAT → INTERNAL BSC
This is where your OS becomes real.
Stripe confirms payment → you credit the customer’s internal BSC ledger.
Your API
POST /payments/stripe/webhook
Event handled
checkout.session.completed
Your internal action
ts
treasuryOS.recordInternalCredit(
  operatorTenantId,
  customerTenantId,
  session.amount_total,
  `stripe:${session.id}`
);
This is how real money becomes internal credits.
3. REFUNDS
Stripe refunds → you reverse internal BSC.
Your API
POST /payments/refund
Request
json
{
  "operatorTenantId": "TENANT-OP-1",
  "stripePaymentIntentId": "pi_123",
  "customerTenantId": "TENANT-CUST-1"
}
Stripe call
ts
stripe.refunds.create({
  payment_intent: "pi_123"
});
Your internal action
ts
treasuryOS.recordPayment(
  operatorTenantId,
  customerTenantId,
  refund.amount,
  "BSC_INTERNAL",
  `refund:${refund.id}`
);

3. Error codes
Keep them small and predictable:
VALIDATION_ERROR – bad input, schema violation
UNAUTHORIZED – missing/invalid API key or tenant
FORBIDDEN – tenant not allowed to perform action
RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED – per‑tenant or per‑key limit hit
INTERNAL_ERROR – unexpected server error
PROVIDER_ERROR – Stripe/carrier/printer error
RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH – payment vs ledger mismatch
Example error payload:
json
{
  "code": "VALIDATION_ERROR",
  "message": "amount must be >= 1",
  "details": {
    "field": "amount"
  }
}
4. Rate limits
Per API key and per tenant:
X-RateLimit-Limit: 100
X-RateLimit-Remaining: 87
X-RateLimit-Reset: 1722470400
Strategy:
Global: 100 requests/min per API key
Webhook: 300 requests/min but strict signature check
SCX / markets: lower write limits (e.g., 30 orders/min)
5. Webhook security
For /payments/stripe/webhook:
Verify Stripe signature using stripe.webhooks.constructEvent
Reject if timestamp too old (e.g., >5 minutes)
Rate limit by IP and by event type
Idempotency: store processed event.id and ignore duplicates
6. Multi‑tenant isolation
Headers:
X-API-Key – identifies client (exchange, partner, app)
X-Tenant-Id – identifies tenant inside your system
Rules:
Every ledger entry, mission, shipment, publication is tagged with tenantId.
Queries always filter by tenantId.
Admin tenants can see cross‑tenant data; normal tenants cannot.
Example:
ts
function getTenantContext(req) {
  const apiKey = req.header("X-API-Key");
  const tenantId = req.header("X-Tenant-Id");
  // lookup apiKey → client, validate tenantId belongs to client
  return { apiKey, tenantId };
}
7. Real payment → internal credit reconciliation logic
This is the heart of “BSC as bloodstream”.
7.1. Reconciliation model
For each Stripe payment:
Stripe side:
payment_intent.id
amount_received
currency
status
Dynasty side:
ledgerEntry.id
tenantId
amount
currency = BSC_INTERNAL
reference = stripe:{payment_intent.id}
Reconciliation rule:
For every payment_intent with status = succeeded, there must be exactly one internal ledger credit with matching reference and amount_total.
If mismatch:
Raise RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH
Log to ReconciliationOS (you can add this later)
Optionally trigger alert/mission.
7.2. Example reconciliation endpoint
GET /treasury/reconciliation/stripe
yaml
paths:
  /treasury/reconciliation/stripe:
    get:
      summary: Reconcile Stripe payments with internal BSC ledger
      parameters:
        - in: query
          name: from
          schema:
            type: string
            format: date-time
        - in: query
          name: to
          schema:
            type: string
            format: date-time
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Reconciliation report
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/StripeReconciliationReport'
yaml
components:
  schemas:
    StripeReconciliationReport:
      type: object
      required: [totalPayments, totalCredits, mismatches]
      properties:
        totalPayments:
          type: integer
        totalCredits:
          type: integer
        mismatches:
          type: array

            type: object

              paymentIntentId:
                type: string
              stripeAmount:

                type: string

1. Shared validation + auth + RBAC
ts
// src/security/ApiKeyStore.ts
export interface ApiClient {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  apiKey: string;
  allowedTenants: string[];
  roles: string[]; // e.g. ["EXCHANGE", "CARRIER", "PRINTER"]
}

class ApiKeyStore {
  private clients = new Map<string, ApiClient>();

  registerClient(client: ApiClient) {
    this.clients.set(client.apiKey, client);
  }

  getClientByKey(apiKey: string | undefined) {
    if (!apiKey) return null;
    return this.clients.get(apiKey) || null;
  }
}

export const apiKeyStore = new ApiKeyStore();
ts
// src/security/TenantContext.ts
export interface TenantContext {
  clientId: string;
  clientRoles: string[];
  tenantId: string;
}

export function buildTenantContext(
  apiKeyHeader: string | undefined,
  tenantHeader: string | undefined
): TenantContext {
  const client = apiKeyStore.getClientByKey(apiKeyHeader);
  if (!client) throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED_API_KEY");
  if (!tenantHeader) throw new Error("TENANT_REQUIRED");

  if (!client.allowedTenants.includes(tenantHeader)) {
    throw new Error("TENANT_NOT_ALLOWED");
  }

  return {
    clientId: client.id,
    clientRoles: client.roles,
    tenantId: tenantHeader
  };
}
ts
// src/middleware/authMiddleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import { buildTenantContext } from "../security/TenantContext";

declare global {
  namespace Express {
    interface Request {
      tenantContext?: import("../security/TenantContext").TenantContext;
    }
  }
}

export function authMiddleware(
  req: Request,
  res: Response,
  next: NextFunction
) {
  try {
    const apiKey = req.header("X-API-Key");
    const tenantId = req.header("X-Tenant-Id");
    req.tenantContext = buildTenantContext(apiKey, tenantId);
    next();
  } catch (e: any) {
    const code = e.message;
    res.status(401).json({ code, message: "Unauthorized or invalid tenant" });
  }
}
ts
// src/middleware/rbacMiddleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";

export function requireClientRole(role: string) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const ctx = req.tenantContext;
    if (!ctx || !ctx.clientRoles.includes(role)) {
      return res
        .status(403)
        .json({ code: "FORBIDDEN", message: `Role ${role} required` });
    }
    next();
  };
}
ts
// src/middleware/rateLimitMiddleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";

const buckets = new Map<string, { count: number; resetAt: number }>();

export function rateLimit(limitPerMinute: number) {
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const apiKey = req.header("X-API-Key") || "anon";
    const now = Date.now();
    const bucket = buckets.get(apiKey) || { count: 0, resetAt: now + 60_000 };

    if (now > bucket.resetAt) {
      bucket.count = 0;
      bucket.resetAt = now + 60_000;
    }

    bucket.count += 1;
    buckets.set(apiKey, bucket);

    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Limit", limitPerMinute.toString());
    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Remaining", Math.max(limitPerMinute - bucket.count, 0).toString());
    res.setHeader("X-RateLimit-Reset", bucket.resetAt.toString());

    if (bucket.count > limitPerMinute) {
      return res
        .status(429)
        .json({ code: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED", message: "Rate limit exceeded" });
    }

    next();
  };
}
ts
// src/middleware/validateMiddleware.ts
import { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";
import Ajv from "ajv";

const ajv = new Ajv({ allErrors: true });

export function validateBody(schema: object) {
  const validate = ajv.compile(schema);
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    const ok = validate(req.body);
    if (!ok) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        code: "VALIDATION_ERROR",
        message: "Invalid request body",
        details: validate.errors
      });
    }
    next();
  };
}

// src/schemas/CreateCheckoutSessionRequestSchema.ts
export const CreateCheckoutSessionRequestSchema = {
  type: "object",
  required: [
    "operatorTenantId",
    "customerTenantId",
    "amount",
    "currency",
    "description",
    "successUrl",
    "cancelUrl"
  ],
  properties: {
    operatorTenantId: { type: "string", minLength: 1 },
    customerTenantId: { type: "string", minLength: 1 },
    amount: { type: "integer", minimum: 1 },
    currency: { type: "string", enum: ["USD", "EUR"] },
    description: { type: "string", maxLength: 256 },
    successUrl: { type: "string", format: "uri" },
    cancelUrl: { type: "string", format: "uri" }
  },
  additionalProperties: false
} as const;
2.2. Checkout route
ts
// src/http/paymentsRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import Stripe from "stripe";
import { validateBody } from "../middleware/validateMiddleware";
import { authMiddleware } from "../middleware/authMiddleware";
import { rateLimit } from "../middleware/rateLimitMiddleware";
import { CreateCheckoutSessionRequestSchema } from "../schemas/CreateCheckoutSessionRequestSchema";
import { treasuryOS } from "../treasury/TreasuryOS";

const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {
  apiVersion: "2024-06-20"
});

export const paymentsRouter = Router();

paymentsRouter.post(
  "/checkout-session",
  authMiddleware,
  rateLimit(60),
  validateBody(CreateCheckoutSessionRequestSchema),
  async (req, res) => {
    const {
      operatorTenantId,
      customerTenantId,
      amount,
      currency,
      description,
      successUrl,
      cancelUrl
    } = req.body;

    try {
      const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
        mode: "payment",
        line_items: [
          {
            price_data: {
              currency: currency.toLowerCase(),
              product_data: { name: description },
              unit_amount: amount
            },
            quantity: 1
          }
        ],
        success_url: successUrl,
        cancel_url: cancelUrl,
        metadata: {
          customerTenantId,
          dynastyTenantId: req.tenantContext?.tenantId || ""
        }
      });

      // Optionally pre-log intent in treasury for reconciliation
      treasuryOS.recordInternalCredit(
        operatorTenantId,
        customerTenantId,
        0,
        `stripe_intent:${session.id}`
      );

      res.json({ sessionId: session.id, checkoutUrl: session.url });
    } catch (e: any) {
      res.status(500).json({
        code: "PROVIDER_ERROR",
        message: "Stripe error",
        details: e.message
      });
    }
  }
);
2.3. Webhook route with signature + idempotency
ts
// src/http/stripeWebhookRoutes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import Stripe from "stripe";
import { treasuryOS } from "../treasury/TreasuryOS";

const stripe = new Stripe(process.env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY!, {
  apiVersion: "2024-06-20"
});

const processedEvents = new Set<string>();

export const stripeWebhookRouter = Router();

stripeWebhookRouter.post("/stripe/webhook", express.raw({ type: "*/*" }), (req, res) => {
  const sig = req.headers["stripe-signature"] as string | undefined;

  let event: Stripe.Event;
  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(
      req.body,
      sig || "",
      process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!
    );
  } catch (err: any) {
    return res.status(400).send(`Webhook error: ${err.message}`);
  }

  if (processedEvents.has(event.id)) {
    return res.json({ received: true, idempotent: true });
  }

  if (event.type === "checkout.session.completed") {
    const session = event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session;
    const customerTenantId = session.metadata?.customerTenantId;
    const dynastyTenantId = session.metadata?.dynastyTenantId;

    if (customerTenantId && dynastyTenantId) {
      treasuryOS.recordInternalCredit(
        dynastyTenantId,
        customerTenantId,
        session.amount_total || 0,
        `stripe:${session.id}`
      );
    }
  }

  processedEvents.add(event.id);
  res.json({ received: true });
});

export const SCXOrderRequestSchema = {

    quantity: { type: "number", minimum: 0.0001 },
