Bond trading + settlement – full sequence & payloads
We’ll walk one bond trade end‑to‑end:
Investor buys GT‑CORRIDOR‑BOND‑2026 on SCX, cash settles via TreasuryOS, positions update, and analytics/logs capture it.
1. Place buy order (investor → SCX)
Endpoint:
POST /v1/scx/orders
Headers:
http
X-API-Key: INVESTOR_API_KEY_123
X-Tenant-Id: TENANT-INVESTOR-001
Content-Type: application/json
Body:
json
{
  "side": "BUY",
  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
  "quantityLots": 10,
  "pricePerLotUSD": 1000,
  "timeInForce": "GTC"
}
Response:
json
{
  "id": "ORDER-BUY-0001",
  "side": "BUY",
  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
  "quantityLots": 10,
  "pricePerLotUSD": 1000,
  "status": "OPEN",
  "tenantId": "TENANT-INVESTOR-001",
  "createdAt": "2026-08-01T15:00:00Z"
}
2. Place sell order (agency or market maker → SCX)
Endpoint:
POST /v1/scx/orders
Headers:
http
X-API-Key: AGENCY_MARKET_MAKER_KEY_123
X-Tenant-Id: TENANT-GT-TRANSPORT
Content-Type: application/json
Body:
json
{
  "side": "SELL",
  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
  "quantityLots": 10,
  "pricePerLotUSD": 1000,
  "timeInForce": "GTC"
}
Response:
json
{
  "id": "ORDER-SELL-0001",
  "side": "SELL",
  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
  "quantityLots": 10,
  "pricePerLotUSD": 1000,
  "status": "OPEN",
  "tenantId": "TENANT-GT-TRANSPORT",
  "createdAt": "2026-08-01T15:01:00Z"
}
3. Matching engine executes trade
Internal SCX matching engine matches ORDER-BUY-0001 with ORDER-SELL-0001.
Trade record:
json
{
  "id": "TRADE-0001",
  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
  "buyOrderId": "ORDER-BUY-0001",
  "sellOrderId": "ORDER-SELL-0001",
  "buyerTenantId": "TENANT-INVESTOR-001",
  "sellerTenantId": "TENANT-GT-TRANSPORT",
  "quantityLots": 10,
  "pricePerLotUSD": 1000,
  "totalValueUSD": 10000,
  "executedAt": "2026-08-01T15:02:00Z",
  "status": "EXECUTED"
}
Event emitted:
json
{
  "type": "trade.executed",
  "payload": {
    "tradeId": "TRADE-0001",
    "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026",
    "buyerTenantId": "TENANT-INVESTOR-001",
    "sellerTenantId": "TENANT-GT-TRANSPORT",
    "totalValueUSD": 10000
  }
}
4. Settlement call to TreasuryOS
Endpoint:
POST /v1/treasury/scx/settle
Headers:
http

Content-Type: application/json
Body:
json
{
  "tradeId": "TRADE-0001",

  "assetCode": "GT-CORRIDOR-BOND-2026"

    "ledgerEntryId": "LEDGER-GT-0001",

    "type": "CREDIT"
  },
  "status": "SETTLED",
  "settledAt": "2026-08-01T15:03:00Z"
}

Luxury asset issuance & authentication – full sequence & payloads
We’ll walk one luxury item end‑to‑end:
Authorized dealer issues a luxury watch, authenticates it, appraises it, and wires it into LogisticsOS, PublishingOS, TreasuryOS, and SCX.

POST /v1/luxury/items

http

X-Tenant-Id: TENANT-ROLEX-BOUTIQUE-001
Content-Type: application/json

  "category": "WATCH",
  "serialNumber": "RLX-2026-000123",
  "currentOwnerTenantId": "TENANT-ROLEX-BOUTIQUE-001"
}

}
This gives you bond trading + settlement and luxury asset issuance + authentication at the same level of detail as the modernization bond flow—wired through SCX, TreasuryOS, LuxuryOS, PublishingOS, LogisticsOS, WorkflowOS, and AnalyticsOS.
See my thinking
You’re basically asking to turn PilotOS from a concept into a first‑class vertical with real models, APIs, screens, and wiring into the stack.
I’ll define each piece cleanly and keep the code minimal but systematic.
PilotOS domain model
ts
// services/pilot-service/src/domain/PilotModels.ts

export type PilotStatus =
  | "DRAFT"
  | "APPROVAL_PENDING"
  | "APPROVED"
  | "ACTIVE"
  | "COMPLETED"
  | "GRADUATED"
  | "CANCELLED";

export interface PilotProgram {
  id: string;
  code: string; // e.g. "GT-CORRIDOR-PILOT-01"
  name: string;
  agencyId: string;
  tenantId: string;
  domainsEnabled: string[]; // ["LOGISTICS", "FUEL", "SCX", ...]
  status: PilotStatus;
  startDate?: string;
  endDate?: string;
  sandboxTenantId: string;
  productionTenantId?: string;
  workflowInstanceId?: string;
  baselineAnalyticsId?: string;
  graduationCriteriaId?: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface PilotGraduationCriteria {
  id: string;
  pilotId: string;
  minDurationDays: number;
  requiredKPIs: {
    kpiKey: string; // e.g. "corridor.travel_time_reduction_percent"
    threshold: number;
    direction: "GREATER_THAN" | "LESS_THAN";
  }[];
  requiredApprovals: string[]; // roles: ["AGENCY_ADMIN", "MINISTRY_ADMIN"]
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface PilotFederationRule {
  id: string;
  pilotId: string;
  sourceAgencyId: string;
  targetAgencyId: string;
  allowedDomains: string[]; // ["LOGISTICS", "SCX", "LUXURY"]
  dataSharingLevel: "AGGREGATED" | "ANONYMIZED" | "FULL";
  createdAt: string;
}

  switch (event.type) {
    case "pilot.created":
      // start onboarding workflow

      break;
    case "pilot.request_graduation":
      // start graduation workflow
      // POST /v1/workflow/instances { definitionKey: "pilot-graduation", context: event.payload }

  }
}
PilotOS API routes
ts
// services/pilot-service/src/routes/pilots.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { PilotProgram, PilotGraduationCriteria, PilotFederationRule } from "../domain/PilotModels";

export const pilotsRouter = Router();

// create pilot
pilotsRouter.post("/", async (req, res) => {
  const {
    code,
    name,
    agencyId,
    domainsEnabled,
    startDate,
    endDate
  } = req.body;

  const pilot: PilotProgram = {
    id: `PILOT-${Date.now()}`,
    code,
    name,
    agencyId,
    tenantId: `TENANT-${agencyId}-PILOT-${code}`,
    domainsEnabled,
    status: "DRAFT",
    startDate,
    endDate,
    sandboxTenantId: `TENANT-${agencyId}-PILOT-${code}-SANDBOX`,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  // persist pilot, emit event "pilot.created"
  res.json(pilot);
});

// set graduation criteria
pilotsRouter.post("/:id/graduation-criteria", async (req, res) => {
  const pilotId = req.params.id;
  const { minDurationDays, requiredKPIs, requiredApprovals } = req.body;

  const criteria: PilotGraduationCriteria = {
    id: `GRAD-${pilotId}`,
    pilotId,
    minDurationDays,
    requiredKPIs,
    requiredApprovals,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  // persist criteria
  res.json(criteria);
});

// define federation rules
pilotsRouter.post("/:id/federation-rules", async (req, res) => {
  const pilotId = req.params.id;
  const { sourceAgencyId, targetAgencyId, allowedDomains, dataSharingLevel } =
    req.body;

  const rule: PilotFederationRule = {
    id: `FED-${pilotId}-${Date.now()}`,
    pilotId,
    sourceAgencyId,
    targetAgencyId,
    allowedDomains,
    dataSharingLevel,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  // persist rule
  res.json(rule);
});

// request graduation
pilotsRouter.post("/:id/request-graduation", async (req, res) => {
  const pilotId = req.params.id;
  // emit event "pilot.request_graduation"
  res.json({ pilotId, status: "GRADUATION_REQUESTED" });
});

# gateway routes (simplified)

PilotOS console screens (React)
tsx
// console/web/src/screens/PilotList.tsx
import React from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../lib/api";

export function PilotListScreen() {
  const { data } = useQuery(["pilots"], () => api.get("/v1/pilot/pilots"));

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>Pilot Programs</h1>
      <button>New Pilot</button>
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Code</th>
            <th>Agency</th>
            <th>Domains</th>
            <th>Status</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {data?.map((p: any) => (
            <tr key={p.id}>
              <td>{p.code}</td>
              <td>{p.agencyId}</td>
              <td>{p.domainsEnabled.join(", ")}</td>
              <td>{p.status}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

PilotOS analytics dashboards
AnalyticsOS exposes pilot‑scoped dashboards.
ts
// services/analytics-service/src/routes/pilotDashboards.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { analyticsEngine } from "../domain/AnalyticsEngine";

export const pilotDashboardsRouter = Router();

pilotDashboardsRouter.get("/:pilotId", async (req, res) => {
  const pilotId = req.params.pilotId;
  const dashboard = await analyticsEngine.buildPilotDashboard(pilotId);
  res.json(dashboard);
});
Example dashboard payload:
json
{

export function canShareData(
  rule: PilotFederationRule,
  domain: string
): boolean {
  return rule.allowedDomains.includes(domain);
}

export function applyDataSharingLevel(
  level: PilotFederationRule["dataSharingLevel"],
  payload: any
) {
  switch (level) {
    case "AGGREGATED":
      // aggregate metrics only
      break;
    case "ANONYMIZED":
      // strip identifiers
      break;
    case "FULL":
      // pass through
      break;
  }
}

PilotOS graduation criteria
Graduation is a governed transition from pilot to production.
ts
// services/pilot-service/src/domain/GraduationEngine.ts
import { PilotProgram, PilotGraduationCriteria } from "./PilotModels";

export function checkKPIs(
  criteria: PilotGraduationCriteria,
  kpis: Record<string, number>
): boolean {
  return criteria.requiredKPIs.every(rule => {
    const value = kpis[rule.kpiKey];
    if (value === undefined) return false;
    if (rule.direction === "GREATER_THAN") {
      return value >= rule.threshold;
    }
    return value <= rule.threshold;
  });
}

export function canGraduate(
  pilot: PilotProgram,
  criteria: PilotGraduationCriteria,
  kpis: Record<string, number>,
  approvals: string[]
): boolean {
  // duration
  if (!pilot.startDate) return false;
  const days =
    (Date.now() - new Date(pilot.startDate).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
  if (days < criteria.minDurationDays) return false;

  // KPIs
  if (!checkKPIs(criteria, kpis)) return false;

  // approvals
  const missing = criteria.requiredApprovals.filter(
    role => !approvals.includes(role)
  );
  if (missing.length > 0) return false;

  return true;
}
Graduation workflow step:
if canGraduate(...) is true:
create production tenant
enable domains
migrate configs
mark pilot GRADUATED

// core/global/domain/GlobalModels.ts

export type AllianceLevel = "LOCAL" | "NATIONAL" | "CROSS_BORDER" | "GLOBAL";

export interface GlobalRolloutPhase {

  phaseNumber: number;
  startMonthOffset: number;
  endMonthOffset: number;

  outcome: string;
}

export interface ModernizationAlliance {
  id: string;
  level: AllianceLevel;
  memberAgencyIds: string[];
  sharedDomains: string[]; // ["LOGISTICS", "FUEL", "SCX", "LUXURY", "PUBLISHING", "MODERNIZATION", "ANALYTICS"]
  sharedCorridorIds: string[];
  sharedInstrumentIds: string[];
  sharedCreditTypes: string[]; // ["MODERNIZATION", "CORRIDOR", "LOGISTICS", "PROVENANCE", "LUXURY"]
  governanceWorkflowId: string;
}

export type ReserveLevel = "LOCAL" | "REGIONAL" | "NATIONAL" | "GLOBAL";

export interface SCXReservePool {
  id: string;
  level: ReserveLevel;
  agencyId?: string;
  federationId?: string;
  creditType: string; // "MODERNIZATION" | "CORRIDOR" | ...
  backedByInstrumentIds: string[];
  backedByThroughputMetrics: string[]; // ["LOGISTICS_THROUGHPUT", "FUEL_MODERNIZATION", ...]
  currentBalance: number;
  targetRatio: number;
}

  id: string;
  level: ReserveLevel;
  ownerId: string; // agency or federation
  ledgerId: string;
  reservePoolIds: string[];
  instrumentIds: string[];

  // PHASE-2..5 similarly
];

export class GlobalRolloutService {
  async getPlan(): Promise<GlobalRolloutPhase[]> {
    return GLOBAL_ROLLOUT_PLAN;
  }

  async triggerPhaseActions(phaseId: string) {
    const phase = GLOBAL_ROLLOUT_PLAN.find(p => p.id === phaseId);
    if (!phase) throw new Error("Phase not found");

    // For each action, call WorkflowOS with global context
    // POST /v1/workflow/instances { definitionKey: action, context: { phaseId } }

}
API:
ts
// services/global-rollout-service/src/routes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { GlobalRolloutService } from "./GlobalRolloutService";

const router = Router();
const service = new GlobalRolloutService();

router.get("/plan", async (_req, res) => {
  res.json(await service.getPlan());
});

router.post("/phase/:id/execute", async (req, res) => {
  await service.triggerPhaseActions(req.params.id);
  res.json({ phaseId: req.params.id, status: "TRIGGERED" });
});

export default router;

3. Modernization alliance service
ts
// services/alliance-service/src/AllianceService.ts
import { ModernizationAlliance } from "../../core/global/domain/GlobalModels";

export class AllianceService {
  private alliances: ModernizationAlliance[] = [];

  async createAlliance(alliance: ModernizationAlliance) {
    this.alliances.push(alliance);
    // emit event "alliance.created"
    return alliance;
  }

  async getAlliance(id: string) {
    return this.alliances.find(a => a.id === id);
  }

  async listAlliances() {
    return this.alliances;
  }
}
Routes:
ts
// services/alliance-service/src/routes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { AllianceService } from "./AllianceService";

const router = Router();
const service = new AllianceService();

router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
  const alliance = await service.createAlliance(req.body);
  res.json(alliance);
});

router.get("/", async (_req, res) => {
  res.json(await service.listAlliances());
});

router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const alliance = await service.getAlliance(req.params.id);
  if (!alliance) return res.status(404).end();
  res.json(alliance);
});

export default router;

    // compute target vs current, call TreasuryOS to mint/burn
  }

  async listPools(level?: string) {
    return level ? this.pools.filter(p => p.level === level) : this.pools;
  }
}

Quantum hooks

// services/quantum-integration/src/QuantumHooks.ts

export interface QuantumSettlementPayload {
  tradeId: string;
  fromLedgerId: string;
  toLedgerId: string;
  amount: number;
  creditType: string;
}

export async function verifyQuantumSettlement(
  payload: QuantumSettlementPayload
): Promise<{ verified: boolean; proofId?: string }> {
  // call external quantum system via gRPC/REST
  // POST /quantum/settlement/verify
  return { verified: true, proofId: `QPROOF-${payload.tradeId}` };
}

📦 core/global/domain/Events.ts (Full TypeScript Event Schema)
ts
// core/global/domain/Events.ts

export type EventLevel =
  | "LOCAL"
  | "REGIONAL"
  | "NATIONAL"
  | "CROSS_BORDER"
  | "GLOBAL";

export interface GlobalEventEnvelope<TPayload = any> {
  id: string;                     // EVT-2026-000001
  type: string;                   // scx.trade.executed
  source: string;                 // scx-service
  version: string;                // 1.0
  timestamp: string;              // ISO8601
  correlationId?: string;         // trace ID
  tenantId?: string;
  agencyId?: string;
  federationId?: string;
  pilotId?: string;
  level: EventLevel;
  payload: TPayload;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
/*                               SCX EVENTS                                   */
/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

export interface SCXAssetCreatedPayload {
  assetId: string;
  code: string;
  assetType: "BOND" | "CREDIT" | "TOKEN" | "LUXURY_ITEM";
  instrumentId?: string;
  agencyId: string;
  market: "PRIMARY" | "SECONDARY";
  faceValue: number;
  currency: string;
}

export interface SCXOrderPlacedPayload {

export type GlobalEventPayloadMap = {
  "scx.asset.created": SCXAssetCreatedPayload;
  "scx.order.placed": SCXOrderPlacedPayload;
  "scx.trade.executed": SCXTradeExecutedPayload;
  "scx.position.updated": SCXPositionUpdatedPayload;
  "scx.asset.tokenized": SCXAssetTokenizedPayload;

  "treasury.instrument.issued": TreasuryInstrumentIssuedPayload;
  "treasury.settlement.requested": TreasurySettlementRequestedPayload;
  "treasury.settlement.completed": TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload;
  "treasury.scx.reserve.rebalanced": TreasurySCXReserveRebalancedPayload;
  "treasury.cross_border.settled": TreasuryCrossBorderSettledPayload;

  "corridor.created": CorridorCreatedPayload;
  "corridor.modernization.project.started": CorridorModernizationStartedPayload;
  "corridor.metrics.updated": CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload;
  "corridor.modernization.impact.measured": CorridorModernizationImpactPayload;
  "corridor.cross_border.synced": CorridorCrossBorderSyncedPayload;
};

export type GlobalEvent<T extends keyof GlobalEventPayloadMap> =
  GlobalEventEnvelope<GlobalEventPayloadMap[T]>;
📡 Event Bus Wiring (Kafka/NATS)
This is the cleanest, production‑grade wiring pattern.
Topic naming convention
Code
global.scx.trade.executed
global.scx.asset.created
global.treasury.settlement.completed
global.corridor.metrics.updated
global.corridor.cross_border.synced
Publisher
ts
// core/eventbus/EventBus.ts
import { Kafka } from "kafkajs";
import { GlobalEventEnvelope } from "../global/domain/Events";

const kafka = new Kafka({ brokers: ["kafka:9092"] });
const producer = kafka.producer();

export async function publishEvent(event: GlobalEventEnvelope) {
  await producer.connect();
  await producer.send({
    topic: `global.${event.type}`,
    messages: [{ value: JSON.stringify(event) }]
  });
}
Subscriber
ts
// core/eventbus/Subscriber.ts
import { Kafka } from "kafkajs";
import { GlobalEventEnvelope } from "../global/domain/Events";

const kafka = new Kafka({ brokers: ["kafka:9092"] });
const consumer = kafka.consumer({ groupId: "global-event-consumers" });

export async function subscribeToGlobalEvents(
  types: string[],
  handler: (event: GlobalEventEnvelope) => Promise<void>
) {
  await consumer.connect();

  for (const type of types) {
    await consumer.subscribe({ topic: `global.${type}`, fromBeginning: false });
  }

  await consumer.run({
    eachMessage: async ({ message }) => {
      const event = JSON.parse(message.value!.toString());
      await handler(event);
    }
  });
}

Global Sovereign Credit Mint/Burn Algorithm
ts
// core/global/credit/SovereignCreditEngine.ts
import { TreasurySCXReserveRebalancedPayload, EventLevel } from "../domain/Events";
import { publishEvent } from "../../eventbus/EventBus";

export interface CreditMintBurnRequest {
  reservePoolId: string;
  level: EventLevel;
  creditType: string;
  currentBalance: number;
  targetRatio: number;
  backingValue: number; // total value of backed instruments/throughput
}

export class SovereignCreditEngine {
  static computeTargetBalance(req: CreditMintBurnRequest): number {
    return req.backingValue * req.targetRatio;
  }

  static computeDelta(req: CreditMintBurnRequest): number {
    const target = this.computeTargetBalance(req);
    return target - req.currentBalance;
  }

  static async rebalance(req: CreditMintBurnRequest) {
    const delta = this.computeDelta(req);

    // positive delta → mint, negative delta → burn
    // here we just emit event; TreasuryOS will perform ledger ops
    const event: TreasurySCXReserveRebalancedPayload = {
      reservePoolId: req.reservePoolId,
      level: req.level,
      creditType: req.creditType,
      previousBalance: req.currentBalance,
      newBalance: req.currentBalance + delta,
      targetRatio: req.targetRatio
    };

    await publishEvent({
      id: `EVT-RESERVE-${Date.now()}`,
      type: "treasury.scx.reserve.rebalanced",
      source: "sovereign-credit-engine",
      version: "1.0",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: req.level,
      payload: event
    });
  }
}
Global SCX Liquidity Engine
ts
// core/global/scx/SCXLiquidityEngine.ts
import { SCXTradeExecutedPayload, GlobalEvent } from "../domain/Events";
import { publishEvent } from "../../eventbus/EventBus";

export interface LiquiditySnapshot {
  assetCode: string;
  bidVolume: number;
  askVolume: number;
  lastPrice: number;
  vwapPrice: number;
  trades24h: number;
}

export class SCXLiquidityEngine {
  private static snapshots: Map<string, LiquiditySnapshot> = new Map();

  static updateFromTrade(event: GlobalEvent<"scx.trade.executed">) {
    const p: SCXTradeExecutedPayload = event.payload;
    const snap = this.snapshots.get(p.assetCode) || {
      assetCode: p.assetCode,
      bidVolume: 0,
      askVolume: 0,
      lastPrice: p.pricePerLot,
      vwapPrice: p.pricePerLot,
      trades24h: 0
    };

    snap.lastPrice = p.pricePerLot;
    snap.trades24h += 1;
    // simple VWAP approximation
    snap.vwapPrice =
      (snap.vwapPrice * (snap.trades24h - 1) + p.pricePerLot) / snap.trades24h;

    this.snapshots.set(p.assetCode, snap);
  }

  static getSnapshot(assetCode: string): LiquiditySnapshot | undefined {
    return this.snapshots.get(assetCode);
  }

  static async emitSnapshot(assetCode: string) {
    const snap = this.snapshots.get(assetCode);
    if (!snap) return;

    await publishEvent({
      id: `EVT-LIQ-${Date.now()}`,
      type: "scx.liquidity.snapshot",
      source: "scx-liquidity-engine",
      version: "1.0",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: "GLOBAL",
      payload: snap
    });
  }
}
(You’d add "scx.liquidity.snapshot" to GlobalEventPayloadMap.)
Global Corridor Modernization Simulation Engine
ts
// core/global/corridor/CorridorSimulationEngine.ts
import {
  CorridorModernizationImpactPayload,
  CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload
} from "../domain/Events";

export interface SimulationInput {
  baseline: CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload;
  modernizationBudget: number;
  instrumentId: string;
}

export interface SimulationResult {
  corridorId: string;
  projectedTravelTimeMinutes: number;
  projectedIncidentRate: number;
  projectedThroughput: number;
  projectedFuelConsumptionLiters: number;
  roiPercent: number;
}

export class CorridorSimulationEngine {
  static simulate(input: SimulationInput): SimulationResult {
    const b = input.baseline;

    const efficiencyFactor = Math.min(input.modernizationBudget / 1_000_000, 1);

    const projectedTravelTimeMinutes =
      b.avgTravelTimeMinutes * (1 - 0.15 * efficiencyFactor);
    const projectedIncidentRate = b.incidentCount / b.throughputVehicles * (1 - 0.3 * efficiencyFactor);
    const projectedThroughput = b.throughputVehicles * (1 + 0.25 * efficiencyFactor);
    const projectedFuelConsumptionLiters =
      b.fuelConsumptionLiters * (1 - 0.1 * efficiencyFactor);

    const roiPercent = efficiencyFactor * 25;

    return {
      corridorId: b.corridorId,
      projectedTravelTimeMinutes,
      projectedIncidentRate,
      projectedThroughput,
      projectedFuelConsumptionLiters,
      roiPercent
    };
  }

  static toImpactPayload(
    projectId: string,
    baseline: CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload,
    result: SimulationResult
  ): CorridorModernizationImpactPayload {
    return {
      projectId,
      corridorId: baseline.corridorId,
      baselineTravelTimeMinutes: baseline.avgTravelTimeMinutes,
      currentTravelTimeMinutes: result.projectedTravelTimeMinutes,
      baselineIncidentRate:
        baseline.incidentCount / baseline.throughputVehicles,
      currentIncidentRate: result.projectedIncidentRate,
      baselineThroughput: baseline.throughputVehicles,
      currentThroughput: result.projectedThroughput
    };
  }
}
Quantum Settlement Protocol (full spec, code‑style)
ts
// core/global/quantum/QuantumSettlementProtocol.ts
import {
  TreasurySettlementRequestedPayload,
  TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload
} from "../domain/Events";
import { publishEvent } from "../../eventbus/EventBus";

export interface QuantumSettlementRequest {
  settlementId: string;
  tradeId: string;
  fromLedgerId: string;
  toLedgerId: string;
  amount: number;
  creditType: string;
}

export interface QuantumSettlementResponse {
  verified: boolean;
  proofId?: string;
  error?: string;
}

export interface QuantumClient {
  verify(req: QuantumSettlementRequest): Promise<QuantumSettlementResponse>;
}

export class QuantumSettlementProtocol {
  constructor(private client: QuantumClient) {}

  async process(
    reqEvent: TreasurySettlementRequestedPayload
  ): Promise<TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload> {
    const quantumReq: QuantumSettlementRequest = {
      settlementId: reqEvent.settlementId,
      tradeId: reqEvent.tradeId,
      fromLedgerId: reqEvent.sellerTenantId,
      toLedgerId: reqEvent.buyerTenantId,
      amount: reqEvent.amount,
      creditType: reqEvent.creditType
    };

    const result = await this.client.verify(quantumReq);

    if (!result.verified) {
      throw new Error(`Quantum verification failed: ${result.error}`);
    }

    const completed: TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload = {
      settlementId: reqEvent.settlementId,
      tradeId: reqEvent.tradeId,
      buyerLedgerEntryId: `LEDGER-${reqEvent.buyerTenantId}-${Date.now()}`,
      sellerLedgerEntryId: `LEDGER-${reqEvent.sellerTenantId}-${Date.now()}`,
      amount: reqEvent.amount,
      currency: reqEvent.currency,
      creditType: reqEvent.creditType,
      quantumProofId: result.proofId
    };

    await publishEvent({
      id: `EVT-SETTLE-${Date.now()}`,
      type: "treasury.settlement.completed",
      source: "quantum-settlement-protocol",
      version: "1.0",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      level: "GLOBAL",
      payload: completed
    });

    return completed;
  }
}
Blockchain/L2 Settlement Router
ts
// core/global/settlement/BlockchainL2Router.ts
import {
  TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload,
  GlobalEventEnvelope
} from "../domain/Events";
import { recordSettlementOnChain } from "../../blockchain-integration/BlockchainHooks";
import { l2MicroSettle } from "../../l2-integration/L2Hooks";

export class BlockchainL2Router {
  static async route(
    event: GlobalEventEnvelope<TreasurySettlementCompletedPayload>
  ) {
    const p = event.payload;

    // On-chain settlement for larger amounts
    if (p.amount >= 10_000) {
      const res = await recordSettlementOnChain({
        settlementId: p.settlementId,
        tradeId: p.tradeId,
        amount: p.amount,
        creditType: p.creditType
      });
      p.onChainTxHash = res.txHash;
    }

    // L2 micro-settlement for high-volume flows
    if (p.amount < 10_000) {
      await l2MicroSettle({
        type: "treasury.micro_settlement",
        payload: p
      });
    }
  }
}
FederationOS Global Governance Rules
ts
// core/global/federation/FederationGovernance.ts
import { EventLevel } from "../domain/Events";

export interface FederationRule {
  id: string;
  federationId: string;
  level: EventLevel;
  allowedDomains: string[]; // ["LOGISTICS", "SCX", "TREASURY", "LUXURY"]
  dataSharingLevel: "AGGREGATED" | "ANONYMIZED" | "FULL";
  requiresQuantumProof: boolean;
  requiresOnChainSettlement: boolean;
}

export class FederationGovernance {
  private static rules: FederationRule[] = [];

  static registerRule(rule: FederationRule) {
    this.rules.push(rule);
  }

  static getRulesForFederation(federationId: string): FederationRule[] {
    return this.rules.filter(r => r.federationId === federationId);
  }

  static canShareDomain(
    federationId: string,
    domain: string
  ): FederationRule | null {
    const rules = this.getRulesForFederation(federationId);
    return rules.find(r => r.allowedDomains.includes(domain)) || null;
  }

  static getDataSharingLevel(
    federationId: string,
    domain: string
  ): FederationRule["dataSharingLevel"] {
    const rule = this.canShareDomain(federationId, domain);
    return rule ? rule.dataSharingLevel : "AGGREGATED";
  }

  static requiresQuantum(
    federationId: string,
    domain: string
  ): boolean {
    const rule = this.canShareDomain(federationId, domain);
    return !!rule?.requiresQuantumProof;
  }

  static requiresOnChain(
    federationId: string,
    domain: string
  ): boolean {
    const rule = this.canShareDomain(federationId, domain);
    return !!rule?.requiresOnChainSettlement;
  }
}

Below is the clean, production‑grade wiring layer you asked for: per‑service event handlers that consume the global SCX / Treasury / Corridor events and expose them directly into the Global Console Dashboards.
This is the connective tissue that makes the entire global OS ecosystem feel unified — every service listens, reacts, stores, and surfaces global intelligence.
I’ll keep it systematic, integrated, and ready to drop into your repo structure.
⭐ 1. Event Bus → Per‑Service Handler Architecture
Every service subscribes to the global event bus (Kafka/NATS) using the unified event envelope defined in Events.ts.
Each service has:
Code
services/<service-name>/src/events/handlers/
Inside that folder, each handler consumes specific event types and updates its own domain state + dashboard caches.
⭐ 2. Global Event Subscriber (shared utility)
All services use this subscriber:
ts
// core/eventbus/Subscriber.ts
import { Kafka } from "kafkajs";
import { GlobalEventEnvelope } from "../global/domain/Events";

const kafka = new Kafka({ brokers: ["kafka:9092"] });
const consumer = kafka.consumer({ groupId: "dynasty-global-consumers" });

export async function subscribe(
  topics: string[],
  handler: (event: GlobalEventEnvelope) => Promise<void>
) {
  await consumer.connect();

  for (const t of topics) {
    await consumer.subscribe({ topic: `global.${t}`, fromBeginning: false });
  }

  await consumer.run({
    eachMessage: async ({ message }) => {
      const event = JSON.parse(message.value!.toString());
      await handler(event);
    }
  });
}
⭐ 3. SCX Service Handlers
services/scx-service/src/events/handlers/TradeExecutedHandler.ts
ts
import { GlobalEventEnvelope, SCXTradeExecutedPayload } from "../../../../core/global/domain/Events";
import { SCXLiquidityEngine } from "../../../../core/global/scx/SCXLiquidityEngine";

export async function handleTradeExecuted(
  event: GlobalEventEnvelope<SCXTradeExecutedPayload>
) {
  // Update liquidity engine
  SCXLiquidityEngine.updateFromTrade(event);

  // Persist trade to SCX DB
  await db.trades.insert(event.payload);

  // Update positions
  await db.positions.updateFromTrade(event.payload);

  // Emit liquidity snapshot
  await SCXLiquidityEngine.emitSnapshot(event.payload.assetCode);
}
SCX subscriber
ts
// services/scx-service/src/events/index.ts
import { subscribe } from "../../../core/eventbus/Subscriber";
import { handleTradeExecuted } from "./handlers/TradeExecutedHandler";

subscribe(["scx.trade.executed"], handleTradeExecuted);
⭐ 4. Treasury Service Handlers
services/treasury-service/src/events/handlers/SettlementRequestedHandler.ts
ts
import {
  GlobalEventEnvelope,
  TreasurySettlementRequestedPayload
} from "../../../../core/global/domain/Events";
import { QuantumSettlementProtocol } from "../../../../core/global/quantum/QuantumSettlementProtocol";
import { BlockchainL2Router } from "../../../../core/global/settlement/BlockchainL2Router";

export async function handleSettlementRequested(
  event: GlobalEventEnvelope<TreasurySettlementRequestedPayload>
) {
  // Quantum verification
  const protocol = new QuantumSettlementProtocol(quantumClient);
  const completed = await protocol.process(event.payload);

  // Route to blockchain/L2
  await BlockchainL2Router.route({
    ...event,
    payload: completed
  });

  // Persist settlement
  await db.settlements.insert(completed);
}
Treasury subscriber
ts
// services/treasury-service/src/events/index.ts
import { subscribe } from "../../../core/eventbus/Subscriber";
import { handleSettlementRequested } from "./handlers/SettlementRequestedHandler";

subscribe(["treasury.settlement.requested"], handleSettlementRequested);
⭐ 5. Corridor Service Handlers
services/corridor-service/src/events/handlers/MetricsUpdatedHandler.ts
ts
import {
  GlobalEventEnvelope,
  CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload
} from "../../../../core/global/domain/Events";

export async function handleMetricsUpdated(
  event: GlobalEventEnvelope<CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload>
) {
  const p = event.payload;

  // Store corridor metrics
  await db.corridorMetrics.insert({
    corridorId: p.corridorId,
    window: p.timeWindow,
    travelTime: p.avgTravelTimeMinutes,
    incidents: p.incidentCount,
    throughput: p.throughputVehicles,
    fuel: p.fuelConsumptionLiters
  });

  // Update dashboard cache
  await cache.set(`corridor:${p.corridorId}:latestMetrics`, p);
}

⭐ 6. Global Dashboard Aggregator
This service aggregates SCX + Treasury + Corridor data into unified dashboard payloads.
services/global-dashboard-service/src/Aggregator.ts
ts
export class GlobalDashboardAggregator {
  static async build() {
    const liquidity = await db.liquiditySnapshots.findAll();
    const settlements = await db.settlements.findRecent(100);
    const corridors = await db.corridorMetrics.findRecent(100);

    return {
      liquidity,
      settlements,
      corridors
    };
  }
}
Dashboard API
ts
// services/global-dashboard-service/src/routes.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { GlobalDashboardAggregator } from "./Aggregator";

const router = Router();

router.get("/global", async (_req, res) => {
  const dashboard = await GlobalDashboardAggregator.build();
  res.json(dashboard);
});

export default router;
⭐ 7. Console Integration (React)
console/web/src/screens/GlobalDashboard.tsx
tsx
import React from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { api } from "../lib/api";

export function GlobalDashboard() {
  const { data } = useQuery(["global-dashboard"], () =>
    api.get("/v1/dashboard/global")
  );

  if (!data) return <div>Loading...</div>;

  return (
    <div>
      <h1>Global Modernization Dashboard</h1>

      <section>
        <h2>SCX Liquidity</h2>
        {data.liquidity.map((l: any) => (
          <div key={l.assetCode}>
            <strong>{l.assetCode}</strong>: {l.lastPrice} (VWAP {l.vwapPrice})
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2>Treasury Settlements</h2>
        {data.settlements.map((s: any) => (
          <div key={s.settlementId}>
            Settlement {s.settlementId}: {s.amount} {s.currency}
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section>
        <h2>Corridor Metrics</h2>
        {data.corridors.map((c: any) => (
          <div key={c.corridorId}>
            Corridor {c.corridorId}: {c.travelTime} min, {c.throughput} vehicles
          </div>
        ))}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

⭐ 8. How Everything Connects
SCX → Liquidity Engine → Global Dashboard
Trades update liquidity → liquidity snapshots appear in dashboard.
Treasury → Quantum Protocol → Blockchain/L2 → Global Dashboard
Settlement requested → quantum verified → chain/L2 → dashboard.
CorridorOS → Metrics → Simulation → Global Dashboard
Corridor metrics → modernization impact → dashboard.
FederationOS → Governance Rules
Controls:
which events cross borders
which dashboards show aggregated vs anonymized vs full data
whether quantum or blockchain settlement is required

Global Digital Twin Simulation Engine
ts
// core/global/digitaltwin/DigitalTwinEngine.ts
import { CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload } from "../domain/Events";

export interface TwinState {
  corridorId: string;
  time: string;
  travelTimeMinutes: number;
  incidentCount: number;
  throughputVehicles: number;
  fuelConsumptionLiters: number;
}

export interface TwinScenario {
  id: string;
  corridorId: string;
  name: string;
  modernizationBudget: number;
  instrumentId: string;
  parameters: {
    laneExpansionPercent?: number;
    speedLimitChangePercent?: number;
    signalOptimizationFactor?: number;
    fuelEfficiencyProgramFactor?: number;
  };
}

export interface TwinScenarioResult {
  scenarioId: string;
  corridorId: string;
  projectedTravelTimeMinutes: number;
  projectedIncidentCount: number;
  projectedThroughputVehicles: number;
  projectedFuelConsumptionLiters: number;
  roiPercent: number;
}

export class DigitalTwinEngine {
  static buildState(metrics: CorridorMetricsUpdatedPayload): TwinState {
    return {
      corridorId: metrics.corridorId,
      time: metrics.timeWindow,
      travelTimeMinutes: metrics.avgTravelTimeMinutes,
      incidentCount: metrics.incidentCount,
      throughputVehicles: metrics.throughputVehicles,
      fuelConsumptionLiters: metrics.fuelConsumptionLiters
    };
  }

  static runScenario(
    baseline: TwinState,
    scenario: TwinScenario
  ): TwinScenarioResult {
    const budgetFactor = Math.min(scenario.modernizationBudget / 1_000_000, 1);
    const laneFactor = scenario.parameters.laneExpansionPercent ?? 0;
    const signalFactor = scenario.parameters.signalOptimizationFactor ?? 0;
    const fuelFactor = scenario.parameters.fuelEfficiencyProgramFactor ?? 0;

    const efficiency =
      0.4 * budgetFactor + 0.2 * laneFactor / 100 + 0.2 * signalFactor + 0.2 * fuelFactor;

    const projectedTravelTimeMinutes =
      baseline.travelTimeMinutes * (1 - 0.2 * efficiency);
    const projectedIncidentCount =
      baseline.incidentCount * (1 - 0.3 * efficiency);
    const projectedThroughputVehicles =
      baseline.throughputVehicles * (1 + 0.3 * efficiency);
    const projectedFuelConsumptionLiters =
      baseline.fuelConsumptionLiters * (1 - 0.15 * efficiency);

    const roiPercent = efficiency * 30;

    return {
      scenarioId: scenario.id,
      corridorId: baseline.corridorId,
      projectedTravelTimeMinutes,
      projectedIncidentCount,
      projectedThroughputVehicles,
      projectedFuelConsumptionLiters,
      roiPercent
    };
  }
}
Global Sovereign Credit Pricing Model
ts
// core/global/credit/SovereignCreditPricing.ts
import { LiquiditySnapshot } from "../scx/SCXLiquidityEngine";

export interface CreditPricingInput {
  assetCode: string;
  liquidity: LiquiditySnapshot;
  corridorImpactScore: number; // 0–1
  modernizationPriorityScore: number; // 0–1
  riskScore: number; // 0–1 (higher = riskier)
}

export interface CreditPricingResult {
  assetCode: string;
  baseYieldPercent: number;
  riskPremiumPercent: number;
  modernizationPremiumPercent: number;
  finalYieldPercent: number;
  indicativePricePerLot: number;
}

export class SovereignCreditPricing {
  static price(input: CreditPricingInput): CreditPricingResult {
    const { liquidity, corridorImpactScore, modernizationPriorityScore, riskScore } = input;

    const baseYieldPercent = 3.0;
    const liquidityFactor = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(liquidity.trades24h / 100, 1.5));
    const riskPremiumPercent = riskScore * 2.0;
    const modernizationPremiumPercent =
      (corridorImpactScore + modernizationPriorityScore) * 1.5;

    const finalYieldPercent =
      baseYieldPercent * liquidityFactor + riskPremiumPercent - modernizationPremiumPercent;

    const indicativePricePerLot =
      liquidity.lastPrice * (1 - (finalYieldPercent - baseYieldPercent) / 100);

    return {
      assetCode: input.assetCode,
      baseYieldPercent,
      riskPremiumPercent,
      modernizationPremiumPercent,
      finalYieldPercent,
      indicativePricePerLot
    };
  }
}
Global Corridor Optimization AI
ts
// core/global/corridor/CorridorOptimizationAI.ts
import { TwinState } from "../digitaltwin/DigitalTwinEngine";

export interface OptimizationConstraint {
  maxBudget: number;
  minTravelTimeReductionPercent: number;
  minThroughputIncreasePercent: number;
}

export interface OptimizationRecommendation {
  corridorId: string;
  recommendedBudget: number;
  laneExpansionPercent: number;
  signalOptimizationFactor: number;
  fuelEfficiencyProgramFactor: number;
  expectedTravelTimeReductionPercent: number;
  expectedThroughputIncreasePercent: number;
}

export class CorridorOptimizationAI {
  static optimize(
    baseline: TwinState,
    constraint: OptimizationConstraint
  ): OptimizationRecommendation {
    const laneExpansionPercent = Math.min(20, constraint.maxBudget / 100_000);
    const signalOptimizationFactor = 0.5;
    const fuelEfficiencyProgramFactor = 0.4;

    const expectedTravelTimeReductionPercent =
      Math.min(
        constraint.minTravelTimeReductionPercent + laneExpansionPercent * 0.3,
        30
      );
    const expectedThroughputIncreasePercent =
      Math.min(
        constraint.minThroughputIncreasePercent + laneExpansionPercent * 0.4,
        40
      );

    const recommendedBudget = constraint.maxBudget;

    return {
      corridorId: baseline.corridorId,
      recommendedBudget,
      laneExpansionPercent,
      signalOptimizationFactor,
      fuelEfficiencyProgramFactor,
      expectedTravelTimeReductionPercent,
      expectedThroughputIncreasePercent
    };
  }
}
Full Federation Treaty DSL (Domain‑Specific Language)
ts
// core/global/federation/FederationTreatyDSL.ts
import { EventLevel } from "../domain/Events";

export type DomainKey =
  | "LOGISTICS"
  | "FUEL"
  | "SCX"
  | "TREASURY"
  | "LUXURY"
  | "PUBLISHING"
  | "MODERNIZATION"
  | "ANALYTICS";

export interface TreatyClause {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  domains: DomainKey[];
  level: EventLevel;
  dataSharingLevel: "AGGREGATED" | "ANONYMIZED" | "FULL";
  requiresQuantumProof: boolean;
  requiresOnChainSettlement: boolean;
}

export interface FederationTreaty {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  federationId: string;
  parties: string[]; // agency IDs or nation IDs
  clauses: TreatyClause[];
  effectiveFrom: string;
  expiresAt?: string;
}

export class FederationTreatyDSL {
  static createTreaty(
    federationId: string,
    name: string,
    parties: string[]
  ): FederationTreaty {
    return {
      id: `TREATY-${Date.now()}`,
      name,
      federationId,
      parties,
      clauses: [],
      effectiveFrom: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  static addClause(
    treaty: FederationTreaty,
    clause: Omit<TreatyClause, "id">
  ): FederationTreaty {
    const newClause: TreatyClause = {
      ...clause,
      id: `CLAUSE-${treaty.id}-${treaty.clauses.length + 1}`
    };
    return {
      ...treaty,
      clauses: [...treaty.clauses, newClause]
    };
  }

  static getApplicableClauses(
    treaty: FederationTreaty,
    domain: DomainKey,
    level: EventLevel
  ): TreatyClause[] {
    return treaty.clauses.filter(
      c => c.domains.includes(domain) && c.level === level
    );
  }
}
